The town planner of the entire world is the tenth intellect. *'Ashir*. *'Ashir*, *'ashir*, we who... recite in the prayers, he is the tenth intellect, his rank... So remember that this is a summary of the *ma'dhuniyyat*, which is the realm of intellects. This is... it's very long. I am just giving brief indications. But it's very big, how the forms were created, what they did, and what kind of gaze there was, what it is. *La ta'khudhuhu sinatun wala nawm.* (Neither drowsiness nor sleep overtakes Him.) Neither drowsiness overcomes him, nor sleep. Meaning, the helper is not lazy. *Lahu ma fi al-samawati wa ma fi al-ard.* (To Him belongs whatever is in the heavens and whatever is on the earth.) The heavens and the earth, whatever is in them, is His. Meaning, in the manifest and the hidden. The manifest and the hidden. Now the manifest and the hidden began. The lofty gaze and the base gaze. Everywhere, this is what it is, the lofty gaze and the base gaze. The one who has a base gaze should understand that the one with the lofty gaze is superior. This... this should be accepted. *Man dhalladhi yashfa'u 'indahu illa bi-idhnihi.* (Who is it that can intercede with Him except by His permission?) Whose power is it that without his permission, a request can be made in his presence, an intercession can be made? Meaning, now see, the *Ta'li*. The *Ta'li*. The *Ta'li* is clapped with two hands. The *Munba'ith-e-Awwal* and the *Munba'ith-e-Thani*. Or the *Ta'li* has become. The *Ta'li* is what? It is the being of the *Munba'ith-e-Awwal*, who became the veil of the *'aql-e-awwal*. And from here, he became the next, the follower. He became the one who follows. And besides him, no one can intercede. The *Munba'ith-e-Awwal*, besides him... meaning, only within his circle can you... all our requests that go, we think that they reach the Imam of the time here. But in reality, the circle goes up to there, up to the *Munba'ith-e-Awwal*. He is the master of all.

Now, it has been explained that the *'aql-e-awwal*, who did it without an inspirer... this is a special thing to understand, that the *'aql-e-awwal* who did it without a teacher and without an inspirer, he was removed from the circle. He was made higher. And the circle of unity (*da'irah-e-wahdah*) was made. *Wahdah* means he is the one and only being, that is the being of the *'aql-e-awwal*. That's it, it ends there. With the *Munba'ith-e-Awwal*, there are a few. And with his third intellect, there are more. In the fourth, there are more. The soldiers of Allah, I mean, the army, only Allah knows how many there are. It was divided into seven circles. And for this reason, seven heavens were made. And in this way, down to the realm of the human being. So, the *Munba'ith-e-Awwal*, he is the Holy Presence (*hadirat al-quds*). The Garden of Abode (*jannat al-ma'wa*) is near the Holy Presence. And here in our world, he is the Imam of the time, and then his... who is it? The Gate of Gates (*Bab al-Abwab*). The Gate of Gates is the Garden of Abode (*jannat al-ma'wa*), which is near the Lote-tree of the Utmost Boundary (*sidrat al-muntaha*). Remember this, this is of the world here... the state.

So now it is being said here that besides his *Bab-e-Ta'li*, no one can intercede. *Munba'ith-e-Awwal*, it is written here. *Ya'lamu ma bayna aydihim.* Read, brother. *Ya'lamu ma bayna aydihim.* (He knows what is [presently] before them.) He knows those things which are before them. Meaning, the *du'at* of the *A'immah*... and their *hujjat* (proofs), who are the manifest representatives in the heavens and the earth, he knows them. *Wa ma khalfahum.* (And what will be after them.) And those who are behind or after them. Meaning, those who will be established in their ranks after them, their successors (*khulafa'*). Yes. *Wala yuhituna bi-shay'in...* Now, the same thing happened in that realm. In the realm of intellects, when seven intellects were formed, after the seven intellects, 82 intellects were formed. This is all a very subtle matter, it might be difficult to understand. But this is it, that 82 intellects were formed after that. And this, Sayyidna Hasan Khan, who is... 'Abd al-Malik, he has explained it with great clarity, how in the realm of intellects, seven were formed, and then seven went up from below. And then seven *Nutuqa'* were formed. Then seven opposites were formed. And this is our count, meaning, that is, we carry around the 28 letters. From *alif* to *ya*, there are 28. In the 28, there are four pieces of seven each, or two of fourteen each. So remember that all this, until we understand it, the *ta'wil* will not be understood, and if the *ta'wil* is not understood, the realities will not be understood. *Ya'lamu ma bayna aydihim*, meaning the *du'at* and *hujjat* of the *A'immah*, who are the manifest ones in the heavens and the earth, the representatives. *Wa ma khalfahum*, and those who will come after them, meaning those who will be established in their ranks, their successors. *Wala yuhituna bi-shay'in min 'ilmihi illa bima sha'.* (And they encompass not a thing of His knowledge except for what He wills.) And the people... people cannot be aware of any part of his knowledge, except for that much which he wishes. Meaning, it is in the authority of Allah that... here, what is written? It is in the authority of the *Sabeq-e-Ta'ala* that how much knowledge is given to you. It is not in anyone's authority. We, the... what is it for us? That the acquired intellect exists, and the innate intellect exists. Now, the common people know, neither the scientists know, where the intellect is. Where is the intellect? What is the innate intellect called? And what is the acquired intellect? Is it in the brain or in the heart? Now, you can look it up, you can ask a philosopher or a scientist. I don't want to speak today, tomorrow, God willing, we will do it, it has become too much. Go on, tomorrow, God willing, I will explain. *Wala yuhituna bi-shay'in min 'ilmihi illa bima sha'.* And those people cannot be aware of any part of his knowledge, except for that much... as much as he is made aware of. Meaning... Meaning, the *A'immah*, *hujjat*, and *du'at* all know from his knowledge only as much as he has taught them. Yes. *Wasi'a kursiyyuhu al-samawati wa al-ard.* (His Throne extends over the heavens and the earth.) His throne has spread over the heavens and the earth. Meaning, the throne, whose representative is the *wasī*, it is dominant over the *A'immah* and the *hujjat*. *Wala ya'uduhu hifzuhuma.* Now, there are many things in this, I don't want to make the discussion long now. Otherwise, what is the meaning of the throne, whose... the *wasī* of the Prophet, who is dominant over the *A'immah* and the *hujjat*. What does dominant mean? Mawlana 'Ali (sallallahu 'alayhi), who is the *wasī* of the Prophet, Mawlana 'Ali (sallallahu 'alayhi), he is the father of the Imams (*Abu al-A'immah*). He is the leader of the pious (*Imam al-muttaqin*). He is the leader of the radiant ones (*qa'id al-ghurr al-muhajjalin*). And from where the noble *da'wat* begins, he is the *wasī* of the Prophet. And all the Imams who are coming, from Mawlana Hasan to the present Imam, all of them are veils (*hijab*) of Mawlana 'Ali. They are veils. Now to understand this, let's leave this matter, it's enough. This is... it has been said... what has been said? "Paradise lies at the feet of the mother." "Paradise lies at the feet of the mother." What does this mean? "Paradise lies at the feet of the mother," have you understood? What does it mean? A dutiful one... his ethical advice, all this is in its place, that one should also have love for the mother, and one should also have affection for the mother, and one should also obey the mother. But... "Paradise lies at the feet of the mother," what does it mean? So it has been explained in other books that this is a saying of the Prophet, that "O 'Ali, I and you, O 'Ali, are the two fathers of the believers (*abawayn al-mu'minin*)." "I and you, O 'Ali..." ...are the parents of all believers. So, the father is you, and the mother is Mawlana 'Ali (sallallahu 'alayhi)'s being. Now see, this is a point to understand. Should one love the father or the mother? What does nature say? Love is for the mother. And for this reason, the *wilayah* (love/allegiance) of Amir al-Mu'minin has been made obligatory. The *wilayah* of the Prophet, has it been mentioned anywhere? That if there is no love for the Prophet or if he is not obeyed, then we are not even Muslims. But after becoming a Muslim, until... the *wasī* of the Prophet, 'Ali al-Murtada (sallallahu 'alayhi), there is no love for him... without the *wilayah* of Amir al-Mu'minin, neither your prayer, nor your fast, nor your pilgrimage, nor your zakat, nothing is valid. So what this means is that in religion, the ranks that are there, or the believers, or the respondents, whatever they are, for every one of them, in the rank of the mother is the being of Mawlana 'Ali. At the feet of the mother is... what is it? Paradise. At the feet of the parents... the common mother, you obey her anyway, and your love for her awakens, everything is there. But the real mother is Mawlana Amir al-Mu'minin (sallallahu 'alayhi)'s being. And his feet are from Mawlana Hasan to here, to the 48th Imam. They are the feet (*khudum*). Whose love has been made obligatory. You cannot leave a single Imam. All of them, you have to remember them by name, so that our prayer is complete. So, feet means this.

Another example is also there. I will just mention it. More than just reading... that wherever analogies (*tamthilat*) have been given, to understand religion, that *Allahu nur al-samawati wa al-ard.* (Allah is the Light of the heavens and the earth.) Allah is the light of the heavens and the earth. So what is the earth, and what are the heavens? The earth is the being of Mawlana Amir al-Mu'minin (sallallahu 'alayhi), and the heavens are the being of the Prophet. This is the second example. The third, what is it? ... The sun and the moon. Who is the sun of the religion? The spiritual sun is the being of Mawlana the Prophet. And Mawlana 'Ali is the moon of the *da'wat*. So what this means is, how will you interpret the sun? How are you saying that... the Prophet is in it? How... how will you say this? So it has been explained that where... it has been explained that where... where the mention of the sun has come in the Quran, there... there it has been explained that "*sirajan wahhaja*." (a burning lamp). "A lamp that is blazing." Blazing means it is giving light, it is giving heat, it is giving warmth. "*Sirajan wahhaja*," this is the attribute of the sun. And when the example of the Prophet is given, there it is said "*sirajan munira*." (a luminous lamp). He has also been called a lamp. So both became lamps. That is why we say that the spiritual sun is the Prophet, and the spiritual moon is Mawlana 'Ali (sallallahu 'alayhi)'s being. Now you see that we... can we sit in the light of the sun? You sit for two hours, your life will be at risk. The commands of the Prophet, the religious obligations, fasting, praying, doing this, doing that, you get tired of it. But we have to believe in it because it is the Prophet's... all these commands have come through revelation. But if you see this in an example, not the sun, you sit under the moon. You sit in a moonlit night, you can sit for hours. You benefit from it. Because the Prophet is in the station of the sun, so his light is called *ziya'* (radiance). The light that is self-generated is called *ziya'*, and the light that is borrowed from others is called *nur* (light). So the light in the moon is borrowed, it is taken from the sun. The Prophet... it is taken from him. And Mawlana 'Ali says that "I learned a thousand chapters of knowledge from the Prophet, and from each thousand, a thousand more chapters opened up for me." So what this means is that the Prophet gave, and 'Ali took. So you... you also, here in the world, science tells us that all the light in the moon is a gift from the sun. So the spiritual sun is the Prophet, and the spiritual moon is Mawlana 'Ali (sallallahu 'alayhi). And that is why we... divine sciences, or lordly sciences, which are the streams of Mawlana 'Ali, which are his sciences, his realities, we can listen to 'Ali's for hours. There is difficulty in the commands. "Hey, the fast has become 14 hours long, it has become 16 hours long, so-and-so happened, how to give this much zakat, so-and-so." All these are difficulties. So the matter of the sun is different, and the matter of the moon is different. So the three examples are, the sun and the moon are on the Prophet and 'Ali. The parents, the father and mother, are also them. And the third is this... what is it? The heavens and the earth. The heavens are the *Nutuqa'*, and the *wasī* of the Prophet is... he is. God willing, if there is another discussion, I will explain it.

Now one thing remains, that is the intellect. Where is the intellect? You should find out where the intellect is. Science says one thing, geographers say another, philosophers say another, that it is inside the brain. And we have to think about something, so the brain has to be used. And for us, what do the *du'at* teach? That the intellect is in the heart as well as in the brain. The intellect. But there is a difference between the two. One is the acquired intellect (*'aql-e-muktasib*), and one is the innate intellect (*'aql-e-gharizi*). The innate is a different thing. Acquired, grasping power. The child slowly learns and grows, that is related to the brain. And for us, the structure of the Imam of the time, the *haykal-e-imami*, inside it, who is in the station of the brain, who is in the station of the heart, all this needs to be known. God willing, when the opportunity comes, I will explain. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim... ...Al-Fatiha.

<!-- AUG:ibrahim-2015 lecture 6 -->

...to the Imam of the time, and the da'wah of the Imam of the time is the da'wah of the Wasi. So, from this perspective, if you look, the da'wah of the Wasi is the da'wah of the divine religion, and there is no compulsion to enter it. La ikraha fi al-din (There is no compulsion in religion). This is the statement of Allah Ta'ala, and its explanation, that is, with the word 'yani' (meaning), is the ta'wil of the Holy Qur'an which Sayyidna Husayn Husamuddin stated. qad tabayyana al-rushdu min al-ghayy (the right course has become distinct from the wrong). Hidayah (guidance) has become clear from zalalah (misguidance). Meaning, the distinction between the station of the Wasi and the station of the Zidd has been established. fa man yakfur bi al-taghuti wa yu'min billahi... The station of the Zidd... who was the Zidd of the Wasi of the Prophet, Maulana Ali Salawatullah 'alayh, it has become known from them who is right and who is wrong. fa man yakfur bi al-taghuti wa yu'min billahi (So whoever disbelieves in Taghut and believes in Allah). The person who rejects the Taghut and brings iman (faith) in Allah... yani (meaning) the one who rejects the Zidd al-Awwal (the first opponent) and accepts the Wasi. Ji. fa qad istamsaka bi al-'urwati al-wuthqa (has grasped the most trustworthy handhold). So he has grasped the strong rope of Allah. Look, this, this is: he rejects the Zidd al-Awwal. As I have mentioned before, if one is to come to the da'wah of the Wasi, meaning to accept the wilayah of Amir al-Mu'minin, then one must also perform bara'ah (disassociation) from those who are against him. Until there is bara'ah, it cannot happen. It should be in the heart, whether it is expressed by the tongue or not. Meaning, in the era of taqiyah, it is not necessary. But the bara'ah should be from one's heart, that 'we do not accept them'. The Khalifah, the true janashin (successor) of the Prophet is Maulana Ali Salawatullah 'alayh, who was raised in Ghadir Khumm. So the meaning is that the one who was made [successor] in Ghadir Khumm is the true janashin. The one whom the people made is not the true janashin. And what happened at the time of the Prophet's passing, you all have heard in the majalis that at the Prophet's final moment, it became very difficult. Janab Ali and Janab Fatima were sitting beside him, and all the people were holding a committee, having a meeting there. At Saqifah, which is behind the Masjid al-Nabawi, there. That when the Prophet passes away, and the situation is such, what should we do now? Who will lead the prayer and who will be the leader? So all that meeting was happening, and in the midst of it, Janab Umar, who is the Khalifah Thani, he said to Abu Bakr Sahib, the Khalifah Awwal, that 'bring, extend your hand, I will give you my...' ...I do it. I give bay'ah (pledge of allegiance). And he agreed. And so, the Awwal (first one) became the Khalifah Awwal. He led the prayer and all these things happened. And at that time, Maulana Ali is sitting, with the Prophet. So this method is that if the father passes away, will the children stay with the father or with the treasury? This is what has happened in Islam. The meaning of which is that here, Janab Ali and Janab Fatima, he took his last breath on their faces, the Prophet Salawatullah 'alayh. And that too, we have very high things told to us in the haqa'iq, what kind of sughrat and huliyat were granted to you at that time. Yani (meaning) he will reject the Zidd al-Awwal and accept the Wasi... ...so he has grasped the strong rope of Allah. Now, the 'urwat al-wuthqa, we take it to mean Maulana Ali. Here, the ta'wil that is written, look at it. Yani (meaning)... Yani (meaning) with this, the ma'rifah (gnosis) of Fatima al-Zahra was obtained. And people at that time were deprived of your ma'rifah. Deprived. la infisama laha (with no break in it). Janab Fatima... Janab Fatima is that personality who, after the Prophet Salawatullah 'alayh and Ali, her rank comes. She is the Hujjat of... Abu... Maulana Abu Talib, and besides that, she is also the Hujjat of Maulana Ali. She is the Hujjat 'Usma. In this, it is told that, as I always say, that Mawla-e-Kainat is the remover of difficulties of both worlds. Mawla-e-Kainat is... the title of Janab Ali. And Sarwar-e-Kainat is what they call Janab... the Prophet. Mawla-e-Kainat is the remover of difficulties of both worlds, and the remover of difficulties of Mawla-e-Kainat is Fatima. Fatima is the one who used to remove the hardships of Maulana Ali. You, for your whole life, after marriage, the conditions you lived in and how you lived, the Prophet knew and Ali knows. The one who obtained the ma'rifah of Fatima al-Zahra, the bad-bakht (unfortunate) people are those who... are deprived of your ma'rifah. He says bad-qismat (ill-fated) people, they are deprived. la infisama laha (with no break in it). It will never break. Which one? Habl (rope). Yani (meaning) al-'urwat al-wuthqa, which is Hablullah al-Matin (the firm rope of Allah), it will never break. Yani (meaning)... Yani (meaning) Fatima is connected to the Wasi. Therefore, there is no possibility of them being cut or broken. Wallahu Sami'un 'Alim (And Allah is Hearing and Knowing). And Allah is the one who hears and knows. Yani (meaning) the Wasi hears the du'a (supplication) of the one who prays, and is aware of his people of... the people of wilayah. Ji. Allahu waliyyu alladhina amanu... Sami'un 'Alim, where, as I have mentioned before, the word Kabir comes, the word Basir comes, there, the reference is made to Allah, and Allah is the one about whom one becomes astonished, or about whom one is made to doubt. That is our Allah. And Allah comes from 'walah', as we read yesterday. Sami'un 'Alim means the one who hears and knows. Yani (meaning) the Wasi, he knows all his haqq walon (rightful followers), who is on the right path and in what manner. He is also the hearer of du'a and is also aware of the people of wilayah. Allahu waliyyu alladhina amanu yukhrijuhum min al-zulumati ila al-nur (Allah is the protector of those who have believed. He brings them out from darknesses into the light). Allah is the friend of the believers. He brings them out of darkness into the light. Yani (meaning) the Wasi is the wali (protector) of the believers. He brings them out of doubts and uncertainties into the light of clear explanation. wa alladhina kafaru awliya'uhum al-taghutu yukhrijunahum min al-nuri ila al-zulumati (And those who disbelieve - their allies are Taghut. They take them out of the light into darknesses). And the awliya' (allies) of the kafirs (disbelievers) are the Taghut. They take them out of the light into darkness. Yani (meaning) the friends of the deniers of the station of the Wasi are the Taghut. They are the leaders of misguidance. People... Taghut means the rebellious people. The rebellious people who, in that era, the names, if you read history, it will be known. There is Marwan, there is Abu Sufyan, there is Mu'awiyah, whoever they were, all of them were those people. They are the leaders of misguidance. They, from the ma'rifat-e-ilahi (divine gnosis)... ...they take them out from the light of ma'rifat-e-ilahi and pull them into the darkness of jahalat (ignorance). ula'ika ashabu al-nari hum fiha khalidun (Those are the companions of the Fire; they will abide eternally therein). Those people are the inhabitants of hell. They will remain in it forever. Yani (meaning) the ashab (companions) of the Zidd al-Awwal will first burn in the fire of shukuk (doubts), and then they will be cast into the eternal punishment of hell. Ji. This, the Ayat al-Kursi is now finished, at khalidun. Up to khalidun. After that, what is there is that there are du'aiyah kalimat (supplicatory words), that will come later. Now, after this, in the Holy Qur'an, wherever this 'tilka al-rusulu' has begun, there the incident of Janab Ibrahim has been narrated. The story of Ibrahim 'alayh al-salam. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. alam tara ila alladhi hajja Ibrahima fi rabbihi (Have you not considered the one who argued with Abraham about his Lord)? Have you not seen this person who argued with Ibrahim about their Lord? Yani (meaning) that person who quarreled with Ibrahim about his wasi (successor). Because he himself was an aspirant for wasa'it... ...an aspirant for wasa'it, rather a claimant. an atahu Allahu al-mulk (because Allah had given him kingship). Because Allah had given him saltanat (kingdom). Yani (meaning) Nimrud. Nimrud was the king. Nimrud was the king and he was... this Nimrud, Fir'awn, they were great in their own places. They were kings and they were that. But where the rank of Musa is great, the rank of 'Isa is great, or whoever, Ibrahim's rank is great, at that time they did not accept. This is what has always happened. Yani (meaning) Nimrud... Yani (meaning) Nimrud, the Zidd of Ibrahim and Isma'il, whom Allah had blessed with a great apparent government. So he also became an aspirant, rather a claimant, for the authority of the batini (inner) da'wah. He had the zahiri (apparent) da'wah, and he thought that the da'wah-e-batin would also be with him. There is a lot of difference between the two. For three or four days I have been continuously saying that the tanzili stages are different, and the ta'wili ones are different. There is a difference between the two, zahir and batin. idh qala Ibrahimu rabbiya alladhi yuhyi wa yumitu (When Abraham said, "My Lord is the one who gives life and causes death,"). When Ibrahim said to him, 'My Lord is the one who gives life and causes death.' Yani (meaning), my Wasi has this power that he, with his ta'idi (supportive) strength, makes the dead of jahalat (ignorance) into an 'alim (knower). And by removing this ta'yid, he pushes them into the darkness of jahalat. qala ana uhyi wa umit (He said, "I give life and cause death."). He said, 'I too give life and cause death.' Yani (meaning), the Zidd of Isma'il, who was the Hujjat of Ibrahim's da'wah, said, 'What is the big deal in this? The work of granting ifadah (beneficence) and taking it away, I can do that too. Whomever I wish, I teach, and whomever I do not wish, I stop.' Ji. This, this the Zidd said. That is, ana uhyi wa umit. These are special verses and special phrases that came from the mouth of Janab Ibrahim, about how the argument with him took place. What happened in the time of Fir'awn was with Janab Musa. You all know that. Ifadah and sulb. Now look, what is the meaning of uhyi wa umit? To give ifadah is to give life. And to change from it, to take it away (salb kar lena), is the matter of stopping it. qala Ibrahimu fa inna Allaha ya'ti bi al-shamsi min al-mashriqi fa'ti biha min al-maghribi (Abraham said, "Indeed, Allah brings up the sun from the east, so bring it up from the west."). Ibrahim said, 'Allah brings out the sun from the east. You bring it out from the west and show me.' Yani (meaning), the Wasi, his Imamate... Imamate and... Imamate and his own... he gives the proof of the اشاره (indication) towards himself from this Hadd-e-'Alawi ruhani (spiritual higher limit) which helps the Hudud-e-jismani (physical limits). So, prove your claim by the ishara (indication) of this person who is the bab (gate) of this Hadd-e-'Alawi, who is called the mashriq (east). fa buhita alladhi kafara wa Allahu la yahdi al-qawma al-zalimin (So the disbeliever was overwhelmed [by astonishment], and Allah does not guide the wrongdoing people). So the kafir (disbeliever) was astonished and speechless. And Allah... When he said that he brings it out from the mashriq, Allah Ta'ala, you bring it out from the maghrib and show me. The sun never rises from the maghrib. And look at its explanations, wherever the reason has been given. That it is the statement of the Prophet that 300 years after me, the sun will rise from the maghrib. Now this is also a statement, and that is also a statement. If the meaning is not understood, the Qur'an Sharif is not understood. Here, the meaning is that that is... the reference is made to the Hudud-e-ruhani, the Hadd-e-'Alawi. The reference is made to the Hadd-e-'Alawi. And what is there? That the Prophet says that 300 years after me, the sun will rise from the maghrib. What does that mean? The sun never rises from the maghrib. It rises from the mashriq and sets in the maghrib. But what is the meaning there? That 300 years later, where the era of the Prophet passed and the... the count of the Du'at... yani (meaning) of the Imams began, from Maulana Hasan, the first hafta (week/cycle) passed, the second hafta passed, and at that time, who... Janab Mehdi, yani (meaning) the 'alam (world) of satr (concealment) that was, in it, Maulana Mehdi Salawatullah 'alayh, who is our 11th Imam. The 11th Imam. And after him is Maulana Qa'im. So what is the count like? That after Maulana Muhammad bin Isma'il, there were three masturin Imams. After three masturin, that is, eight, nine, ten. And the 11th Imam is our Maulana Mehdi Salawatullah 'alayh. Who came from Murakush (Morocco). So Murako, that is... they call it maghrib. The sun rose from the maghrib. Now people didn't understand this. The sun never rises from the maghrib. What is the statement of the Prophet, we don't know. What happened? We have its meanings explained that he came out from Murakush. He came to Mahdiyyah. From Murakush... after coming out from Murakush, he founded Mahdiyyah and after that, the da'wah moved forward. So there was an immediate announcement. And how did it happen? Just as the Prophet was called Mim-e-Awwala. Mim-e-Awwala is the person of Janab Prophet. This is Mim-e-Awsata. Who? Janab Mehdi. Hazarat Imam Mehdi. So the kafir was astonished and speechless, and Allah does not give hidayah (guidance) to the zalimin (wrongdoers). Yani (meaning) Sabiq-e-Tala did not show the right path to the deniers of His awliya' and those who usurp their rights. aw ka alladhi marra 'ala qaryatin wa hiya khawiyatun 'ala 'urushiha (Or [consider such an example] as the one who passed by a township which had fallen into ruin). Or like this person who passed by a town that was fallen on its roofs. According to some narrations, this person was Hazrat... Hazrat Armiya bin Harqiya, Armiya bin Harqiya Nabi. And according to some, Hazrat Uzayr bin Sarayah Nabi. These two are also prophets. The Armeni people who live there, in Armenia, it comes from this, Armiya. And 'Uzayr, who was 'Uzayr? A Nabi (prophet). He was a Nabi, and 'Uzayr's name, Janab... Allah has put it in the Holy Qur'an. When was it put? Before whom? 'Isa. Before 'Isa. Before 'Isa, after Musa. So this is Janab 'Uzayr. And what did Allah say? That those people... the verse that has come, perhaps it will come in this. That Allah... one servant, who is 'Uzayr, people wanted to make him their son, Allah's son. Who was doing this? Saint Paul. Saint Paul, who was the bishop of the Christians, and he was... corrupt. And he is... his relationship has been described, what he was to 'Isa, and so on. But we are not concerned with that. The point is that it was Saint Paul's wish that now, in the religion... the shari'ah of Musa that is going on, to bring a disturbance into it. And this happened, that he wanted to make Allah's son, 'Uzayr. But at that very time, Janab 'Isa arrived. And 'Isa Nabi is a very special prophet. He is called Ruhullah. And Janab 'Isa, he has the fifth rank. What is the fifth rank? Skeleton. That is, in the body, it is the rank of the bones. The rank of the bones. When 'Isa Masih came, the meaning we understand of Masih is that Masih Masih means the one who touched and brought the dead to life, and cured the blind. It would be something, a miracle is with every prophet. But what is the real thing? That Masih is from 'siyahat' (traveling). That he used to travel. He never stayed in one place. And he had no house at all, Janab 'Isa. And 'Isa Nabi is the one who spread the biggest religion, that is, Christianity. It was a very big religion before Islam. But what was his age? 32 years. A little less than 33 years. He passed away at 32. Now, in his youth, I mean, at the age of 32. You, he... used to do tabligh (preaching) to the people, used to do all this. So those who believed, believed, and those who didn't, didn't. You used to, from one place, put your clothes and things on your shoulder and keep going. And he said, from here to there, from there to here, you go. At that time, his followers said, 'O 'Isa, O Ruhullah, we all have houses and you have no house. We want you to have a house too. And we are your followers, so we want you to have a house. And where will you build it? You tell us.' So Janab 'Isa said, 'Alright, I will tell you tomorrow morning.' The next morning, he went and while walking, reached a place, on a hill. And the slope of the hill, he said, 'Build on this.' They became worried, that how will a house be built on this slope? He said, 'The condition of the world is also like this, it is a slope. What is the use of building a house on this? The house should be built there. In the hereafter.' This has been told. That Masih is from 'siyahat', and you, he... what were they? About 'Isa Masih, it has been told that you, in the place where you were, your father was Yusuf bin Habib al-Najjar, that was his name. Yusuf was the name. Yusuf bin Habib al-Najjar. His rank was less than Janab 'Isa. He was Janab 'Isa's father. And what happened was that Janab Maryam, who is given with the Ahl al-Bayt, with the prophets, that Hazrat Maryam, her son was 'Isa, and she wanted that now, my son has grown up, he should work somewhere. Become a craftsman. And at that time, the craftsmanship that existed, he wanted to do that work in his place. And just as you know what Adam 'alayh al-salam's profession was, after that Nuh 'alayh al-salam was a carpenter, Janab Prophet Ibrahim was a builder, and after that Musa was a... he used to graze goats and was a shepherd. And after that Janab 'Isa, what... what is this? Janab 'Isa, he... used to do the work of a dyer. So in one place, the dyers of that time... now its ta'wil is different. I am leaving the ta'wil, I will tell that. But the work of a dyer. So Janab Maryam placed him there. He stayed for two or four days, then left. Then another, he went to another place, left there. He wouldn't stay and work anywhere. So the mother was worried that the child doesn't stay and work anywhere. But he went to one place and started working and became an expert in the work of a dyer. In dyeing clothes. Now what is meant by clothes? Sibghatullah, you all know. This is... its ta'wil is different. But what happened? That one day his master, who had employed him, he said, 'Ya 'Isa, I am going to the neighboring village. You have an order for these clothes, and you dye them. Red, yellow, blue, there were seven or six colors that he told. And in that, you dye them all separately and keep them. I will come in four days.' When he came after four days, he saw that 'Isa 'alayh al-salam was just standing there. So he said, 'What did you do?' He said, 'In one dye pot, I put all the bundles of cloth.' So he got worried. He said, 'You ruined the whole thing. The order is ruined.' So Janab 'Isa said, 'What do you want?' He said, 'This... I want the red color.' So by the power of Allah, it became red. When you said it, it became red. Then he took out yellow, so yellow came out. Now when he started looking, every color was coming out. So at that time, he was astonished, the master, that 'I thought he was a craftsman, but he is a waliullah (friend of Allah). He is Allah's wali.' And he got worried. He became worried and the dyers of the surroundings, he called all of them. And said, 'Look, this boy whom I had kept, the young man, and I had given him this work. But he has... it's a strange... he is a waliullah. Whatever he says, happens.' So they all started to accept him too. And the dyers who were there, they were all Hudud. And he was also a Hadd. As soon as it was known that he is a waliullah, they all became his disciples. They are the 12 disciples who were with him at the Last Supper. The Hawariyyun, as they are called. So such miraculous events also happened with 'Isa. So here, it is the matter of Nimrud. And what is it? aw ka alladhi marra... Yani (meaning) that Da'i reached a village where the da'wah had ceased. Not a single Mumin remained there. Okay, this talk that was happening was about Janab 'Uzayr. About Janab 'Uzayr it is said, Allah says that those people wanted to make 'Uzayr a son. Yani (meaning) who? Saint Paul and the Christians. But when they saw that 'Isa's shari'ah has started, Musa's shari'ah is ending... ending. And there is... Janab 'Isa's shari'ah had such a great impact that immediately this Saint Paul entered it, came into Christianity. He came into Christianity and became a great bishop there. And after becoming that, he said that 'now I will come into this religion and make Allah's son.' Whom? 'Isa. So the trick of making 'Isa a son, all of it belongs to him. To Saint Paul. And in some narrations, what is it? That he makes 'Uzayr a son, or Armiya. Yani (meaning) that sahib reached a village... Yani (meaning) that sahib reached a village where the da'wah had ceased. Not a single Mumin remained there. It is a necessary thing that ifadah (beneficence) and istifadah (receiving beneficence) had ended. qala anna yuhyi hadhihi Allahu ba'da mawtiha (He said, "How will Allah bring this to life after its death?"). He said, 'How will Allah resurrect this town after its ruin?' Yani (meaning), how will the Imam of the time promote the withered da'wah again? fa amatahu Allahu mi'ata 'amin thumma ba'athahu (So Allah caused him to die for a hundred years; then He revived him). Then Allah himself made him dead for 100 years. Then he brought him back to life. Yani (meaning), the Imam of the time, from the 100 Hudud of Allah who are the 100 mamsul of 100 years, he cut off their connection. Then he joined the connection again and blessed them with ruhani (spiritual) help. As it has happened in different places. And later on, what are 100 years, what are 1000 years, they are all special durations. Just as I told about 300 years, so is... the companions of the cave. Now here, he has not gone into the details, he has explained the ta'wil, that the 100 who are mamsul, their connection was cut off. This does not mean 100 years, but it is about 100 mamsul. Then their connection was joined again, and they were blessed with spiritual help. qala kam labithta qala labithtu yawman aw ba'da yawm (He said, "How long have you remained?" He said, "I have remained a day or part of a day."). Allah asked, 'How long were you here?' He replied, 'One day or a part of a day.' Yani (meaning), the ma'addat-e-ta'id from me was suspended only for the duration of one Imam's qiyam (era), no, not even that, but only for a short period. qala bal labithta mi'ata 'amin fa unzur ila ta'amika wa sharabika lam yatasannah (He said, "Rather, you have remained one hundred years. Look at your food and your drink; it has not changed with time."). Allah said, 'Rather, you stayed here for a full 100 years. Look at your food and drink, they have not spoiled or rotted.' Yani (meaning), you are in ghaflah (heedlessness). The connection of 100 Hudud-e-Imam was cut off from you. Your test was tough. Look at the condition of your 'ilm-e-zahir (apparent knowledge) and 'ilm-e-batin (inner knowledge), that still no change has occurred in it. wa unzur ila himarika (and look at your donkey). And look at your donkey. Yani (meaning), their Zidd who was the opponent. wa li naj'alaka ayatan li al-nas (and We will make you a sign for the people). And so that We may make you a sign for the people. Yani (meaning), We may make you an Imam for the people attached to the religion. wa unzur ila al-'izami kayfa nunshizuha thumma naksuha lahma (And look at the bones - how We raise them and then We cover them with flesh.). And look at the bones, how We join them, then how We clothe them with flesh. Look, this is a special verse, that how Allah Ta'ala joins us. I will turn your body into dust, and after turning it to dust, I will breathe the soul into it and raise you up. So how? I will make bones in it. On the bones, I will clothe it with flesh. Wa kasawna al-'izama lahma (And We clothed the bones with flesh). And on that... what is the meaning? The shari'ah of Janab 'Isa is described as being the abode of bones. The fifth shari'ah that came into the world, Adam, Nuh, Ibrahim, Musa, 'Isa, and Janab 'Isa, which is the fifth shari'ah, and after that, the Prophet's is the sixth. So at the time of the sixth, the entire structure of the shari'ah was completed. That the bones were clothed with flesh. In the mother's womb, when the fifth month is complete, the skeleton is ready. In the sixth month, the bone and skin... er, flesh and skin are formed. In the seventh month, the soul is breathed in. What does 'breathing the soul' mean? The vegetative soul is in it, but the animal soul appears. When it comes out of the mother's womb. So how is it? It doesn't happen in the seventh month. The seventh month is of the moon, and of Khalq-e-Akhar (another creation). But very rarely, in the seventh month, they are born. After the seventh month, then Zuhal's (Saturn's) kingship happens, the effect of Zuhal is there. Zuhal's effect is always in the habit of freezing. And the thing is, in the eighth month, that child starts to freeze again. And that is why it is told to women that in the eighth month, be careful. The pregnancy that happens, in the eighth... because in the first month, I have told this many times before, that the first month belongs to Zuhal, the second to Mushtari (Jupiter), the third to Mirrikh (Mars), the fourth to Shams (Sun), all these are separate. The fourth is of Shams, meaning movement begins in it, the power of Shams. And the fifth month is of... Zuhra (Venus), and the sixth month is of... 'Atarid (Mercury). Now from here, look, that in the seventh month, when Qamar's (Moon's) kingship is there, the effect of Qamar is there, that is, Qamar and Shams, both are special luminaries. When in the child, that... the sensory perception is created. The vegetative soul is in it, but now, now if the child is born, comes out of the mother's body, then what will happen? It will live. But if the eighth month comes, which is rare in the seventh month, the eighth month, then again Zuhal's effect is there. And it wants to freeze again. In the first month also it did. If it didn't freeze in the first month, the sperm wouldn't have set. But here, after it has set, and movement has started, bones have formed, flesh has been put on, and after that if again it has the habit of freezing, then it will be ruined. After that, the ninth month belongs to whom? Mushtari. After Zuhal. And at that time, birth happens. We have it told that the ninth... there is a whole ta'wil of it, how the seventh month is, then the eighth month and in the ninth month, a person comes out. Then how we clothe them with flesh. Yani (meaning), those Hudud who were scattered and had gone here and there, how we gather them and establish the da'wah. And then how the teaching of ifadah and istifadah begins. Sometimes it also happens that, like today's era, the da'wah is spread in three jazirahs (islands/regions). Sometimes it also happens that when the era is of the final daur (cycle) or some such thing, then in one jazirah, the da'wah remains. Out of the 12 jazirahs, one. The da'wah shrinks so much that one... यानी (meaning) no mumin is visible. It is written, isn't it? that no mumin is visible. But Allah Ta'ala is the possessor of power. He, if He wills, then all of it, in which ifadah and istifadah and the nurturing of mumins happens. fa lamma tabayyana lahu qala a'lamu anna Allaha 'ala kulli shay'in qadir (And when it became clear to him, he said, "I know that Allah is over all things competent."). So when the reality became apparent to him... And look, this is also a thing, that the daur that is going on with us, in which the era of Sayyidna Husayn Husamuddin was such an era that all ifadah and istifadah were stopped. Sometimes it happens that it is stopped. He knows what time this is, the end of the Fath is going on. It is stopped. Because it is better than the 'aqidah (creed) getting corrupted that the thing be stopped. There should be no leakage. It is stopped. This is a favor upon Hindustan, yani (meaning) Hindustan and Pakistan, this Hind-Sind, that the ifadah is ongoing here. Otherwise, there, in Najran and in Yemen and in other places, all ifadah is stopped. So the one who teaches, I have spoken to them, they said, 'Our knowledge has rusted.' We cannot teach. There is no command. So when we can't read, what will we teach? This has become the situation. fa lamma tabayyana lahu... So when the reality became apparent to him, he said, 'I know that Allah has power over all things.' Yani (meaning), when he experienced it himself, he understood that my doubt was wrong. And he acknowledged that the Imam of the time is the master of power. This mentioned person, 'Aziz, is 'Uzayr bin Sarayah Nabi, whom Allah tested severely. wa idh qala Ibrahimu rabbi arini kayfa tuhyi al-mawta (And [mention] when Abraham said, "My Lord, show me how You give life to the dead."). And when Ibrahim requested, 'O my Lord, show me how you resurrect the dead.' Yani (meaning), he requested his Muqim, 'Tell me how the propagation of the da'wah is done, so that I may command my bab to act in the same way.' qala a wa lam tu'min qala bala wa lakin li yatma'inna qalbi (He said, "Have you not believed?" He said, "Yes, but [I ask] only that my heart may be satisfied."). Allah said, 'Do you not have iman (faith)?' He replied, 'I have iman, but I want this so that my heart may be at ease.' Yani (meaning)... Yani (meaning), have these stages not passed over you? Have you not been called to the da'wah? And through this, have you not become safe from doubts and uncertainties? So he replied, 'Yes, this is true, but now I want to satisfy my wasi with your method of teaching.' qala fa khudh arba'atan min al-tayri fa surhunna ilayka (He said, "Take four birds and commit them to yourself."). He said, 'Take four birds and tame them.' Yani (meaning), from your Hujjats, single out four and elevate them to the rank of 'uruf. thumma ij'al 'ala kulli jabalin minhunna juz'an thumma ud'uhunna ya'tinaka sa'ya (Then [after slaughtering them] put on each hill a portion of them; then call them - they will come [flying] to you in haste.). Then place a piece of them on every mountain. Then call them. Look, the incident is like this, that they will come running. Four of them will come running. Now, you cut up the birds, put four pieces, and place one piece on each mountain, and then call out. They themselves will come flying. What is the meaning of this? If this is the meaning that the world understands, which is explained in the Qur'an Sharif, then there is no meaning. It is not understood. Until its ta'wil is explained. Yani (meaning)... Yani (meaning), send them to different places and spread them out separately. Then command them to invite towards you. They will immediately say 'labbayk' (at your service) to your command. And will quickly be engaged in spreading your da'wah. wa a'lam anna Allaha 'Azizun Hakim (And know that Allah is Exalted in Might and Wise.). And know that Allah is All-Powerful, All-Wise. Yani (meaning), the Wasi has no nazir (equal) or mathil (peer), and is Hakim (wise) in his actions. mathalu alladhina yunfiquna amwalahum fi sabili Allahi ka mathali habbatin anbatat sab'a sanabila fi kulli sunbulatin mi'atu habbatin (The example of those who spend their wealth in the way of Allah is like a seed [of grain] which grows seven spikes; in each spike is a hundred grains.). Those who spend their wealth in the way of Allah, their example is like that of a grain which grows seven ears, and in each ear, there are a hundred grains. 'Baal' means the ears that come out of the wheat, that. Now you have heard the lesson of Hazrat Da'i, how there are benefits of Zakat. If you spend in the way of Allah, how much benefit there is. The meaning of Zakat is 'safai' (cleanliness). It comes from 'zakka'. So what is the meaning? And its, the meanings of Zakat are told to be very high. That Zakat is the rank after namaz (prayer). It is not fasting. Fasting comes later. Aqimu al-salata wa atu al-zakat (Establish prayer and give zakat). So, establish prayer and give Zakat. So Zakat is... for Janab Ali Salawatullah 'alayh, and Salat is for the Prophet. Meaning, follow the Prophet's shari'ah and 'Ali's ta'wil. Aqimu al-salata wa atu al-zakat. Zakat is a very special thing, that out of 40 rupees, take out one rupee. The wealth you have, to take out 1 for 40 from it, your wealth does not decrease. Rather, that 1 for 40 is Allah's right. If you do not give, your 39 are ruined. And if you give, then the 39 become pure. So this is the concept of Zakat. And Sayyidna Husayn... Isma'il bin Hiba, Allah Ta'ala says to him, he says that by giving this, your wealth does not decrease. And by not giving, there is no... it doesn't happen that your wealth increases. So the meaning of this is that wherever... and it has been told from your side that wherever there is loss for the mumineen in the dry land and the sea, it happens for this reason, that they do not give Zakat fully. They do not give it fully. They give, but less. And for that, the narration of Maulana Ja'far al-Sadiq that you heard just now, how an old woman comes and troubles a man. Suhayl was his name. And he... started to complain, came to Maulana Ja'far al-Sadiq and said, 'Look, an old woman comes to me at night, she scares me. And I am worried.' So a friend of mine said, that friend came and said that my friend has this thing. So Maulana Ja'far al-Sadiq said, 'Ask him, to whom does he give Zakat?' He asked. He said, 'I will ask tomorrow and tell.' The next day he came and asked. He said, 'I take out 1/40th. What do you do with the 1/40th?' He said, 'I take it out and distribute it with my own hands.' He said, 'This is the reason she troubles you.' That it should go up in the da'wah. It should go to the Imam of the time. If there is no Imam, then to the Da'i al-Mutlaq. If there is no Da'i, then to those who are great, it should reach there. From all places, it comes up, and then from up, it is distributed. Among whom? Eight people. The names of the eight people are all given. That how, they who are the great ones of the da'wah, they distribute it. So the names of all are given. That there is ibn sabil, 'amilin, and besides that, poor, needy, orphans, captives, and on whom a case is running, or understand, who are in such... difficulties, have committed a murder. To such people it is given. And besides that, one is also mu'allafat al-qulub. Mu'allafat al-qulub means to win over hearts. To win over their hearts, the great ones of the da'wah also send something to them. And it happens that in the Da'wah Sharif, in many places, such people who want to harm the da'wah, it is given to them so that they remain quiet, that is called mu'allafat al-qulub. Yani (meaning) those who try to establish the position of the Sahib-e-Haqq and are like the Natiq, in whose daur (era) there are seven Imams, and under each Imam, there are one hundred Hudud. Wallahu yuda'ifu li man yasha'u wa Allahu Wasi'un 'Alim (And Allah multiplies [His reward] for whom He wills. And Allah is all-Encompassing and Knowing.). And Allah, for whomever He wishes, increases. And Allah is All-Encompassing, All-Knowing. Yani (meaning) Sabiq-e-Tala, among these servants, whomever's ranks He wants to increase, He increases it according to their capacity and ability. And Sabiq-e-Tala has this power. And He also knows to whom more should be given. Bas. Sabiq-e-Tala is who? The dhat (essence) of 'Aql-e-Awwal. Sabiq-e-Tala is the dhat of 'Aql-e-Awwal. We, about Mubdi'-e-Tala, neither our thoughts go there, nor our eyes go there. So whatever is, it starts from 'Aql-e-Awwal. And between 'Aql-e-Awwal and Mubdi'-e-Tala, there is only one thing that is created, and that is the Kalimah. It is the Kalimah, that is, the Amr-e-Ilahi (Divine Command). And it is the hukm (command), that it comes out. So from 'Aql-e-Awwal, things emanate downwards, and in the 'alam-e-jismani (physical world), they reach the Natiq of the daur, who is the Prophet Salawatullah 'alayh. So from there, to whomever they wish, they give. And after Janab Prophet, it comes to Janab Wasi. After the Wasi, the Imams begin. So the Imam is the muhafiz-e-shari'at (guardian of the shari'ah). The real responsible one for the shari'ah is him. We are doing it according to our own capacity. But the real muhafiz is the person of Janab Imam. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. [Du'a in Arabic] al-Fatiha.

<!-- AUG:ibrahim-2015 lecture 7 -->

Baqarah, 158. This is page 26. 159.

Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim (In the name of God, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful). Ya ayyuha-lladhina amanu (O you who have believed)...

`wa asabahumu l-kibaru wa-lahu dhurriyyatun du`afa'u fa-asabaha i`sarun fihi narun fahtaraqat. Kadhalika yubayyinu llahu lakumu l-ayati la`allakum tatafakkarun` (and old age befalls him while he has weak offspring, and a whirlwind containing fire strikes it [the garden] and it is burned. Thus does Allah make clear to you His verses that you might give thought).

And old age befalls him and his offspring are weak, then a fiery whirlwind comes into the garden and the garden is burned. In this way, Allah makes the signs clear to you.

This has been mentioned before, and this is always mentioned in the events of Karbala, that Islam is a garden planted by the Prophet. It is the garden of Islam. And in it there are various things, just as there are in a garden. And... Yezid is a fiery whirlwind that came and turned it to ashes. `Fahtaraqat. Kadhalika yubayyinu llahu...` (so it was burned. Thus does Allah make clear...). This happens when `asabahu l-kibru` (old age befalls him), meaning its gardener, who is the guardian, when he becomes old, then his offspring are made weak. And after that, it is mentioned that the garden was burned and turned to ashes. This, in the events of Karbala, this comparison is often read.

This matter was completed yesterday. It's finished.

`Ya ayyuha-lladhina amanu anfiqu min tayyibati ma kasabtum wa-mimma akhrajna lakum mina l-'ard` (O you who have believed, spend from the good things which you have earned and from that which We have produced for you from the earth).

Meaning, O followers of the wasi, you... seekers... those esoteric sciences, pure esoteric things... Its translation... Leave the translation... they have left it blank... The translation has been left out, it's blank. Because "meaning" is the actual `ta'wil` (esoteric interpretation).

`Ya ayyuha-lladhina amanu anfiqu min tayyibati...` (O you who have believed, spend from the good things...). O people, whatever sustenance you receive, take some out from it so that... which is a thing that grows from the earth, so that you may give it. But what is its meaning? They start with "meaning." Meaning...

O followers of the wasi, you must teach the seekers those esoteric sciences, the pure esoteric things, that you have derived from exoteric knowledge. And also benefit [others] with what We have given you from the knowledge of the wasi.

Meaning, where there is a matter of `ta'wil`, `ta'wil` is given. And where there is a matter of translation, the translation is told. It should be both exoteric and esoteric. The esoteric sciences emerged from Sayyidna 'Ali, `salawatullah 'alayh`. And explain it. To whom? To your `mustafidin` (seekers). ...esoteric sciences... read it. And whatever We have given in exoteric knowledge, meaning the knowledge of the Prophet, the knowledge of `tanzil` (revelation). A continuous point is being made: `tanzil` is the Prophet's part, and the knowledge of `ta'wil` belongs to Sayyidna Wasi. And from the knowledge of the `wasi`, whatever We have given you... Read it. And from the knowledge of the `wasi`... And... And from the knowledge of the `wasi`, whatever We have given you, also provide benefit with it. Yes.

`wa-la tayammamu l-khabitha minhu tunfiquna wa-lastum bi-akhidhihi illa an tughmidu fih. Wa'lamu anna llaha ghaniyyun hamid` (And do not aim toward the defective therefrom, spending [from it] while you would not take it [ yourselves] except with closed eyes. And know that Allah is Free of need and Praiseworthy).

And do not even touch an impure thing to spend in the way of Allah. You must not touch an impure thing. Meaning, look, `tunfiquna` comes for `zakat`. Money that is `haram` (forbidden) should not go into `zakat`. You have been hearing this for two or three lessons, how Sayyidna Isma'il has explained the reasoning of `zakat`. And various things have been explained. That the `zakat` which is one rupee that you take out from forty, those thirty-nine become pure for you. And if that one rupee is not taken out, then those forty become spoiled. And Sayyidna Isma'il says that where there is theft or, you can say, loss, whether on land or at sea, all of it happens because of not paying `zakat` properly. Remember this.

...in a state where you yourselves would not take it... When you yourselves don't take it, then how can you give that same money to elders? When you yourselves... to give it... you hesitate. Don't even touch it. In a state where you yourself are not willing to take it, then how do you give it to others? For this...

...except that you overlook it. And know that Allah is free of need, Praiseworthy.

Meaning, that knowledge which is based on doubt, do not teach it. Especially when you yourself do not accept it or believe in it. Then why teach it to others? ...except that you cursorily study it. In it... So the knowledge that is wrong... knowledge is of two types. For example, knowledge is an example of water. So water is sweet and it is also brackish. The knowledge of the river has salt mixed in it, so it is brackish. So what is in this world, brackish knowledge is more, and pure knowledge, or you could say, sweet knowledge, is less. Which is told on behalf of the `Awliya Allah`. So don't even touch that either. And don't... do that. The thing which is based on doubt, do not teach it. When you teach it... Today, you see on TV, `hadith-e-sahih` (authentic traditions), `hadith-e-gharib` (unfamiliar traditions), all are being narrated daily. And whether the `hadith` is authentic or not, it is not known. But they give arguments from it. So there are several types of `hadith`. One is `hadith-e-sahih`, one is `gharib`, one is `mashhur` (famous), one is `qudsi`, and one is `mawdu`' (fabricated). `Mawdu`' means fabricated, meaning which has been created. And they are full of such `hadith`. The `sittah` which is... the `sihah sittah` (six authentic collections)... six hundred thousand `hadith` have been fabricated. Now they are taking them out from it. They are refining it, as to which is authentic, which is `gharib`, and which is this.

...especially when you yourself do not accept it or hold that belief. Then why teach it to others? Except that you cursorily study it. Do not ponder over it, nor consider it worthy of attention. And know that the wasi is free of need from the deniers and is praised by the obedient.

`Ash-shaytanu ya'idukumu l-faqra wa-ya'murukum bil-fahsha'. Wallahu ya'idukum maghfiratan minhu wa-fadla. Wallahu wasi'un 'alim` (Shaytan threatens you with poverty and orders you to immorality. But Allah promises you forgiveness from Him and bounty. And Allah is all-Encompassing and Knowing).

Shaytan threatens you with poverty and commands evil deeds. And Allah promises to forgive you on His behalf and to give you more. And Allah is vast, All-Knowing. Meaning, the Second Opponent frightens you that, remember, if you follow 'Ali, you will be deprived of the gnosis of the religion, as if it were in his possession and property. And then he says, "Come to our side, we are the `Khalifat al-Muslimin` (Caliphs of the Muslims)." ...have been made. Follow us, which is entirely an evil deed.

So look, the Second Opponent. You all know who the Second Opponent is. And he is the one who brings the thought that "We are the `Khalifat al-Muslimin`, come this way. Don't go towards 'Ali." Now look, what this wrong thing is for: he frightens with poverty. What is it? Shaytan... you... Shaytan. You understand Shaytan to be Harith bin Murrah, who was the Shaytan in the time of Adam. So he is the total Shaytan, of the entire era of `sitr` (concealment). And his disciples have existed in every era. In every era, those who teach evil deeds are the Shaytan... his disciples. So in every era, after him there is Qabil, there is Namrud, there is Fir'awn (Pharaoh), there is Haman, there is Shaddad, and there are many such people who are all... teaching... who are... calling towards the wrong path. Now see, the one who only frightens with poverty. "Man, what is this? I pray from morning to evening and my condition is not good. And those who do pray, look at their condition, how Allah blesses them." So in one era, it has come in a book that Sayyidna Sulayman Saheb... his grandfather, Sayyidna Yusuf bin Sulayman. In his time, where he used to lead prayers in Sidhpur, there a person was very poor and was very punctual in his prayers. And he was always regular in his prayers. A time also came when he was overcome by greed. "Man, what is this, so many people come to the mosque, they are much better than me, and I am so punctual in my prayers. I remain a poor man." Some thing should come. Now Shaytan possessed him. Which Shaytan? The Second Opponent. He said, "Man, this is not right." Now it came to be known that in a few days, his condition began to change. What changed? That he would come to the mosque and sell `miswak` (tooth-sticks). Miswak. In that era, whatever the currency was, `rupiya` or `anna-pai`, he would sell them. And he would give it to the person next to him... "I have `miswak`, it has come from the Imam al-Zaman (Imam of the Time)." And from Imam al-Zaman! They would immediately take it. The person next to him took it, the third one took it, the fourth one took it. And five rupees, two rupees, whatever it was... Daily, his condition started improving. As it improved, his clothes changed, his place changed, his house changed, all things changed. And he became very... quite... wealthy. A time came when a person... in that era, for travel... they used to walk from one village to another. A gentleman was crossing through the jungle. He saw that the same gentleman had also arrived there at the time of `maghrib` (evening prayer). And from the front, a woodcutter is cutting `miswak` and bringing it. He is bringing a bundle. And this person, by giving five or ten rupees, bought the entire bundle. Bought the bundle. And brought it... and sold it. He came and informed Sayyidna Yusuf. "My lord, this is what's happening." When Sayyidna Yusuf found out... "Alright, is that so?" He called him. He called him and said, "Brother, it seems your connection with the Imam has been established. We are `da'is` (missionaries), we have no such connection." Although Sayyidna Yusuf did have a connection. In your time, Mahmud... came, in the recent era. Anyway, so he said, "Brother..." He asked, "This is a strange thing, that you have reached... you are a `da'i`, you are this, of the Imam al-Zaman, but you have the grace of Allah, that from the Imam al-Zaman, `miswak` comes to you." `Miswak` is a special thing, and people like it. One after another... He said, "You... this is a great thing for you." He said, "No, there is no such thing, sir." "No, if there is, then tell me, brother. We will also benefit." "We don't know that such things can come from the Imam." When he was compelled more, or he was asked with some stratagem, at that time he said, "Yes, sir." As soon as he confessed, he [Sayyidna Yusuf] was standing, and he kicked him in the chest. And he went and fell far away. At that time, he got angry. "O deceiver, you practice deceit? You deceive the `mu'minin` (believers)?" At that time, he confessed, "Yes, I had a way, I used to do this, and I... out of greed... did this." So he started doing all this. So, after that, he gave the order, "Whosever money you have taken, return it to all of them." Now his condition changed, it kept changing. Whatever he had taken from people, he returned it all, and he came back to the same state of poverty. So this state of poverty, Shaytan frightens with it. And what does he do? He directs attention towards evil deeds. And Allah promises you forgiveness from Himself and to give more. And Allah is vast... Meaning, giving is in Allah's hands. Sustenance is not in anyone's hands. Sustenance is only in Allah's hands. We think that we are working in a bank, we are farming, we are doing this, we are doing that, we are getting money from there. You are not getting anything from there. The Giver is sitting above. So there are four things that are exclusively for Allah, for no one else. And what are those four things? `Al-Mulk Lillah` (Sovereignty belongs to Allah). The whole world, the whole earth, is Allah's earth. It is not anyone's earth. "This is my field, this is my house, this is my so-and-so." It belongs to no one. Everyone is going to go into two yards of land. Nothing belongs to anyone. Everything belongs to Allah Ta'ala. So, `al-mulku lillah`. And from the mouth of Sayyidna al-Qa'im, `salawatullah 'alayh`, this will emerge, `Liman al-mulk?` (To whom belongs the sovereignty?). `Al-mulku lillahi l-wahidi l-qahhar` (Sovereignty belongs to Allah, the One, the Prevailing). Sovereignty is for the One, the Prevailing, it is for Allah Ta'ala. This is the first thing, sovereignty. The second thing is `Al-Rizq 'ala Allah` (Sustenance is upon Allah). Sustenance is upon Allah. It is on Allah. How much to give to whom, wherever it reaches, his sustenance reaches him. So sustenance is Allah's responsibility. Every... rich and poor, and... poor, sick, whoever gets two grains, he is alive with that. So `al-rizqu 'ala Allah`. What is the third thing? `Al-rizqu 'ala Allah`, `al-mulku lillah`... and the third thing is... `Al-mulku lillah`... `Al-Amru Lillah` (The Command belongs to Allah). The command... The quality of giving commands, except for Allah, belongs to no one. We think that he is giving the command, so we have to obey, he is giving the command, he is an elder, so-and-so. Who gives the command to the elders? The One above gives. It goes up to the Imam, and from the Imam, it goes further up. So `al-rizqu 'ala Allah`, `al-mulk` is for Allah Ta'ala, and `al-amru lillahi ta'ala`. And what is the fourth thing? The fourth thing... `Al-'Ibadah` (Worship). The `ibadah`. Worship, `sajdah` (prostration). `Al-'ibadatu lillahi faqat` (Worship is for Allah alone). Worship is only for Allah Ta'ala. It is not for anyone. We think that we know the `mansus` (designated successor) very well, I know more than you, I pray a lot of... you should keep the `mansus` in your mind. But at the time of prayer, when the intention is made, it is only for Allah Ta'ala. `Lillahi faqat` (For Allah alone). That Allah who is the creator of the spiritual realm, we pray for Him, who is not visible, neither do thoughts reach Him, nor do our eyes reach Him. Nothing reaches Him, it is for Him we pray. So `al-'ibadatu lillahi ta'ala`.

And the Wasi, `alayhi al-salam`, gives you the promise, "Come, submit to me. Your sins will also be forgiven, and your ranks will also be elevated." The Wasi is the possessor of vast knowledge. He encompasses all ranks and is aware of all sciences.

`Yu'ti l-hikmata man yasha'. Wa-man yu'ta l-hikmata fa-qad utiya khayran kathira. Wa-ma yadhdhakkaru illa ulu l-albab` (He gives wisdom to whom He wills, and whoever has been given wisdom has certainly been given much good. And none will remember except those of understanding).

Allah grants wisdom to whom He wills. And he who is given wisdom has been given great wealth. No one understands it except people of intellect. Meaning, the Wasi teaches esoteric knowledge to whom he wants. And whoever receives esoteric knowledge from the Wasi, in reality, has received a very great wealth. These matters and symbols, no one understands them except the possessors of esoteric knowledge. Because esoteric knowledge is the essence and summary of exoteric knowledge.

It is he who has the appreciation for it. Those who have an appreciation for esoteric knowledge, they will understand. He who knows the value of sweet water will not go near salty water. Even though salty water is much more abundant in the world and... Look, an example from years ago, it has been 40 years... When I used to teach in Kuwait. So at that time, in conversation, I said that at night when I go, I have a book of `haqa'iq` (realities), a book of `ta'wil`, I read a little and go to sleep. So the questioner asked, it's been years, "Why do you do this?" I said, "It's a habit of mine that I fall asleep with it..." and it's... If you read a book of philosophy or some literary piece, you will fall asleep immediately. If you read an interesting book, you will keep reading it all night. The thing you don't understand... So the sciences of `haqa'iq` are not easy, they are a hard nut to crack. As much as I read... I keep reading... so after that... whatever my habit was, I told him. He said, "What is the purpose of this, sir?" I said, "Look, it's like this: in the world, from morning to evening, the media is working, you are reading newspapers, watching TV, meeting people, meeting good people, meeting bad people, listening to everyone's talk. Various things are happening." And so... But, now look, the exercise for the body... the body is separate and the soul is separate. The nourishment for the soul and the nourishment for the body are different. For the body, to be healthy, the exercise that is done, in it cycling is mentioned, so-and-so is... In that era, I gave the example, I was 40 years old then. That, "You do these things, but the best exercise is considered to be swimming, that `tayrna` (swimming) is the best, because the whole... all your limbs work." So I said, when you go to the sea, to the ocean, and swim, you swim for as much as you want, you swim for two hours. But when your father or elder says, "Come out, it's been a long time, two hours have passed." You say, "Alright, I'm coming." It takes time to come out, but as soon as you come out, you first take a shower. You will bathe with fresh water, and then you will come out. "Why is this? You were in the water for two hours." "You were in the water for two hours." So I said, "This... the body is getting sticky. The pores are... getting..." I said, "I take a shower and in five minutes, it's done." What it means is that our whole day, whatever we hear, our minds get polluted. Some things... at night, when we read... the knowledge of the `din` (religion), which is correct knowledge, we should read it, so that our minds become clean. Then he understood.

`Wa-ma anfaqtum min nafaqatin aw nadhartum min nadhrin fa-inna llaha ya'lamuh. Wa-ma li-l-zalimina min ansar` (And whatever you spend or whatever vow you make, indeed, Allah knows it. And for the wrongdoers there are no helpers).

And whatever you have spent, and whatever vow you have made, Allah knows it. And the wrongdoers have no helper. Meaning, the `da'wat` (mission) of the wasi and to spread his knowledge, for which you are doing service, and you make it incumbent upon yourself, and you make it obligatory like a vow, then listen, the wasi knows it. He will give its reward. And those who usurp the right of the wasi, they will not get any help from Allah in their religion. It's clear.

`In tubdu s-sadaqati fa-ni'imma hiya, wa-in tukhfuha wa-tu'tuha l-fuqara'a fa-huwa khayrun lakum, wa-yukaffiru 'ankum min sayyi'atikum. Wallahu bima ta'maluna khabir` (If you disclose your charitable expenditures, they are good; but if you conceal them and give them to the poor, it is better for you, and He will remove from you some of your misdeeds. And Allah, with what you do, is [fully] Acquainted).

If you disclose your charities, it is good. And if you hide it and give it to the poor, then that is better for you. And it will wipe your sins clean. And Allah is aware of your deeds. Meaning, if you affirm your `Waliyan-e-Amr` (Guardians of the Command) openly, then it is a good thing. But if in the era of concealment, due to `taqiyyah` (precautionary dissimulation), when the `mu'minin` are also generally completely unaware of the ranks of the Imams and `du'at`, then at such a time of dire need, to introduce these unaware needy of knowledge to the `sahiban-e-maratib` (possessors of ranks), then its value and station is very high, in comparison to the era of `kashf` (manifestation), in making them aware of their stations. Because at that time, this work can be done easily.

It can be done easily. In the era of `kashf`, there is not much need to explain. The Imam of the time is present, and in `kashf`, such people come who are themselves knowledgeable. Their hearts are transparent things, their minds are not polluted. Here, in the era of `sitr` (concealment), all things are there. And to wash it, there is a `dhobi ghat` (laundry place). Until the cloth is cleaned there, it will not come out from here. For that, what is it? That the translation you read above is different. That charity, do it openly and also... secretly. If you do it openly, it is good, it is fine. And if you hide it... for the poor... giving it to the poor, charity is not meant to be given by announcing it. But where there are `Waliyan-e-Amr`, to whom it is to be given, there one has to give it by announcing. So give it. There is no harm in it. But where it is to be hidden, and especially in the era of `sitr`, if you hide in this `sitr`, it is much better for you. And for this... that to affirm the `Waliyan-e-Amr` openly, and that is a good thing. But if in the era of `sitr`, an era in which... the ranks... they are not even aware of the `du'at` (missionaries). So explain to them. If there are 25 people and four are knowledgeable, then the four knowledgeable ones... they understand with a signal. But those who do not understand, you must explain the whole method to them. You must explain to them. Until they are fed, the purpose is not fulfilled. So the unaware, the needy of knowledge, introduce them to the `sahiban-e-maratib` (possessors of ranks), then its value and station is very high. In comparison to that era which is the era of `kashf`. The era of `kashf` is coming, and we are waiting for the era of `kashf`, that when it begins, it is not known. Its... and to make them aware of their ranks. Meaning, in `kashf`, there is no need to make them aware of the ranks. But here, there is so much darkness that people understand with great difficulty.

And your this action will also save you from the attacks of enemies who, through oppression and transgression, have unjustly laid claim to the station of `wasa'it`. And the Wasi, `alayhi al-salam`, is aware of your every action. Yes.

`Laysa 'alayka hudahum wa-lakinna llaha yahdi man yasha'` (Their guidance is not your responsibility, but Allah guides whom He wills).

O Messenger, to guide them, to bring them to the desired destination, is not incumbent upon you. Rather, Allah guides whom He wills. Meaning, to teach esoteric knowledge is not incumbent upon you. But this work belongs to the wasi. He is the one who will teach esoteric knowledge to the person who will wear the collar of your obedience. Look. This is specific. It has been discussed for three or four [sessions]. That the stages of `tanzil`... `tanzil` is the rulings of the `shari'ah`... the brilliant `shari'ah`, the Muhammadan `shari'ah`, the constitution of life. The Qur'an was revealed upon the Prophet for this. And all the rulings that are there - prayer, fasting, `zakat`, Hajj, etc. - all of these, the Prophet explained. And `tanzil`... `tanzil` means to descend. The Noble Qur'an descended upon the heart of the Prophet, in the form of the light of the Qur'an, and he explained it... the Prophet explained it. So all the rulings, the `tanzil`, that is the Prophet's part. But you are being told that it... you should not tell them. The esoteric part is the right of 'Ali. 'Ali, `salawatullah 'alayh`, will explain. What is it that... 'Ali's... read. `mustahdi... maqam-e-wasa'it...` And your this action will save you from the attacks of enemies. And what is it... read on. Who, through oppression and transgression, have unjustly laid claim to the station of `wasa'it`. These who are sitting as caliphs. You were told yesterday too that many such incidents happened in Islam, that... a delegation came from Rome, and from there... questions were sent... In the Torah and Zabur (Psalms), the things that were there, those people asked questions. But there was no one to answer. The Prophet had passed away. They found out that a prophet had been born in Madinah. They said, "Let's go and ask him." They came from there as a delegation. And they said, "These are our questions." The first `khalifah`... He said, "The prophet has passed away." He said, "Now who is in his place?" He said, "The first `khalifah` is there." He was asked. He had no answer. He was unable to answer. The second, unable to answer. Finally, they themselves said... And sometimes it also happened that 'Abdullah bin 'Abbas, who was the uncle of the Prophet, Sayyidna 'Abbas, and his son 'Abdullah bin 'Abbas, he was the `tarjuman-e-da'wat` (spokesman of the mission), he had great knowledge. He was also in that gathering. When the second one was unable to answer... he said, "I don't know this. The Prophet knew, but we don't know its answer." That "Where is Allah? What is He like?"... many different questions were asked. At that time, 'Abdullah bin 'Abbas said, "O 'Umar, if you don't know, then point towards the one who does know." He said, "Do you know who he is?" He said, "Yes, I know." 'Abdullah bin 'Abbas... take me. So he was taken to Sayyidna 'Ali. And Sayyidna 'Ali is... with a pen on his ear, he is sitting. And at that time, those questions were presented. He started giving the answers. And many such occasions came... such times came, and he said, "What answer should I give? My children will give it." "My children will give it." Hasan and Husayn were sitting there. So Sayyidna Hasan... it was mentioned yesterday too... that Mawlana Hasan, `salawatullah 'alayh`, gave the answer. So they did not have the answer.

`Wa-ma tunfiqu min khayrin fa-li-anfusikum. Wa-ma tunfiquna illa btigha'a wajhi llah` (And whatever good you spend, it is for your own selves. And you do not spend except seeking the countenance of Allah).

And whatever you spend, its benefit is for you yourselves. And do not spend in any other work except to obtain the pleasure of Allah. Meaning, in all the knowledge you explain, make the stations of your Imams clear. And remember, never hesitate to prove the station of the wasi. Always mention him. He is the face of the `Natiq` (Speaker-Prophet), whose gnosis has left the people of the entire era bewildered and perplexed, helpless and powerless.

`Wa-ma tunfiqu min khayrin yuwaffa ilaykum wa-antum la tuzlamun` (And whatever good you spend, it will be fully repaid to you, and you will not be wronged).

And whatever you spend from your wealth, you will get its full reward. And you will not be wronged. Meaning, from the wealth of knowledge, whatever you will spend, its good reward you will get in full. Your reward will not be wasted, and you will not be wronged.

`Lil-fuqara'i lladhina uhsiru fi sabili llah, la yastati`una darban fi l-ard, yahsabuhumu l-jahilu aghniya'a mina t-ta`affuf` (For the poor who have been restricted for the cause of Allah, unable to move about in the land. An ignorant [person] would think them self-sufficient because of their restraint).

Give your charity to those poor who have been restricted in the path of Allah. They cannot go out of the city anywhere. Because of their self-restraint... because of their self-restraint, the one who knows thinks them wealthy and self-sufficient. Although it is not so. Meaning, those who are poor in knowledge but are turned purely towards Allah and His `awliya` (friends), teach them esoteric knowledge. And those who, in the service of the `da'wat` of the wasi, are prevented from the ranks they deserve, and due to `taqiyyah`, they cannot do `da'wat` openly. They cannot go here and there. They are sitting at home. They serve silently. So from their ranks, an unaware person thinks that they do not do anything. Although they are held back because of `taqiyyah`.

`Ta'rifuhum bi-simahum, la yas'aluna n-nasa ilhafa. Wa-ma tunfiqu min khayrin fa-inna llaha bihi 'alim` (You would know them by their mark; they do not ask people persistently. And whatever good you spend - indeed, Allah is Knowing of it).

You will recognize them by their character. They do not ask people by flattering. And whatever you spend from wealth, Allah knows it well. Meaning, they have good character. They are forbearing and pious. And they have reverence. They have all those qualities that are found in the `Awliya Allah`. In the age of `taqiyyah`, they do not unnecessarily get entangled in arguments to prove their point with the exoteric people of the `shari'ah`. They are self-respecting. They maintain their dignity and selfhood. And whatever you spend from the wealth of knowledge, the Imam al-Zaman knows it. Yes. And this "spending"... we think it means giving money. `Zakat` is...
