But the spending of knowledge... that is why in `Shahrullah`, I mean Rajab, which begins, the lessons etc. that are taught, that is also an `infaq` (spending). That is also spending. That whatever Allah Ta'ala has given you, from esoteric knowledge, explain it to the people. So that they may be enriched with the wealth of faith.

`Alladhina yunfiquna amwalahum bil-layli wa-n-nahar, sirran wa-`alaniyatan, fa-lahum ajruhum `inda rabbihim, wa-la khawfun 'alayhim wa-la hum yahzanun` (Those who spend their wealth by night and day, secretly and publicly, they will have their reward with their Lord. And no fear will there be concerning them, nor will they grieve).

Those people who spend their wealth, night and day, secretly and openly, for them their reward is with their Lord. They will have no fear, and they will have no grief. Meaning, those who in the eras of concealment and manifestation, openly and secretly, spend their wealth of knowledge and material wealth, their reward is with their Cherisher. They will have no fear of going astray, and they will not grieve for having left the people of error. Yes.

Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim... [Du'a in Arabic]... Al-Fatihah.

<!-- AUG:ibrahim-2015 lecture 8 -->

Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim (In the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful). `laysa 'alayka hudahum walakinna Allaha yahdi man yasha'u` (It is not upon you to guide them, but Allah guides whom He wills). Hey Rasul (Messenger), it is not incumbent upon you to guide them, that is, to make them reach the intended destination. Rather, Allah guides whom He wills. Meaning, it is not incumbent upon you to give the teaching of *Ilm-e-Batin* (knowledge of the inner reality). But this work is of the *Wasi* (Successor). He will give the answer to that person… to that person who will wear the robe of your *ta'ah* (obedience); he will teach him *Ilm-e-Batin*.

Look, he has distinguished between the two.

Here, it has been specified for the Rasulullah, what is the matter? `Laysa 'alaykum huda` (Upon you is not their guidance). That is, O Rasul, it is not incumbent upon you to guide them, to make them reach the intended destination. You have already given the guidance; whatever are the stages of *tanzil* (revelation), you have taught the *kalimah* (declaration of faith) and made them Muslim. But now, the intended destination means you are on the path. Its meaning is that the *'alam-e-Islam* (the world of Islam) is still on the path; it has not reached the station of *iman* (faith). It is not incumbent upon you. Rather, Allah wills, whom He wills. This is the translation: "Allah wills." But what is its meaning? That is, to give the teaching of *Ilm-e-Batin* is not incumbent upon you. Meaning, the Prophet, *Salawatullah 'alayh*, has stated the *ahkamat* (rulings): how to fast, how to perform *namaz* (prayer). But its meanings, all of that is the right of Ali. Mawlana Ali, *Salawatullah 'alayh*. Meaning, to give the teaching of *Ilm-e-Batin* is not incumbent upon you. This work... but this work is of the *Wasi*. He is the one who will teach *Ilm-e-Batin* to the person who wears the robe of your *ta'ah*. "Of your *ta'ah*" means... *ta'ah* is being performed by everyone. The robe of *ta'ah*. The robe of *ta'ah* is not that. Because within them, that Islamic life is active, the *Imani* (life of faith) has not come. So, all those who are there, they are not with *ikhlas* (sincerity). That's why it wasn't there in the time of the Nabi (Prophet), and after that, the life of the Sahabah (Companions), the life of the Tabi'in (Successors), the life of the Tabi' Tabi'in (Successors of the Successors), whatever has passed, all of it is against the teachings of Rasulullah. That's why teaching them *Ilm-e-Batin* is not necessary for them. So, the one who will wear his robe with true *ikhlas*—meaning, who will take the *'ahd* (covenant)—will be taught *Ilm-e-Batin*.

`Wa ma tunfiqu min khayrin fa li anfusikum wa ma tunfiquna illa ibtigha'a wajhi-llah` (And whatever good you spend, it is for yourselves, and you do not spend except seeking the countenance of Allah). And whatever you spend, its benefit will be for you. And do not spend it on any work other than to attain the pleasure of Allah. Meaning, the knowledge... Now look, all of this... the *zahiri* (outer) matter is what? For example, for *zakat* (alms). If you are giving, then give with *ikhlas*. And what you spend, its benefit will reach you. "Benefit will reach" means that whatever... like I said yesterday, on forty rupees, one rupee of *zakat* should be given. If that one rupee is not given, then all thirty-nine are ruined. And if one rupee is given, it means the thirty-nine are purified. So who benefits? We do.

And... meaning... "meaning" you say. Meaning, the... Meaning... Meaning, the knowledge that you narrate, in it, clarify the stations of your *A'immah* (Imams). And remember, never fail to establish the station of the *Wasi*.

The narration of Mawla Ali, that is, narrating his ranks (*maratib*), should not be missed. It should be narrated, and narrated with *ikhlas*. And the most important thing is that if there is turning away from Ali, then Jahannam (Hell) is certain. Now look at how it is, that such things happen... that, in a state of helplessness, it was the era of Mu'awiyah, and the era after that. So in that, it was made compulsory for Ali to be cursed. Curses were given from the pulpits (*mimbar*). And people like us were listening. Many people would get agitated, but they had to adopt silence. So its meaning is that in such a bad time, in the time of *sitr* (concealment), adopting silence, which is *taqiyyah* (precautionary dissimulation), remaining silent is much better. But where there is an opportunity to speak, now look, one such incident has passed in Yemen, that the *mansub* (a high-ranking dignitary) there, them... the government arrested them. Because they knew the *Khatt-e-Sirr* (secret script). And the *Khatt-e-Sirr* is such a script we have, *Khatt-e-Dabi* and *Sulaymani*, these are two scripts. Due to which, correspondence used to happen between one another. Because the condition of Yemen today is the same as it was at that time. From now, one hundred and fifty... one hundred or one hundred and fifty years ago. So at that time, the *mansub* who were there were very brave (*jari*). They were very brave. So what happened was that they started communicating with each other, and those were signs of a complete rebellion. So at that time, the government became alert and arrested you people. This is from the early days of Sayyidna Taher Saifuddin, son of Ali bin Husain. It has come to be heard. So at that time, they were caught, and imprisonment of two years, three years happened. When that king of that time, the ruler (*hakim*), passed away, his son came to power. At that time, this happens, that when a republic... a king comes to power, the old prisoners are released. They were all brought forward and a promise was taken, that the deeds you have done... at the time of arrest... do you step back from them? Then we will release you. A promise was taken from everyone, and they were released. When he came, his name was Wajih. Ali Wajih. When he came, at that time it was said, "Well, you should declare *bara'ah* (disassociation) from Shaykh Ali." Shaykh Ali was Sayyidna Taher Saifuddin. "And declare *bara'ah* from Ali." Meaning, Ali... until there. Ali *Rahmatullah 'alayh*. So what did he do? He took one step forward and bowed his neck. That you can cut off my neck, but *bara'ah* from Ali will not happen. Do whatever you want. *Bara'ah* from Ali will not happen. At that time, two or three times he said it, and after that, he stepped forward, patted his back, and said that when you honor your master so much and are in his obedience, then go, I release you. You will also be in our obedience. And then all the things that were... were restored to them. The shops that had been looted, this and that had happened, all of it was returned. So this courage... there are many such stories. In the time of Sayyidna Taher Saifuddin, in the time of Sayyidna Ja'far, if you read, you will find out... if you read the book *Kutub al-Az'an*, or *Kutub al-Andal*, where the stories of courage are. So the one who stays on the *Haqq* (Truth), he is brave. He is not a coward (*buzdil*). And he puts his point across and stays. In teaching *Ilm-e-Batin*, he does not step back. Remember that this is what? Make the stations of the *A'immah* manifest. Clarify them. And what is after that? And remember that never... in manifesting the station of the *Wasi*... do not fail. What is the rank of Ali? State it. Let the neck be cut, but he... The opportunity for *bara'ah* comes, let the neck be cut. If the opportunity to curse comes, that has also happened, and curses have been given, but *bara'ah* is not... *bara'ah* once... once it leaves the circle (*halqah*), it leaves the region, it is finished. Then it is difficult to return.

So... yes. He is the face of the *Natiq* (Speaker-Prophet). Always mention him; he is the face of the *Natiq*, through whose *ma'rifah* (gnosis) the people of the entire era are bewildered and wandering, helpless and incapable. Yes.

`wa ma tunfiqu min khayrin yuwaffa ilaykum wa antum la tuzlamun` (And whatever good you spend, it will be fully repaid to you, and you will not be wronged). And whatever you spend from your wealth, you will get its full reward, and you will not be wronged. Meaning, from the wealth of knowledge, from the wealth of knowledge, whatever you spend... will spend, its good... its goodly return you will get in full. Your reward will not be wasted, and no wrong will be done.

`li-l-fuqara'i alladhina uhsiru fi sabili-llahi la yastati'una darban fi-l-ardhi yahsabuhumu al-jahilu aghniya'a...` the ignorant one thinks them wealthy `...min at-ta'affufi` (for the poor who are constrained in the way of Allah, unable to travel through the land. An ignorant [person] would think them rich because of their self-restraint). Give your *sadaqah* (charity) to those poor people who are held back in the way of Allah. They cannot go out of the city anywhere. The unknowing one, due to their self-respect, considers them wealthy and independent. Whereas it is not so. Meaning, those who are *faqir ila-llah* (poor towards Allah), who are turned towards His *awliya'* (friends), `khalisan li-wajhi-llah` (sincerely for the sake of Allah), teach them *Ilm-e-Batin*. Those who, while serving the *da'wah* (mission) of the *Wasi*, are held back from the ranks of which they are deserving. And due to *taqiyyah* (precautionary dissimulation), they cannot go here and there for the purpose of proclaiming the *da'wah*. They are sitting at home. They are sitting. They serve silently. So the person unaware of their ranks thinks that they do nothing at all. Whereas, they are held back due to *taqiyyah*.

Yes. ... `ta'rifuhum bi-simahum la yas'aluna an-nasa ilhafa wa ma tunfiqu min khayrin fa-inna Allaha bihi 'alimun` (You will recognize them by their mark; they do not beg from people importunately. And whatever good you spend - indeed, Allah is Knowing of it). You will recognize them by their character. They do not ask people through flattery. And whatever you spend from your wealth, Allah knows it well. Meaning, they are of good character, forbearing and pious, and well-mannered. The sum of qualities that are found in the *Awliya'-Allah* (Friends of Allah) are in them. In the time of *taqiyyah*, they do not unnecessarily get entangled with the people of the manifest *shari'ah* (law) to prove a point. They are self-respecting, they maintain their honor and self-respect. And whatever you spend from the wealth of knowledge, the Imam of the Time knows it.

Yes. `alladhina yunfiquna amwalahum bi-l-layli wa-n-nahari sirran wa 'alaniyatan fa-lahum ajruhum 'inda rabbihim wa la khawfun 'alayhim wa la hum yahzanun` (Those who spend their wealth by night and day, secretly and publicly, their reward is with their Lord, and there will be no fear concerning them, nor will they grieve). Those people who spend their wealth, day and night, secretly and openly, for them is their reward with their Lord. They have no fear, and they will not grieve. Meaning, those who in the times of *sitr* (concealment) and *zuhur* (manifestation), openly and inwardly, spend their knowledge-based wealth and property, their reward is with their *Murabbi* (Nourisher). They have no fear of going astray and do not grieve for leaving the people of error. Meaning, they do not regret it.

`alladhina ya'kuluna ar-riba la yaqumuna illa kama yaqumu alladhi yatakhabbaṭuhu ash-shaytanu mina-l-mass` (Those who consume interest do not stand except as one stands who is being beaten by Satan into insanity). Those people who consume interest, they do not stand except like that person whom Satan has touched and driven mad. Meaning, those who commit *ghuluw* (exaggeration) in the station of the *Hudud* (ranks of the mission), that is, they raise them above their rank, cannot impress their wrong, false belief on anyone's mind. Their example is of that person who commits *khuluw* (diminution), meaning, he lowers the people of rank from their station. Satan has made him his disciple and misguided him. He... he has lost his senses. The capacity to distinguish between right and wrong is no longer there. In wandering astray, the *Ghali* (exaggerator) and the *Khali* (diminisher) are both equal. The one who exceeds the limit and the one who lessens from the limit...

Look, *Ghali* and *Khali* are two terms. That those who commit *ghuluw* raise someone from their rank, and lower someone from their rank. Both are wrong. *Ghuluw* and *khuluw*. There was a time, pre-Islam, when it was said about the Jews and Christians that they were people who committed *ghuluw* or *khuluw*, regarding 'Isa. But when the time of the Prophet came, of Islam, then at that time, from today's perspective, it is the Shia and the Sunni. The Sunnis diminish the rank of Janab Ali, and the Shias commit a lot of *ghuluw*. So neither *ghuluw* is good, nor *khuluw* is good. Whosever rank it may be, but to commit *ghuluw* in it, this is a sin, in a way. Why? Now look, *ghuluw* and *khuluw*. There are two. With *ghayn* it is *ghuluw*, with *kha'* it is *khuluw*. What we do is, whatever rank someone has, we keep them there. That's it. Remember this. So we walk the middle path. `khayr al-umuri awsatuha` (The best of matters is the middle way). So, the... of *ghayn* there are a thousand, and of *kha'* there are one hundred. One hundred and... between a thousand, there are five hundred. A thousand and between one hundred, there are five hundred. We... `Allahumma thabbitna` (O Allah, make us firm). With `tha'`. `Tha'` is five hundred. That O Allah, keep us steadfast. In what? In the obedience of Allah, in the obedience of the Rasulullah, in the obedience of the Imam of the Time, in the obedience of the Hudud al-Din. This is... So to remain steadfast, this is the sign of the middle. `khayr al-umuri awsatuha`. We neither commit *ghuluw*, nor *khuluw*. And the ranks of the Prophet *Salawatullah 'alayh*, if you read them you will know. This is a cursory matter, but when you read the books of *haqa'iq* (realities), you will know what his station was in reality. And Mawlana Ali *Salawatullah 'alayh*, although he is a *Wasi* Prophet, in the stages of *tanzil* you see that in Islam, he was a subordinate of the Prophet, and he became the *Wasi* Prophet. But what was his reality? That in the era of *sitr*, from Adam until here, a man was not born who was like Ali. This is... the rank after the Qa'im who is to come, you hold. So he is established in the hereafter. But by Allah's command, just as Ali was manifest... in Islam when it was ending, that is, when the Prophet's eyes closed, at that time the first caliph came, the second came, the third came. At that time, you were silent. In the same way, you are in your... But your rank, those who know, the educated people, who read *haqa'iq*, read *daqa'iq*, they know what... which station you were at. To recognize their true rank is not an easy task. Meaning, the matter of *Tawhid* (Oneness of God), *Tawhid* means *Rabb al-'Alamin* (Lord of the Worlds)... it is a very difficult matter, the matter of *Tawhid*. That where is Allah, what is He like... which comes in the sentences of the *'ahd*, that what is He like, where is He, in which direction is He? You cannot say anything about Allah. So *Tawhid* is a very difficult issue, and after that, if there is a delicate issue, it is the ranks of the Prophet and Janab Ali *Salawatullah 'alayh*. That in them, if you make even a slight difference, if you mix his rank with his, or his with his, then that is not right. So, to know the rank of the Prophet and Janab Ali is not an easy task. It has so many... such... complexities within it, that you cannot even imagine. The one who knows, knows what his rank is. And you cannot tell everyone. Everyone's capacity (*zarf*) is not that great. So according to the capacity, one has to tell. This is the correct... thing. So *Khali* and *Ghali*, both are equally... equally wrong. And the one who increases the limit and the one who decreases it, what is it?... not from us. Both are on the same level.

After that is... `dhalika...` `dhalika bi-annahum qalu innama al-bay'u mithlu ar-riba wa ahalla Allahu al-bay'a wa harrama ar-riba` (That is because they say, "Trade is just like interest." But Allah has permitted trade and has forbidden interest). This punishment was caused because they say that trade is also like consuming interest. Whereas Allah has made trade lawful and interest unlawful. Meaning, they say that to clarify the ranks of the *Awliya'-Allah* is equivalent to committing *ghuluw*. Whereas, making known the *ma'rifah* (gnosis) of the *Awliya'-Allah* and the Anbiya' (Prophets) and Awsiya' (Successors) has been deemed permissible by the *Saheb-e-Ta'leem* (Master of Teaching), and He has forbidden *ghuluw*.

Yes. Right. `fa-man ja'ahu maw'izatun min rabbihi fa-ntaha fa-lahu ma salafa wa amruhu ila-llah wa man 'ada fa-ula'ika ashabu an-nari hum fiha khalidun` (So whoever has received an admonition from his Lord and desists, for him is what is past, and his affair is with Allah. But whoever returns [to it] - those are the companions of the Fire; they will abide eternally therein). Then, to whom advice reached from his Lord, and he stopped, then for him... his past wealth is lawful. His matter is with Allah. And those who did the same act again, they are the people of Hell. They will remain in it forever. Meaning, whomever his *Mufeed* (teacher) guided, and he desisted from committing *ghuluw*, then for his... on this false belief, there will be no accountability. And Imam al-Salam will deal with him understandingly. But whoever becomes the perpetrator of this same wrong belief again, he will be counted among the people of the *Da'wat-e-Zalaal* (Mission of Error) and will remain in it forever.

`yamhaqu Allahu ar-riba wa yurbi as-sadaqat wa-llahu la yuhibbu kulla kaffarin athim` (Allah destroys interest and gives increase for charities. And Allah does not like every sinful disbeliever). Allah obliterates interest and increases charities. And Allah... Allah does not befriend the sinner-disbeliever. Meaning, Imam al-Salam obliterates the wrong beliefs of *khuluw* and *waqifi* and elevates the ranks of the true-believing Momineen. And Imam al-Salam dislikes both the *munkir* (denier) and the *Ghali* (exaggerator).

`inna alladhina amanu wa 'amilu as-salihati wa aqamu as-salata wa atu az-zakata lahum ajruhum 'inda rabbihim wa la khawfun 'alayhim wa la hum yahzanun` (Indeed, those who believe and do righteous deeds and establish prayer and give zakah will have their reward with their Lord, and there will be no fear concerning them, nor will they grieve). Those who believed and did good deeds, and established prayer and gave *zakat*, for them... their reward is with their Lord. And they do not fear, and do not grieve.

Look, this is the translation. `aqamu as-salata wa atu az-zakat`. This is to perform *namaz* and to give *zakat*. Their reward is with Allah Ta'ala... their reward is present. But what is the real meaning? Meaning...

Meaning, those who believed in the *Wasi* and the *A'immah*, and appointed their *khalifah* (successor) in their rank, and established the manifest *da'wah*, and became pure by giving the oath of allegiance and accepting the *Wasi*, for them, their reward is with Imam al-Salam. And they have no fear of going astray, and they do not regret having brought faith.

`ya ayyuha alladhina amanu ittaqu-llaha wa dharu ma baqiya mina ar-riba in kuntum mu'minin` (O you who have believed, fear Allah and give up what remains [due to you] of interest, if you should be believers). O Momineen, fear Allah, and whatever interest remains with anyone, leave it, if you are truly Momineen. Meaning, beware of opposing the Imam, and in your belief, if any... if any traces of *ghuluw* have remained, remove them as well, if you truly love the *Wasi*.

Give everyone their due right. That is the point.

`fa-in lam taf'alu fa'dhanu bi-harbin mina-llahi wa rasulihi wa in tubtum fa-lakum ru'usu amwalikum la tazlimuna wa la tuzlamun` (And if you do not, then be informed of a war from Allah and His Messenger. But if you repent, you may have your principal - [thus] you do no wrong, nor are you wronged). If you do not act according to the command, then be prepared for a war from Allah and His Rasul. If you repent, then you will get the principal wealth back. You will neither be the oppressor, nor will you be oppressed. Meaning, if you do not leave the false belief of *ghuluw*, then be prepared to hear the decisive proofs from Allah and His Rasul, after which they will demote you from your ranks. And if you repent, you will surely receive your right and rank. You will be saved from the oppression of *ghuluw*, and your right will also not be taken; you will not be wronged.

`wa in kana dhu 'usratin fa-naziratun ila maysaratin wa an tasaddaqu khayrun lakum in kuntum ta'lamun` (And if someone is in hardship, then [let there be] postponement until an ease. But if you give [from the principal] as charity, it is better for you, if you only knew). If the debtor is in difficulty, then until he has ease, give him respite. And if you forgive everything, the principal as well, then that is much better for you, if you understand. Meaning, the one who establishes the *Da'wat-e-Haqq* (Mission of Truth), if he is in the time of *taqiyyah*, then give him respite and permission until the time of ease, so that he may serve the *da'wah* as he ought to. And O people of rank, if you forgive that deficient servant, it is better for you, if you are aware of the limits of Allah's Deen.

That's it. ... Two hundred and eighty-one. ... [Recites prayers] ... Al-Fatihah.

<!-- AUG:ibrahim-2015 lecture 9 -->

This is 281. This is 282. It has been read, but let it be, no problem. *Wattaqu*. *Wattaqu*, right? *Wattaqu*. Yes, sir.

*A'udhu billahi minash shaitanir rajim* (I seek refuge in Allah from the accursed Satan). *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim* (In the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful). Just a moment, sir. This... *Wattaqu*... *A'udhu billahi minash shaitanir rajim* (I seek refuge in Allah from the accursed Satan). *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim* (In the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful). *Wattaqu yawman turja'una fihi ilallah, thumma tuwaffa kullu nafsin ma kasabat wahum la yuzlamun* (And fear a Day when you will be returned to Allah. Then every soul will be paid in full what it has earned, and they will not be wronged).

Fear that Day when you will be returned to Allah. Then every person will be given full reward according to their deeds. And no injustice will be done to them. Meaning... look, this is... the translation of this verse is that you should fear the day when Qiyamah will come and you will be returned to Allah. Everyone has to return to Him. *Inna lillahi wa inna ilayhi raji'un* (To Allah we belong and to Him we will return). Then every person will be rewarded according to their deeds. According to their deeds, reward will be given, and no injustice will be done to them. Meaning Allah is Just (*'Adil*), He does not do injustice.

Now, its meaning which Sayyidna Husayn has derived is this: that is... Meaning, avoid the opposition of *Bab-e Salmani*, which is linked to the *yawm* (day), so that you may progress to the *Lahut* of the Imam in its wake. Yes. *Bab-e Salmani*... The rank of *Bab-e Salmani* is *Bab al-Abwab*, and that too is... see, it becomes very big. And that too is the fourth rank. By fourth, it is meant that first, there are *Bab*s, *Bab* meaning a door. There are several doors to reach the Imam. One is the *Bab-e Zahir* (Apparent Gate), which exists in the apparent world, in India, Pakistan, and Yemen, in these regions. And after that, there is the *Bab-e Batin* (Hidden Gate). The *Bab-e Batin* is where the Imam resides at that time. It is called *Batin* because they are hidden. At this time, they are in seclusion. And after that, when their rank increases, it is called *Bab-e Salsal*. *Salsal* is a branch within it, an addition. *Salsal*. So, many of our *Bab al-Abwab* have reached the rank of *Salsal*. They are called *Salsal*. Some are at the rank of *Batin*, so they are hidden and among the high-ranking personages, so they are *Bab-e Batin*. Except for those who are in the apparent world, they are *Bab-e Zahir*. Now, after this, when the *Bab-e Salsal* also ends, a high peak is reached, which is called *Bab-e Salmani*. And what is that? It is the rank of Salman al-Farsi, which is the rank of *Inbi'asi*.

So, *Bab-e Zahir*, *Bab-e Batin*, *Bab-e Salsal*, and *Bab-e Inbi'asi*. *Inbi'asi* means one who is raised. This congregation (*majma'*)—when they are gathered, what is it called? Let me explain it. It is called a congregation. All the souls (*arwah*), which souls? The lower souls (*sugharat*), when they are gathered from different places—they have different structures (*haikal*) and different physical forms (*nasut*). The structure (*haikal*) is different, and the *nasut* is also separate. The *nasut* is natural (*taba'i*), skin-like (*jildi*), and special (*khaas*). Now, from here, this is called a congregation. And when the *Bab al-Abwab* presents this to the Imam, saying, "This is the entire revenue (*mahsul*) that has been collected, the revenue of the entire *jamat* or the people of truth (*Ahl-e Haqq*)," that "these lower souls have gathered with me, and I am handing them over to you." When it is handed over, it is not called a congregation, but it becomes *Lahut*. The structure, when those lower souls reach the Imam, they are called *haikal*, or *Imamat*, or *Lahut*. *Lahut*, *haikal*, and *Imamat* are the same thing.

So, the meaning is that *Bab-e Salmani*, which is associated with the *yawm* (day), has a very high rank. It is associated with the *yawm*. Its... what is it... ...avoid its opposition so that you may progress to the *Lahut* of the Imam in its wake. If one reaches *Bab-e Salmani*, or you could say reaches *Bab al-Abwab*, it means that there is a guarantee that you will reach the Imam's *Lahut*. Sometimes it happens that some people have sins, doubts, obligations (*mujibat*), destructive sins (*mubikhat*); there are many things because of which they are stopped somewhere. It is possible that they might be stopped. But, generally, if we understand that one reaches the *Bab-e Salmani*, this *Bab*, then after the *Bab*, the ranks, having been elevated, take on the form of *Lahut*. And *Lahut* is an abstract form. *Nasut* is a visible thing. *Nasut* means... it is a word from the Kutchhi language, meaning that which has density (*kasafat*). I mean, the Imam is transparent (*shaffaf*), but why does he appear? When he is transparent, why does he appear? It is because of our shortcomings (*qusurat*). A person sees what the mirror shows. That is our reflection that falls on him. The structure of the neglectful ones (*muqassirin*) is different, and... there are different structures, and the *nawasit* are also different. The structures are different, and the *nasut*s are also different. So the visible thing is called *nasut*. What is not seen is *Lahut*.

Meaning, avoid the opposition of *Bab-e Salmani*, which is linked to the *yawm*, so that you may progress to the *Lahut* of the Imam in its wake. In this structure... In this structure, each one will be given a rank according to their deservingness (*istihqaq*). And... was given. was given. was given. And certainly, there, the matter of injustice... ...will not be... is not there. That's it. This means that... whoever... justice (*'adl*) is established by Allah. Whatever one does, that will be the result. Sayyidna Ja'far al-Sadiq used to say, "Allah says, 'As you are, so shall it be for you.'" Whatever one does in this world, that is what one will find.

*Ya ayyuhalladhina amanu idha tadayantum bidaynin ila ajalin musamma faktubuh* (O you who have believed, when you contract a debt for a specified term, write it down). O believers, when you... any... for a specified term... transact a loan amongst yourselves... Look, the discussion from the beginning is about loans (*qarz*) and it came about in the context of Zakat, but the meaning is very high and the esoteric interpretation (*ta'wil*) is very different. If you are doing a transaction with someone, say I have given you 50,000, then it should be written down immediately. Write it down, you both amongst yourselves, or make a contract (*iqrar*) that I will give this much at this time. It is necessary to write this. *faktubuh*.

Yes. Write it down. The apparent ruling is established in its place. its place is established. No one can change it. What is this? It is the *Shari'at-e Muhammadi*. The second part of *Da'a'im al-Islam* has it written how, if a loan is given, it should be repaid. I mean, the esoteric meaning... The esoteric meaning of it is that the lender (*mutadayyin*), meaning the one giving the loan, is an example of that person who... is worthy of a high rank in the faith (*din*)... is considered... and the one above him performs *nass* (appointment) and *tawqif* (confirmation) upon him, but the appointed one (*mansus*) is unable to assume his position (*qiyam*) immediately for some reason or another. See, this also happens, that *nass* is performed, but he cannot perform *qiyam*. There is a reason for it. There is a reason of distance, a reason of weakness, or he cannot perform *qiyam*. Due to distance or... or due to childhood. There have been many such Imams who, within four years, were granted *Lahut*, like Maulana Imam Tayyib and others.

So, for them... in an active position... they are a *hujjat*. They see with their own sight, but the missionaries (*du'at*) and others, as per the Imam's command, work accordingly. So in this period... So in this period, to work on his behalf, a person is appointed to stand in. Is appointed. Is... appointed. Who is a deputy (*wakil*) or trustee (*mustawda'*)... or a guarantor (*kafil*) or trustee (*mustawda'*). *Kafil* or *mustawda'*. See, it means an acting member. Someone... look, you be in charge for now. I am writing this down and this is the... I will return it in this period. Then... Then when the time to hand over the trust (*amanat*) comes, and the entitlements (*mawaj*) which... that the guarantor (*kafil*) used to receive during that period of service (*khidmat*), come to an end, and the entitlements and divine support (*ta'yidat*) are transferred from the appointed one to his... See, this is the matter. This is a special point. That the one who was appointed... who was made a guarantor, he has them until the time that the one who was appointed by *nass* grows from a child to an adult. As soon as that happened, the divine support shifted. Just like it happened with Maulana Sayyidna Ja'far. Maulana Muhammad ibn Fahd was very old, a scholar (*'alim*), he had all the qualities. But you... then Sayyidna Ja'far appointed you. Before you was Abdullah. On behalf of Sayyidna Hammad, he was made a trustee. And who is a trustee? One who fulfills a trust. So what were you called? The Trustee of the Mission (*Amin-e Da'wat*). And in opposition to him, Da'ud Qutb was the Traitor of the Mission (*Kha'in-e Da'wat*). He had the same thing. The lower souls from all of India... Hindustan and Pakistan were one then... so at that time, however many lower souls there were, they were all with Da'ud Qutb. And when the time came, when the time of Sayyidna Ja'far came, when he grew from a child to an adult, then it was time to hand it over. Just as Sayyidna Sulayman did with Da'ud, here, what happened? Muhammad ibn Fahd immediately handed it over. What did he do? You know the incident? He was teaching a lesson (*sabaq*). Who? Muhammad ibn Fahd. Muhammad ibn Fahd was such a personality that he was an ocean of knowledge. And you are teaching a lesson. While teaching the lesson, that... someone in front is asking a question, and you are answering. So at that time, you gave an answer, but the person in front was not satisfied. When he was not satisfied, what did they do? Sayyidna Ja'far was sitting next to him. He was a child. In comparison to you, he was a child. He was young in age. And he said, "Maulana, if you say so, I will explain." "Give me permission." It's not like he just blurted it out on his own. "Give me permission." He said, "Alright, speak." You explained the very same thing that Sayyidna Muhammad ibn Fahd was explaining, in two sentences. And the person in front was convinced. As soon as he was convinced, Muhammad ibn Fahd said: *Talab-e mawad al-sariyah al-'an ila Ja'fariyyah, la Muhammadiyyah* (The flow of spiritual sustenance is now towards the Ja'fari, not the Muhammadi). Now those divine supports which were from the Imam, they are no longer Muhammadi, but have become Ja'fari. And after that, he said, "Ja'far, from now on, you don't need to study with me. You have become capable." This... this also happens. So where the rank of the guarantor... of the trustee... when the time comes to hand over the trust, the time has come, and from the guarantor those entitlements... that were for the period of service... the service that was being done, after that it ends, what happens? The entitlements and the divine support, the flowing ones, the entitlements that come from the Imam... if it doesn't come from the Imam, he doesn't have the right to explain. And the person in front cannot even tell him. So the teachers (*mufid*) who are there, who teach, they have to acknowledge that I am not speaking on my own. Someone is speaking, and I am explaining. If one says it on their own, "I have understood this much, I have done this," then it is wrong. All the divine support ends.

So... it is remembered... So what happens? It is transferred to the appointed one. This is the specified term (*ajal musamma*). Meaning, the appointed time. Then the appointed one, taking the affairs of the mission (*da'wat*) into his own hands, gives the guarantor his due right. Yes. Now, how does he give him his right? When Sayyidna Ja'far became... and... Sayyidna Muhammad ibn Fahd said that he has served a great deal. He said, "No, it won't be like this." Now there was a back-and-forth between them. For the rank. Not for the rank, I mean, it's a... a polite refusal. That... Muhammad ibn Fahd was saying, "No, you have to do it, you are the Imam." He said, "No, I won't do it. It doesn't seem right to me, in front of the public and all." "I will lead the prayer, and you will stand behind." "Either you stand behind me..." No, he would not stand. Now, the debate went on and on, until it was decided, "Alright, I will lead the prayer, and for the lesson, at the time of the lesson, you step aside, and... at the time of prayer, you step aside." So, this arrangement was made. It's all there in the life story of Sayyidna Ja'far. But it is mentioned, and at that time, Sayyidna Muhammad ibn Fahd wanted to go to Yemen, but he couldn't go. So it is mentioned that Sayyidna... Ja'far is thinking that he is not praying behind him, but it is written that for five years he kept praying behind him. When he made the intention, Sayyidna Ja'far, then he would go and stand. Sayyidna Muhammad ibn Fahd has a special rank. He is among the lone missionaries (*du'at munfaridin*). So this also happens.

And gives him his due right. Sayyidna Ja'far became a missionary (*da'i*). He didn't remain a missionary, he became a *hujjat* (proof). He became a *hujjat*. This is a very unique thing. For all the ranks... And how is that? That Sayyidna Ja'far became a *hujjat*. He did not remain a missionary. From a missionary, he progressed and became a *hujjat*. He came to the rank of *hujjat*. So in the era of Qiyamah, this was bound to happen. In the era of Qiyamah, Sayyidna Ja'far is the first who is in the rank of *hujjat*. And he is the greatest among the esteemed missionaries. So as soon as you progressed in rank, the position of missionary became vacant. He appointed Muhammad ibn Fahd to his own seat. This is a special thing, that the act of handing over was done.

Meaning, *faktubuhu*, write it down. You are lending, I am... the matter that was mentioned above in the translation... meaning *faktubuhu*, what is it? That you should mention it in front of the people of ranks. There is no need to announce it to people, but those who are studying the lesson or are special people, who are your members, tell them, "Look, this is going to happen." And make it clear. Make it clear. *Walyaktub baynakum katibun bil 'adl* (And let a scribe write between you in justice). *Wala ya'ba katibun an yaktuba kama 'allamahullah, falyaktub* (Let no scribe refuse to write as Allah has taught him. So let him write). And among you, whoever is the scribe, he, without addition or subtraction, should write it down completely and correctly. And the scribe should not refuse to write. Because the scribe is appointed. Just as Da'ud Qutb was the scribe of the time. For... Sayyidna Da'ud ibn 'Ajabshah. And he used to write what Sayyidna Da'ud dictated. But he used to make Sayyidna Sulayman write with his own hand. Sayyidna Da'ud ibn 'Ajabshah used to dictate and make Sayyidna Sulayman write. He used to say, "You write." So he made him write with his own hands. This is called breaking the pen. He cut his hand. By writing. But, he did not accept, he did not accept. When animosity or enmity arose, he changed. So what happens? Without addition or subtraction, he should write it down completely and correctly. And the scribe should not refuse to write. Rather... Allah has taught him, he should write it properly. Yes. Meaning... now this is... as a scribe, that if we are doing a transaction, for that it is so, that he should write what I am saying. He should not add his own words. But what is the meaning of it? That the teacher (*mufid*)... That the *mufid* should not refuse to teach. Yes. The *mufid* should not refuse. Whatever Maulana Imam of the Time has taught him, he should explain it. Whatever has been taught, he should definitely explain it. What is it? You might have learned from anywhere, but where does the divine support come from? All divine support comes from the Imam. No one teaches. You go and study with a missionary, study with a *mu'in*, study with anyone. But where does it come from? All of it comes from the Imam, the divine support. Because these are divine sciences. Whatever Maulana Imam has taught him, he should definitely explain it. *Walyumlil...* *Walyumlililladhi 'alayhil haqqu walyattaqillaha rabbahu wala yabkhas minhu shay'a* (And let the one who has the obligation dictate, and let him fear Allah, his Lord, and not leave anything out of it). And indeed, the one upon whom is the right, should dictate. Should dictate. Should dictate. And he should fear Allah, who is his Lord, and from the right, he should not diminish. He should not diminish. Meaning, the accepted one (*maqbul*), the appointed one (*mansus*), should give his guarantor the right of service (*haqq-e khidmat*). The right of service and... and the reward (*ajr*)... full. The reward (*jaza*)... full. The reward (*jaza*) should be given in full. And of the Imam of the Time... See, the same thing happened, that as soon as Sayyidna Ja'far came, he gave the full right of the guarantor, and made him the missionary, appointed him to the rank of missionary.

And fearing the Imam of the time in his heart, he should give it in full according to what is deserved. Give it in full. Give it in full. Do not diminish it in the slightest. Yes. *Fa'in kanalladhi 'alayhil haqqu safihan aw da'ifan aw la yastati'u an yumilla huwa falyumlil waliyyuhu bil 'adl* (But if the one who has the obligation is of limited understanding or weak or unable to dictate himself, then let his guardian dictate in justice). Then if the one on whom the right is, he is foolish or weak or does not have the strength to dictate, then his guardian, without... diminishing or increasing, should... what is on his conscience... should dictate. Should dictate. Meaning, it often happens that for a child, there is a matter of inheritance or something like that, so he dictates for him. So the one who is writing, if he is an adult, he should not add or subtract anything on his own, but write only what needs to be written. This is in the general sense. If the accepted one, meaning the appointed one, is in the stage of being nurtured (*hadd-e tarbiyat*)... *hadd-e tarbiyat* meaning a child. And he is... So if the accepted, appointed one is in the stage of being nurtured, or is incapable of assuming his position (*qiyam*), or for some special reason cannot give his guarantor his right, then his guardian (*mukim*), with justice, should give the right on his behalf. Yes. The same thing I just explained. *Wastashhidu...* *Wastashhidu shahidayni min rijalikum* (And bring to witness two witnesses from among your men). And make two men witnesses over it. Yes, this is... the testimony requires two witnesses. Meaning, for that... for that guarantorship (*kafalat*), from among the people of ranks, make two men who are at the rank of teacher (*mufid*) as witnesses. Make them aware. Yes, see, at the rank of *mufid*. So there are such people, *mafasih* (eloquent ones), among them, who can teach others. In that... Now the *mafasih* are not... at the rank of *mufid*, at a high rank. Sometimes what happens? They are *mafasih*, but they do not get that rank of *mufid*. What is that like? They do not come under the category of male (*zakar*), they come under the category of neuter (*untha*). There are also such people who are called *untha*. They cannot teach the lesson or do some other work. So they are in the category of feminine (*mu'annas*). And... masculine (*muzakkar*)... for this reason, the special people are called the men of the mission (*rijal-e da'wat*). The men of the mission are separate, and the neuters are separate.

Now see, after this, it comes again. *Fa'in lam yakuna rajulayni...* (And if there are not two men...). Now sometimes if two men are not found, two such teachers are not found, then what should be done? *...farajulun wamra'atani mimman tardawna minash shuhada'* (then a man and two women from those whom you accept as witnesses). *...fatudhakkira ihdahumal ukhra.* (so that if one of them errs, the other can remind her). Yes. If two men are not available, then one man and two women should be chosen, whose testimony you accept. And make them witnesses. So that if one woman forgets, she can be reminded by the other. Yes. It happens with women. And two women are required. So that... the matter is completed. Meaning, if two teachers... Meaning, if two male teachers are not found, then one teacher and two other students (*mustafidin*) whom you like, choose them. So that if two students... Now what are the women? They are the students. Now wherever in the Quran Majid such a thing has come, where women are mentioned, it is not about women, but about students. In the story of our Prophet Musa, when Shuaib Alayhis Salam, who was the last prophet of which era? The era of Ibrahim. Of the era of Ibrahim. Prophet Ibrahim's... the era that was there, the era of Ibrahim, was ending, and the last prophet was who? It was Prophet Shuaib. And Prophet Musa was... from there towards Madyan he went and... Shuaib Alayhis Salam... Now a connection had to be made. He had to receive divine support from them, the last of the era of Ibrahim, the many things, lower souls etc. were there, that had to be connected with Musa. So when he went there, he saw that there is a well. This... all of this is in the allegorical interpretations (*ta'wil*) etc. There is a well where a large crowd is gathered and people are drawing water. The example of water is that of knowledge. So a well means there is a great man who is giving knowledge to everyone. In that, there are many people standing and receiving it, drinking water. They are taking water, collecting it. Now, there are two women as well. They are not getting a chance. Two women, two girls were there. And they are not getting a chance. He kept watching. Now Musa Alayhis Salam is famous for having a majestic temperament (*jalali kaifiyat*). He saw that everyone is getting it, they are not getting it. What's the matter? He went and gave to both of them, you... drank... He made them drink water... I mean, he gave them water. So those people were very happy that a stranger came and did us this favor. And after that, they went and told their father... see this is the means. They told their father that such and such a man has come from afar, and he did us this favor. Then Prophet Shuaib called him and married one of them to him. Meaning, he made her his student.

If some mistake happens among the two female students, she forgets or makes a mistake, then the other student should correct her mistake. Should correct her mistake. Yes. *Wala ya'bash shuhada'u idha ma du'u. Wala tas'amu an taktubuhu saghiran aw kabiran ila ajalih.* (And let not the witnesses refuse when they are called. And do not be weary to write it, whether it is small or great, for its specified term). And it is necessary for the witnesses that when they are called, they should not refuse. And you, whether the matter is small or big, in writing it... do not be weary. Meaning, don't get bored. "I have to write so much." Don't say that, but the scribe in front should write. Meaning the guarantor... Meaning the guarantor, when he forgets his debt, the appointed one of the rank of the master, then he should not hand over his trust to him, then it is incumbent upon the witnesses to remind him. Yes. It is incumbent upon the witnesses to remind him that you had to give, you gave testimony in front of us at that time. And in this matter... And in this matter, do not be weary of teaching. of teaching. Meaning, whatever needs to be told, explain it properly to him. To the students, it's not like you just superficially explain it and finish. They should be satisfied, it should settle in their hearts completely. So... do not be weary of teaching. Small or big... Small or big... reprimand him. Reprimand him. Yes.

*Dhalikum aqsatu 'indallahi wa aqwamu lishshahadati wa adna alla tartabu.* (That is more just in the sight of Allah and more suitable for testimony, and more likely that you will not have doubt). This, in the sight of Allah, is a matter of greater justice. And for testimony, it is more strengthening. And it is closer to the fact that you will not fall into doubt. Doubt and suspicion. Doubt and suspicion. Meaning, Imam Alayhis Salam has justice. And for the people of the mission... the people of the mission... to clarify the position of the appointed one, it is a stronger method. Stronger method. And by this act, the possibility of doubt arising in the ranks... the possibility of doubt is greatly reduced. Meaning, the missionary should... or the elder should, who is doing this, he should clarify it completely. That this is my status, this is his status.

*Illa an takuna tijaratan hadiratan tudirunaha baynakum falaysa 'alaykum junahun alla taktubuhah.* (Except when it is a present trade which you carry out on the spot among yourselves, for then there is no blame upon you if you do not write it down). Except that... cash... cash transaction. a cash transaction... that you do amongst yourselves... then in such a situation, if you do not write, then there is no harm. Meaning, face to face with each other, teaching and learning is happening, then there is no need to explain it to the novices. Over here the questioner asked a question, who is in place of the buyer. And... and the teacher gave an answer, who is in place of the seller. That's it. The matter is over.

What is the number? 282 is to be read.

*Salli 'ala Muhammadin wa Ali Muhammad*. *Allahumma salli 'ala Muhammadin wa Ali Muhammad*. For the intercession of Sayyidna Imam Sayyidna Ja'far... *al-Fatihah*.

<!-- AUG:ibrahim-2015 lecture 10 -->

[unclear] This is the last one, isn't it? The question.

Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim (In the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful).

A little louder.

Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim (In the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful). Wa ashhadu (And bear witness)...

Wa ashhadu (And bear witness)... wa iza taba'ayatum wala yudarru katibun wala shahid. Wa in taf'alu fa innahu fusuqun bikum. Wattaqullah (And when you trade with one another, let there be no harm to the scribe or the witness. And if you do so, it is wickedness in you. And fear Allah). Wa yu'allimukumullah. Wallahu bikulli shay'in 'aleem. (And Allah teaches you. And Allah is All-Knowing of everything.)

And when you buy and sell, then make witnesses. A little louder. And you… a little louder. Speak a bit louder.

And you... and when you buy and sell, then make witnesses. And let no harm be done to the scribe or the witness. And if you do so, it will be disobedience. And fear Allah. And Allah teaches you. And Allah knows everything.

Meaning, when you have an intellectual discussion, then make those who are aware of it witnesses to the validity of the statement. And the `mufid` (teacher) should not harm his `mustafid` (student), and the `shahid` (witness, a higher rank) should not harm his subordinate. And if such an action is manifest, it will be against obedience (`ta'at`). In adhering to the laws of the *din*, keep fearing your Maulana Imam, because he knows the smallest of small things.

It's clear, the passage.

Wa in kuntum 'ala safarin wa lam tajidu katiban fa rihanun maqbuda. (And if you are on a journey and cannot find a scribe, then a pledge in hand [is sufficient].)

And if you are on a journey and cannot find anyone to write, then give something of yours as a pledge into [their] possession.

Meaning, if you are far from the land of `da'wat` and there is no such beneficial `mufid` in whose presence you can entrust the deposit (`wadi'at`) of `da'wat`, then do not give the `mustawda'` (depository) or `kafil` (guarantor) leeway in some matters. Then do not give the `mustawda'` or `kafil` leeway in some matters; make him `mahsus` (confined). Meaning, give the rightful owner his right. `Mahsus` means confined, a restriction is placed. Meaning, if there is such a matter, then the `amileen` (agents) who are `mahsus`, meaning they are where they are—Baroda, Bombay, Hyderabad. But the ones who are doing the work, they remain known or they remain at a high rank. What should be done with them? They should be made `mahsus`. They should be given… they should not be given full and complete authority. What is this? The passage above... Read the translation.

If you are on a journey... The condition of the journey is that during a journey, the `namaz` is [shortened] and should be performed later. If the journey is for one day and you are reaching before `zawal` (midday), then it is fine; if not, then it is counted as a journey.

And if you cannot find anyone to write, then give something of yours as a pawn (`rahn`). Place it in their possession. Meaning, if you are far from the land of `da'wat` and you do not find a beneficial companion in whose presence you can entrust the deposit (`wadi'at`), then do not give the `mustawda'` or `kafil` leeway in some matters. In some things, make them `mahsus`. And make him `mahsus` until he returns the right to its rightful owner.

Fa in amina ba'dukum ba'dan fal yu'addilladhi'tumina amanatahu walyattaqillaha rabbah. (But if one of you entrusts another, then let him who is entrusted discharge his trust, and let him fear Allah, his Lord.)

And if you have trust in one another, then he in whose possession the trust is placed should return it. And he should fear Allah, who is his Lord. Meaning, if the `mustawda'` is trusted, then there is no need to make him `mahsus`. If there is trust in the `mustawda'`, if there is trust (`e'temad`), the `mustawda'` has trust, so you don't make him `mahsus`. The rest, there is a need to make them `mahsus`. And they should return the trust (`amanat`) on time.

From the Imam who is their nourisher (`murabbi`), they should fear. Yes.

Wa la taktumush-shahadah. Wa man yaktumha fa innahu aathimun qalbuh. Wallahu bima ta'maluna 'aleem. (And do not conceal testimony. And whoever conceals it, his heart is indeed sinful. And Allah is All-Knowing of what you do.)

Now this is all about the matter of writing (`kitabat`), it is a matter of a transaction (`len-den`), but the inner matter, the `ta'wil` (esoteric interpretation), is related to our `mufid` and `mustafid`.

Wa la taktumush-shahadah wa la yaktumunna... fa innahu aathimun qalbuhu wallahu bima ta'maluna 'aleem. (And do not conceal testimony... for indeed his heart is sinful. And Allah is All-Knowing of what you do.)

Do not conceal the testimony. And whoever conceals it, his heart will be a sinner. And Allah is aware of your actions.

The meaning is clear.

Lillahi ma fis-samawati wa ma fil-ard. Wa in tubdu ma fi anfusikum aw tukhfuhu yuhasibkum bihillah. (To Allah belongs whatever is in the heavens and whatever is on the earth. And whether you disclose what is in your souls or conceal it, Allah will call you to account for it.)

Everyone knows this, that whatever is from the heavens to the earth, all dominion (`badshahat`) belongs to Allah. And whatever is in your souls... whether you conceal it or reveal it... whatever is in your souls, whether you conceal it or reveal it, and Allah will take its account from you. This is the common meaning, the translation. Now its `ta'wil` is this. Meaning...

The owner of all the knowledge of the `da'wat` of the `A'immah` (Imams) and `Hujjat` (Proofs) is only Ali. In whose gnosis (`ma'rifat`), the intellects of all are bewildered and wandering. The kind of creed (`aqidah`) that the leaders (`ra'is`) of misguidance (`zalalat`) hold, and the hypocritical (`munafiqana`) things they reveal, the account for all of that will surely be taken by Ali Ta'ala.

Fa yaghfiru liman yasha'u wa yu'adhdhibu man yasha'. Wallahu 'ala kulli shay'in qadir. (Then He forgives whom He wills and punishes whom He wills. And Allah is over all things competent.)

Meaning, Ali Ta'ala, whom He wills, according to their creed and action, He will also forgive, and He will also give punishment. It is all in His authority. Many times I have mentioned before that the `Qa'im al-Islam` who is to come, the seventh `Natiq` (Speaker-Prophet), he is the final hour in his own regard. But before him, the `juzwi qa'im` (partial Qa'im) is the person of Maulana Ali. After the time of the Prophet passed, and since then 1400 years have passed, he holds the status of the `juzwi qa'im`. This is a specific topic, `qasas` (stories, history), what `juzwi qa'im` is called, what its meaning is. It is quite complex.

He is the owner of power (`sahib-e-qudrat`).

Aamanar-rasulu bima unzila ilayhi min rabbih wal-mu'minun. Kullun aamana billahi wa mala'ikatihi wa kutubihi wa rusulih. La nufarriqu bayna ahadin min rusulih. (The Messenger has believed in what was revealed to him from his Lord, and [so have] the believers. All of them have believed in Allah and His angels and His books and His messengers, [saying], "We make no distinction between any of His messengers.")

The meaning is that those who revealed the revelation (`wahi`) upon the Messenger, in the `hadd-i ali` (lofty rank), to establish the `wasi` (successor), the command that was revealed, the best of creation (`behtarin-e-khalq`) accepted it and affirmed its truth. And in the same way, the `mu'minin` (believers) also affirmed the station of the `wasi` and believed with their hearts. And they all accepted the appointment (`intiqa`) from Allah Ta'ala and obeyed the `hujjat` (Proof). And those intermediaries (`wasa'it`) who are between the `sabiq` (Preceder) and the `rasul` (Messenger) and are called `mala'ikah` (angels), they were also accepted. And they affirm and declare all the previously revealed books and messengers. And they keep saying that we affirm the messengers, we do not make any distinction. We accept them with equal status.

Since we affirm the Prophet, we affirm them. The Prophet said that 124,000 prophets have passed. So we also believe that those prophets were truthful and trustworthy. Whatever they said is true. Now, the point here is about the `wahi` (revelation) being sent down. The `hadd-i ali` gave the command regarding the `wasi`. The command came that `balligh ma unzila ilayk` (proclaim what has been revealed to you). This was when the tenth Hajj occurred. The command came to him to `balligh ma unzila ilayk`. And then what was the threat? `wa illam taf'al fama ballaghta risalatah` (and if you do not, then you have not conveyed His message). If you do not do this work, then your message will be brought to a halt. `Wallahu ya'simuka minan-nas` (And Allah will protect you from the people). You have heard this in the morning sermons and in the evening as well. This `Wallahu ya'simuka minan-nas` was the last to come, when he was returning towards Ghadir Khumm. He had taken a diversion. After leaving Makkah, he did not go straight to Madinah, but went towards the `miqat` (appointed place), which was the `miqat` of Ghadir Khumm. So now, before this, the instruction (`ta'kid`) came to you several times, that you should do it. Now, in this, from the perspective of beliefs (`aqa'id`), there are many things: why the Prophet was hesitating, for what reason, from whom was he afraid, what fear did he have? But at that time too, it was that "I will see, Insha'Allah we will do it, Insha'Allah we will do it." This conversation was going on. Now, this hesitation that was occurring was not from the Prophet, but from his `hijab` (veil/rank below him), whoever it was. But when the final limit came... when did it come? When the tenth Hajj happened and you returned, `balligh ma unzila ilayk`, the verse came. Now in this `balligh`, there is both `jazm` (firmness) and `tashdid` (intensity). It is a place of great intensity, that you must do that thing which you have to do. Because you have conveyed all the rulings (`ahkamat`) of the shari'ah to the people—how to perform `namaz`, how to do `wudu`, all of it. But this important task (`muhim kaam`) remains pending. And the time of your departure... the surah `iza ja'a` had already come, and you had been informed that the time of my departure is near. So at that time, this `wahi` that came... now what do we say? That `wahi` is from Allah, revealed by Him. And Jibra'il Amin brought it. But where does this `wahi` emerge from? From your `hadd-i ali`. Those who reveal `wahi`, the `hadd-i ali` commanded to establish the `wasi`. Your `hadd-i ali`, who is he? If you study the higher levels, you will know who your `hadd-i ali` is. And then there is a medium, that it came through Jibra'il Amin. What kind of being is Jibra'il Amin? He is from the `mala'ikah` (angels). The `mala'ikah`, it is not known. So in whose form would he come? In which human form would he come? And from him, you would receive the `wahi`. So the coming of `wahi` is a special matter. And remember this `wahi`, that it comes only to the prophets. `Wahi` is a very high rank. And for those who are of a lesser rank, for them there is `ilham` (inspiration), there are `ta'idat` (supports), there are `khawab` (dreams), there is `ru'ya-i sadiqah` (true visions), and there are `tajalliyat` (manifestations), there is `ittihad` (union). There are many things: `tawfiq` (divine grace) happens, `fi'l` (action) happens. There are various things. If you read the books of Sayyidna Ja'far or Al-Sijistani, there are different discussions at every place. They write differently. So in what way? Like Sayyidna 'Ali [bin Muhammad al-Walid] sahib, in the books he has written, in the questions and answers he has written, in them, he writes everywhere that he is writing through the `wasilah` (medium) of... he writes `wasilah`. Whose? Sayyidna Sulayman [bin Hasan], Sayyidna Muhammad bin Tahir, and Sayyidna Ja'far. He writes this paragraph, always in the passage. This is a form of respect (`ehtiram`) and a method of `da'wat`, that he shows reverence for his `murabbi` (spiritual guide). And after that, in the second paragraph, he writes that whatever he is writing, he is writing it from the assembly (`majlis`) of Sayyidna Ja'far. And Sayyidna Ja'far, I have said many times before, Sayyidna Ja'far wrote 40 books, and Sayyidna 'Ali sahib wrote 377 books. Now, making this acknowledgement of the elder brother... because the rank (`rutbah`) of Sayyidna Ja'far was very great, very high. So the acknowledgement of this is that, in my view, Sayyidna Ja'far could not write that many books, but he had them written. Sayyidna 'Ali sahib wrote them. With whose support (`ta'id`) did he write them? So until there is `ta'id`, a person cannot write. So it is required of everyone that whatever work they do, they should understand that it is coming from their `murabbi` and we are delivering it. Now in this, the `wahi` is from your... the Imam of your time, it was coming from him. And in between, the mediums were Jibra'il Amin, and that is also a matter. Now, the `wahi` that comes, look at `wahi`: there are three ways for `wahi`. The `wahi` that comes, one is `wahi` in the form of `wahi`, as it came to the Prophet. And this is the greatest, highest thing, `wahi`. The second rank is... it is shown through someone, a vision (`mushahadah`) is made to happen. The third thing is `rasulan min` (a messenger from)... a messenger is sent, a `qasid` (courier) is sent. So there are three ways. And these three ways are, in our tradition, from the perspective of `haqa'iq` (realities)—perhaps you people may not understand it—the stations (`manzil`) of `jadd`, `fath`, and `khayal`. The highest `wahi` is in the `manzil` of `jadd`. The second is `fath`, and the third is `khayal`. So the `manzil` of `khayal`... `khayal` (thought/opinion) is your immediate boss, above you. "My `khayal` is this." Who are you? "No, my `khayal` is this." If you are sitting in a meeting, you are doing this, "My `khayal` is this." `Khayal` is, you consider yourself... that my `khayal` is the highest. Since you are sitting in the meeting. So `khayal` is a thing that is above you. Above `khayal` is `fath`, and above `fath` is `jadd`. And from another perspective, if you look, `fath` is in the middle, and `jadd` is the highest. The thing emerges from `jadd`. It emerges from above. It comes from `fath`. From `fath`, this is... Now, you know the ranks (`maratib`), if you know, you are aware, that Maulana 'Abd al-Muttalib, salawatullah 'alayh, Maulana Abu Talib, and Maulana 'Abdullah, these are the three stations. The `manzil` of `jadd` is, I mean, your grandfather (`dada`). `Jadd` is indeed the grandfather. Who? Sayyidna 'Abd al-Muttalib, Maulana 'Abd al-Muttalib, salawatullah 'alayh. So he is the highest rank. In this regard, what is it? The highest one, the impurity (`kasafat`) in them is less. In my view, `jadd` has two letters, `jim` `dal`. And `fath` has less than that: `fa`, `ta`, `ha`. Three come. And `khayal` is lower than that; it has four letters (`huroof`). So the letters which Sayyidna Hasan bin Nuh and Hasan bin Malik 'Abd al-Wahhab have extracted, and Sayyidna Hammam, many people have extracted, that from the numbers (`a'dad`) or from the alphabet (`abjad`), it is given strength. But it is not actually so, but its meanings are many. But for the sake of explanation, the `ta'wili` (esoteric) meaning is also this, that it is explained through numbers. Now here, what is it? "The owner of your souls..." Did you read this? Wa qalu sami'na wa ata'na ghufranaka rabbana wa ilaykal-masir. (And they say, "We hear and we obey. [We seek] Your forgiveness, our Lord, and to You is the [final] destination.") The meaning is that the aforementioned affirmers, while making `du'a` (supplication), say: We heard and we obeyed. And the wrong notion that arose in our hearts, that perhaps the `wasi` (successor) is not the rightful one, for that we seek forgiveness. We know that the return is towards you. La yukallifullahu nafsan... Everyone should know this, that whenever it is, to whom is it? `Inna lillahi wa inna ilayhi raji'un` (Indeed, we belong to Allah, and indeed to Him we will return). Because everyone is returning to Allah. And what is Allah? Meaning, the belief of the `mu'minin` is that everyone will return towards Ali. Be it anyone. Even up to the `A'immah`, they follow in his footsteps. Their return will also go there, and they will reach Ali. La yukallifullahu nafsan illa wus'aha. Laha ma kasabat wa 'alayha maktasabat. (Allah does not charge a soul except [with that within] its capacity. It will have [the consequence of] what [good] it has gained, and it will bear [the consequence of] what [evil] it has earned.) Meaning, the `wasi` does not place a burden on any servant of the `da'wat` for establishing the `da'wat` that is beyond his capacity. He doesn't place it. Whatever his capacity (`zarf`) is, he gives that much. He doesn't give more than that. Everyone has their capacity, and their limit. For the inviter (`da'i`), there is a reward for it. And for the opponent, there is punishment for it. Yes. Rabbana la tu'akhidhna in nasina aw akhta'na. Rabbana wa la tahmil 'alayna isran kama hamaltahu 'alalladhina min qablina. (Our Lord, do not impose blame upon us if we have forgotten or erred. Our Lord, and lay not upon us a burden like that which You laid upon those before us.) Meaning... O... meaning, they implore their Lord, the Wasi 'alayhis-salam, that in inviting towards you, the shortcoming (`kotahi`) that occurred from us, and in explaining your station, the mistake (`bhool`) that occurred, and the inattention (`be-tawajjohi`) that was shown, do not seize us for that. Yes. O our Lord, the sin we committed from deficiency in the `da'wat`, do not place its burden upon us as it was placed on those before us. Let us not commit excess (`ghulu`) nor deficiency (`qulu`). Give them their due right properly. Because with Amir al-Mu'minin is that collection of subtleties (`lata'if`) of the predecessors (`sabiq`) who had guided the rebellious ones. They gathered in your presence and became one with you. This... its source is not here. This is a `ta'wil` book, but the realities are being told, which collections (`majami'`) gathered with you. Which ones gathered with the Prophet, and which ones with Janab-e-Ali. Thousands of years' worth gathered. With Janab-e-Prophet, 9,000 years' worth of collections gathered. Collections. And which ones? The initial (`ibteda'i`), the `mustawda'` forms. And for Janab-e-Ali, salawatullah 'alayh, it is said that 10,000 years' worth came. And you yourself say that after every 10,000 years, a great form will manifest in the world, and that is me. Now how will you count 10,000? The `dawr-i satr` (period of concealment) is 7,000 years, and `fatrat` (interim period) is 3,000 years. This is also from that perspective. There are other matters as well, of how your 10,000 years are formed. If the entire period is combined, when the `dawr-i satr` ends... at 47,000, the `dawr-i kashf` (period of manifestation) ends, and after that the period of `fatrat` begins. The period of `kasraniyat` begins. So these are matters that, if you understand a little, by the blessings of Ramadan Sharif, we should understand that the `dawr` is not uniform. Time is always changing, and there is both good and bad. This is how the world is running. It has been running for ages. So today, where we are sitting, is the `dawr-i satr`. `Satr` means hidden. The Imam who is the Master of the Time (`Malik-e-Waqt`) is concealed. And in his place, the `du'at` and `ma'zuneen` work. The Prophet worked in his time. Today, those who do the same work are called `du'at`. So the `dawr-i kashf` is of 47,000 years... 50,000. It is said to be 50,000, but its last 3,000 years become `fatrat`. `Fa`, `ta`. `Fa`, `ta`, not `te`. `Fatrat` means a period of pause, of silence. And in it, there is both good and evil. In what way? As when the `kashf`... when the day is about to end, before `maghrib`, there is darkness as well as light. Both. So at that time, it means that 3,000 such years pass, and in those 3,000 years, people were also turning away from the Imams and also going to the monks (`rahib`). They were going to the astrologers (`nujumi`). They were going to those who tell fortunes. So both things are the requirement of the `dawr-i fatrat`. And as this was happening—one group going this way, one group going that way; those who see goodness and those who run after `amileen` (sorcerers)—while this was happening, suddenly `satr` comes. And the birth of Janab-e-Adam 'alayhis-salam happens. The `dawr-i satr`, meaning as soon as `maghrib` time ended, the night came. And the time of night is called `satr`. So `kashf` is the time of day. So `kashf` and `satr` are just like we see night and day. Night is `satr`, day is `kashf`. And in between, there is a twilight (`jhutputa`). So now it's time for `maghrib`. "Let's go, it's getting dark, it's getting dark." That is the `dawr-i fatrat`. This, night and day, just as you cannot change it... this is a law (`nizam`) of Allah. `Lan tajida li-sunnatillahi tabdila` (You will never find in the way of Allah any change). It is the `sunnah` (way) of Allah which will never be changed. And it will continue to run. So the `sunnah` is Allah's `sunnah`. It is not the Prophet's or anyone else's, that it should be changed. Rather, this is the `sunnah` of Allah. `Wa lan tajida li-sunnatillahi tahwila` (And you will never find in the way of Allah any alteration). And this is Allah's `sunnah` in which there can be no alteration. So it's not this either, but with night and day, just as we cannot change it, so too we cannot change `kashf` and `satr`. After `kashf`, `satr` must come. And after `satr`, `kashf` must come. It will keep running. How many eons and ages have passed, but it is the same. And then the days change, moments change into days, days change into years, centuries pass. After centuries, a `dawr` comes. Several `dawr`s make up a `qawr`. Several `qawr`s make up a `mawr`. So this world is very old, it is continuing, and in it the periods of `dawr` and `qawr`, the durations that have been told by the `awliya Allah`, will continue to run.

So the mistake that happened from us, you forgive that. Meaning, they implore their Lord, the Wasi 'alayhis-salam, that in inviting towards you, the shortcoming (`kotahi`) that occurred from us, and in explaining your station, the mistake (`bhool`) that occurred, and the inattention (`be-tawajjohi`) that was shown, do not seize us for that. O Allah, do not hold us accountable for the mistake we have made. Rather, you show your mercy and... By whom is this happening? In the general Qur'an Sharif, it is from Allah, but the `ta'wil` is what? Janab-e-Ali, salawatullah 'alayh. Now, for the sake of Ali, why? This Sayyidna Husayn [bin 'Ali] has written, that wherever the word "Allah" appears, if you see the first `para` (part), what is the explanation of `Alhamdulillahi Rabbil 'Alamin`? What is the explanation of `Bismillah`? What is `Ar-Rahman Ar-Rahim` called? All of this... I have taught this lesson, but I am saying that there are different explanations. Now, from this perspective, it is seen that this Surah al-Hamd is very special, because in it, all things have been told, from whom the matter has come, from where it has emerged. Why? Because you recite Yasin Sharif daily, you recite it every week, you recite it on Fridays. And what is in it? `Yasin`. Yasin is one of the names of the Prophet. Just as I said, the collections of 9,000 years are Janab-e-Prophet. And that is why what happened? The Surah Taha was revealed. The `Ta` of Taha is the ninth degree. It has nine letters. That is why the Prophet is the possessor of 9,000 years of collections. And you had completed nine ranks. But after that, a person does not sit in silence, but keeps striving. When the Prophet reached the tenth station, at that time the chapter of Yasin Sharif was revealed. The `Ya` of Yasin indicates that he reached the tenth degree. So in this way, there is a difference between the two. And Maulana Ali, salawatullah 'alayh, is the possessor of 10,000 years of collections. 10,000 years. And which ones? In their `istiqrari` (established) forms. The forms, the collections, which are not in the Prophet's share, but in... But we cannot lower the rank of the Prophet. If you make them equal, even by a hair's breadth, then you deviate from monotheism (`tawhid`). So one has to be very careful in explaining this, what the rank of the Prophet is. If the Prophet's rank was not clear, Ali would not have been `mamdud` (extended). So here in this world, Ali came in the form of `hijabat` (veils), and that too, your `ayn-i 'azima` (great essence) is `muhtajib` (veiled). His final `hijab` came. The lowest (`adna`) `hijab` came. And the Prophet's was the middle (`darmiyani`) `hijab`. If the middle `hijab` had not come and the final `hijab` had come, then Ali would never have been `mamdud`. It has been explained. [unclear] You have heard it from Baba Hazrat. That the final `hijab` in which... the `hijab` of Janab-e-Ali came. This is a track, a path, that Allah Ta'ala does this within `satr`. Wasi. Everywhere it is wasi, wasi. You see, the era of Musa, Harun's name does not come up as often. Yusha's doesn't. Habil's doesn't. When Adam is mentioned, Adam, Nuh, Ibrahim, Musa, 'Isa. What is the status of these people? Why are their names famous in the world? The era of Ibrahim, the era of Musa, the era of 'Isa. But the `mustaqarr` (permanent) Imam of their time, that Imam is very high. He is above them. By his command, these people were made. Who made Nuh 'alayhis-salam? Who made Nuh 'alayhis-salam? Janab-e-Hud. Hud was the Imam of that time. And Adam 'alayhis-salam was made by Janab-e-Hunayn. And the Imam of Ibrahim 'alayhis-salam's time was Maulana Salih. Maulana Salih. And for Janab-e-Musa, it is Janab-e-'Ud. `Alif`, `dal`. And for Janab-e-'Isa, it is Maulana Khuzaymah. These five who passed, these five `nutuqa` (Speaker-Prophets) who are very high... they are very high. These five whose names we take, Adam, Nuh, Ibrahim, Musa, 'Isa, how many are they, including the Prophet? Six. These six are `nutuqa`. Not `Ulu 'l-'Azm` (Possessors of Firm Resolve) prophets. `Ulu 'l-'Azm` prophets start from Nuh. So Adam was removed from the `Ulu 'l-'Azm` prophets. It is said about them in the `du'as` you write in books, that they did not have `Ulu 'l-'Azm`. It started from Nuh. So Nuh is the first among the `Ulu 'l-'Azm` prophets. And after that, of a higher rank than them is Janab-e-Ibrahim. Then Musa, then 'Isa, then Janab-e-Prophet. These five, the prayer we are offering, the five daily prayers we are offering, are incumbent upon these five. There are many subtleties, but remember for now that the five `nutuqa` or the five `Ulu 'l-'Azm` prophets, our prayers are incumbent upon them. Now, in this, the special point is that the prayer is `salatan mawquta` (a prayer at a prescribed time). The prayer is a prayer to be offered at its time. If you start praying the `zohr` prayer at 10:30, and people are watching, what is he praying? There is no prayer at 10:30. And if you pray without its time, then it has no significance. So the significance of time is great. The significance of time is there. So the times of prayer, just as we are careful about the time, that `zohr` has happened or not, or the sun has set or not, as we are careful, the times of prayer are greater than the prayer itself. Why are they greater? I have said this many times. The times of prayer are greater than the prayer because the prayer is from the side of the servant, and the times are sent by Allah. From above, the openings that open, the windows that open in the heavens, from there blessings and mercy descend. So that is from Allah's side, so the requirement of its time is greater. And incumbent upon the time are their followers (`motabe'een`), who are the concealed Imam, who is the Prophet. Nuh 'alayhis-salam is a prophet. So Janab-e-Hud sent the Prophet... sent Nuh. Wherever you see, the command came, "O Nuh, build an ark." From whose side did it come? From the side of your `muhtajib` (veiled one), the Imam of that time. And the Imam, who was he? The seventh Imam. And a very high Imam. So Janab-e-Hud, from his side there were signs. Then in Janab-e-Ibrahim's time, the matter that happened, Salih was your father, and it was a different matter there, in Janab-e-Ibrahim's case. Ibrahim is in the lineage of the prophets' forefathers, and he is the son of Janab-e-Salih. That is why Janab-e-Ibrahim's rank is what? It is not just that he was a prophet, or a messenger, or a `natiq`, or an `Ulu 'l-'Azm` prophet. He was a prophet, he became a messenger, after messenger he became a `natiq`, after `natiq` an `Ulu 'l-'Azm` prophet, and after that he attained the rank of `khullat` (friendship). `Ibrahim khalilullah` (Ibrahim, the friend of Allah). And `Ibrahim alladhi waffa` (And Ibrahim who fulfilled). Ibrahim is the one who completely fulfilled his loyalty. And after that, what happened? He attained such a rank that you, it's as if from a narrow-gauge, you went to a broad-gauge. So what does that mean? That you, from the rank of prophethood (`nubuwwah`), moved to the rank of Imamate (`imamah`). `inna ja'iluka linnasi imama` (Indeed, I will make you an Imam for the people). We have made Ibrahim an Imam. So Janab-e-Ibrahim became an Imam, and after that, he kept progressing, the ranks of Imamate, in which there are the higher ranks, after Imamate there is `imamat-i ruhani`, `nafs-i ilahi`, and after that he progresses. So which rank did you attain? This is what needs to be recognized. The `hudud` (ranks), the `hadd-i ali` and `hadd-i adna`, whoever they may be, recognizing their station is our `tawhid` (monotheism). That is why what do we do? We say, `Allahumma thabbitna 'ala ta'atillah wa ta'at-i rasulihi wa ta'at-i imam-uz-zamanih wa hudud-id-dinih` (O Allah, keep us firm in the obedience of Allah, and the obedience of His Messenger, and the obedience of the Imam of the time, and the `hudud` of the *din*). O Allah, keep us steadfast in what? In the obedience of Allah, in the obedience of the Messenger, in the obedience of the Imam of the time. This is all completely equal.
