That is, the reality of the *maratib* is known only by the *Imam al-Zaman* and his *hudud*, the *hazrat*. The *hudud* of the *hazrat*. Not even of the *jaza'ir*. The *hudud* of the *hazrat* know, because they are *layli* (of the night/spiritual) and they are greater than the *nahari* (of the day/physical). They say, "We affirm the *hikmat* (wisdom), and also the fact that its source is the *Imam-e-mustaqarr*. Every word of *hikmat* comes from there. Only the people of intellect know and understand this."

This is... only select people understand this. In the circle of *iman* (faith), there are very few people. "*wa qalilun min 'ibadiya al-shakur* (And few of My servants are grateful)." But in terms of just following along, there are many. In Islam, there are crores of people, but how many came into *iman*? The Shia people came, who have love for Ali, who have malice towards the opponents of Sayyidna Ali. How far do they believe? They believe up to the 7th Imam. We believe up to here, up to the 48th Imam. You cannot leave a single one. Otherwise, your *tawhid* (belief in one God/Imam) becomes flawed. So in between, many such things happened, many historical events, where the Nizaris separated from us, the Shias separated, the Sunnis had already separated. And 400 years ago, a great personality came into existence in the world, and that was Sayyidna Sulayman... At that time, as described in *Burhan-e-Sulaymani* by Sayyidna Shakir Saheb, what happened? When the first trumpet (*sur*) is blown... on the day of Qiyamah, two trumpets will be blown. There will be a great sound twice. In the first sound, those who are disobedient will fall unconscious. And those who are righteous, the ones to be saved, will move forward. And after that, in the final era, in the time of the *Qa'im al-Salam*, the second trumpet will be blown. Then they will rise. "*wa al-sa'atu haqq, wa an al-jannata haqq, wa al-nara haqq, wa al-ba'tha haqq* (And the Hour is true, and Paradise is true, and the Fire is true, and the Resurrection is true)." There will be their resurrection. Sayyidna Shakir Saheb writes that he is writing after benefiting from the treatises of Sayyidna Sulayman Saheb. The first trumpet (*sur-e-awwal*)... "we used to think it would happen in the time of Mahdi al-Salam, the *sur-e-awwal*, and then an era would pass and the *Qa'im* would come." We used to think that. But you read the book, and you will know. Sayyidna Shakir Saheb writes that Sayyidna Sulayman writes, "What heedlessness are you in? The *sur-e-awwal* is me. My appearance is the *sur-e-awwal*." And at that time, the people who are unconscious are these, the people around. These people are in a state of unconsciousness. They don't know what the truth is. They are astray. And when they find out, it will be '*ya laytani kuntu turaba* (Oh, I wish I were dust).' 'I wish I had become a believer.'" As it is in Surah al-Haqqah, "*ya laytani kuntu turaba*." I would have become dust... that is, I wish I had become a believer. I didn't have the desire for high ranks." When? When Qiyamah comes, when the time of punishment comes. When the time of punishment comes, at that time. So the point is that in this world, it is the abode of action, not the abode of reward. Now, why has this period of respite been given? Allah knows. Because there are *qisas* (retributions). What things are there? There is the *zahiri*. We are suffering the *qisas* for some of their actions. Some things... there are such clashes that many things... the momineen have some shortcomings, so they are suffering. Great people have passed. You know, in the time of Syedna Dawood, what kind of people they were, who became like that. They believed, yet they changed. Abdullah al-Mahdi. A very great personality in Yemen. And they were so numerous that they were counted among the most, but they were far away, there in Yemen. Here were Dawood Qutb Shah and his companions, many of them, like Sheikh Adam and others. But when Syedna Dawood's time came... and you read the *sirat* to know... at the very moment the announcement was made, he thought, "The *da'wat* is in Dawood Qutb Shah's control. He has the treasury, he has this, he has that. All the things are in his possession: accounts, money, property, everything." So he turned away, towards Dawood. Abdullah al-Mahdi turned away. Sayyidna Shakir Saheb writes that Fir'awn is separate, Haman is separate, Shaddad is separate. And who are they? He has written it all. That your uncle, who was Isma'il, he was their effigy (*putla*). Of Dawood. That Sayyidna Yusuf, who was the grandfather of Sayyidna Sulayman Saheb, after his father's demise, his uncle said, "The entire property should come to me. I am the brother." And all the things were to go to Sayyidna Sulayman Saheb. So this was the malice. So Sayyidna... Sulayman Saheb's uncle, Isma'il, was a greedy man, he was of the Fir'awnic nature. One had greed, one had envy (*hasad*). His name is also given. And one had arrogance (*takabbur*). The arrogant one is this, Abdullah al-Mahdi. You read the book, *Ziya' al-Iman* by Syedna Sulayman. Every child should know the *sirat* of Sayyidna Sulayman: what calamities befell him, what happened at what time, how the *hujjat* was established, where he was imprisoned, what all happened. All this should be known. Otherwise, what do we know? We know our own *sirat*. We don't know the *sirat* of Sayyidna Muhammad.

So this is the meaning. That is, the reality of the *maratib* is known by the Imam of the time and the *hudud*, the *hazrat*. They affirm the *hikmat* and the fact that its source is the *Imam-e-mustaqarr*. Every word of *hikmat* comes from there. Only the people of intellect know it. People of intellect, meaning those who are rightly guided and who are firm on the *din-e-hanif* (the true religion), they know. Read one more *ayat*.

*Rabbana la tuzigh qulubana ba'da idh hadaytana wa hab lana min ladunka rahmatan innaka anta al-wahhab. Rabbana innaka jami'u al-nasi li-yawmin la rayba fihi inna Allaha la yukhlifu al-mi'ad* (Our Lord, let not our hearts deviate after You have guided us and grant us from Yourself mercy. Indeed, You are the Bestower. Our Lord, surely You will gather the people for a Day about which there is no doubt. Indeed, Allah does not fail in His promise).

That is, the *rasikhun fi al-'ilm*... The translation is not given. The translation... The translation is... the translation of the Quran, you can look it up, it's easy. It's understandable. "That is..." you read from there. "That is..." he has written. Yes, starting from "that is." That is, the *rasikhun fi al-'ilm* (those firm in knowledge), being aware of the *ma'rifat* of the Quran, make this *du'a* (supplication): Ya Allah... *Rabbana*... *Rabbana*, "O our Lord." But who is the Lord? Here it is the Imam of the time. What do they say? They make this *du'a*: Ya Mawlana Imam al-Zaman, after we have received guidance, do not let us deviate from your *da'wat*. And grant us your *ta'yid* (divine support) and mercy. You are the Forgiver. Ya Mawlana Imam, you are the one who gathers these *suwarat* (spiritual forms), which are prepared by your *da'wat*, for the *haykal* (spiritual form/body) of the Imam coming after you. There is no doubt in this. And you have promised that every person will be given a place in the *haykal-e-imami* according to their worthiness. Certainly, you do not go against it.

Look, the whole *ma'rifat* is laid bare. The Imam of the time, the things that are gathered with you, for whom are they? For your son, for the coming Imam. So in a way, "*ihdina al-sirat al-mustaqim*" (Guide us to the straight path) which we read, the *sirat* is the coming *hujjat*, who is your son. We believe in you, of course. We also believe in your son, because it is *wahidan ba'da wahidin*, "*qullun 'an ba'd*" (each from the other). This is its confirmation. Your successor is this. That's it. And in the *haykal-e-imami*, what place will one get? That is his paradise (*jannat*). According to their worthiness, they will be given a place in the *haykal-e-imami*. That is, there will be no violation of it. Meaning, Allah, whatever the thing is, is just. He is 'Adil (Just), not Zalim (Unjust). Allah does not possess injustice (*zulm*). Someone was asked, "Allah has power over everything. Is there something Allah doesn't have? A thing you cannot answer?" The first person couldn't answer, the second couldn't. They came to Ali. "What is it with Allah that He doesn't have?" "Injustice (*zulm*)." That is the one thing Allah doesn't have. What is the wisdom in this? He is just. Allah is 'Adil. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim...







O Allah, grant shifa to your servants from illnesses and afflictions. O Allah, do not make our hearts deviate after You have guided us, and grant us mercy from Yourself, for indeed You are the Bestower... Amen... Maulana Mominin, Salawat and Salam be upon him. Al-Fatihah.

[Recitation of Al-Fatihah]

<!-- AUG:ibrahim-2015 lecture 13 -->

This was finished before. There is a question, you must ask it. Oh brother... from 1350? ...in Islamabad... These have been done... This one above, the... *'ikhrat al-juma'*... *Al-rasikhuna fi'l-'ilm* (The well-grounded in knowledge), the first line. This has been done, right? ... Move it forward a little... reduce it... reduce... yes.

*Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim* (In the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful).

`Rabbana la tuzigh qulubana ba'da idh hadaytana wa hab lana min ladunka rahmatan innaka anta l-Wahhab` (Our Lord, let not our hearts deviate after You have guided us, and grant us from Yourself mercy. Indeed, You are the Bestower). `Rabbana innaka jami'u n-nas li-yawmin la rayba fih, innallaha la yukhlifu l-mi'ad` (Our Lord, surely You will gather the people for a Day about which there is no doubt. Indeed, Allah does not fail in His promise).

Meaning, the *rasikhun fi'l-'ilm* (those well-grounded in knowledge), being aware of the interpretation of the Quran, make this prayer: "O Maulana Imam al-Zaman, after receiving guidance, do not let us deviate from your *da'wah* (call). And grant us your *ta'id* (support). You are the Forgiver."

"O Maulana Imam, you are the one who will gather these *suwarat* (spiritual forms) that are ready from your *da'wah* for the *haykal* (spiritual structure) of the Imam who will come after."

There is no doubt in this, and you have promised that every person will be granted a place in the *haykal-i Imami* (Imam's spiritual structure) according to his *istiqhaq* (merit/deservingness). Indeed, you will not go against it.

This is a continuous explanation from *Rawdat al-Mustafid*, which Sayyidna Husayn Zu'l-Jaddayn wrote. And the subsequent chapters, two or three, were written by Sayyidna Ibrahim (`qaddasa-Llahu ruhahu`). So this, we have *Ta'wil al-Qur'an* (Interpretation of the Quran), which is a book written by Sayyidna Husayn Zu'l-Jaddayn. Its translation was done about 10, 15, or 20 years ago by the order of Hazrat Sayyid sahib, by Mulla Amin sahib. Otherwise, it was written in Arabic 250 years ago.

So here, the continuous explanation is that *al-rasikhuna fi'l-'ilm* are those whom we feel and understand to be the Imam al-Zaman, the Imams of every era; they were *rasikhuna fi'l-'ilm*. A verse will come later: `wa ma ya'lamu ta'wilahu illa-Llah, wa r-rasikhuna fi'l-'ilm` (and no one knows its interpretation except Allah and those well-grounded in knowledge). And its, the Quran's knowledge, is not only known by Allah, but also by the *rasikhun fi'l-'ilm*, who are... increase it a little... who are steadfast in knowledge, embedded in it. They are its deserving ones.

And after that, it is that, "O Imam al-Zaman, we implore that after you have granted us guidance, after we have received guidance from you, then do not let us deviate." This should be the prayer of every believer. We are on the path of *iman*, but we don't know when we might turn back. The world is a place of deception. For this reason, `la tuzigh qulubana ba'da idh hadaytana` (let not our hearts deviate after You have guided us). That is, after we have received guidance, Allah should not turn our hearts. Wrong thoughts should not enter us. A wrong conception about the Imam al-Zaman, about the Prophet... what we hear in public *majalis* (gatherings), there are flaws and defects in them. There are many things that are difficult to understand. For example, the night of Mi'raj, the night of Hijrat. Wherever the name of Maulana 'Ali is mentioned, there, deviation (*inhiraf*) has occurred.

In this regard, see that... do not let us deviate and grant us your support. You are the Forgiver. "O Imam al-Zaman, you are the one who gathers these *suwarat* which are ready from the *da'wah* and includes them in the *haykal* of the Imam who will come after." This is the principle of the Imam al-Zaman: that one Imam invites towards Allah, and in that, *suwarat* from different *jaza'ir* (islands/regions) and other places are gathered. After gathering them, in his final moments, he entrusts that *haykal* to his son. Until that time, those *suwarat* are with the *Bab al-Abwab*; it is called a *majma'* (congregation). And when the *Bab al-Abwab* hands it over, saying, "This was your trust, I am the final limit (*hadd*), and after me is the Imam al-Zaman, I am giving it to you." The moment he gives it, at that very moment, the *Bab al-Abwab* passes away. Because in the *haykal-i Imami*, in the *nasut-i Imami*, the place of the heart is the place of the *Bab al-Abwab*. They give that place, and he passes away. After he passes away, the Imam al-Zaman of our time acquires it for his son. This is a *tahrik* (movement/impulse) from the Imam al-Zaman, and in the 'Alam al-Nur (World of Light), all these movements happen. That is, after this one Imam, the next Imam has to come. So, in that way, when the second Imam comes... And regarding this, I mentioned yesterday the prayer `Ihdina s-sirat al-mustaqim` (Guide us to the straight path). We are already on the straight path, but then we pray, `Ihdina s-sirat al-mustaqim`. "O Allah, keep us on the straight path." The straight path means that after this Imam, the second Imam who will come, we will accept him as well.

In this way, many people have gone astray. After Maulana Ja'far al-Sadiq, they were supposed to accept Janab Isma'il, but they did not. They accepted Musa Kazim. The explanation for this has already been given. It has been explained in the morning lecture and also in the evening lecture, how and where the turns have come, and wherever a great Imam appears, there a duality (*du'i*) is created, and deviation (*inhiraf*) occurs. These are their own shortcomings and the corruption of their beliefs, that when they go somewhere, their view becomes something else. It happened at the time of Maulana Tayyib, and it happened at the time of Musa Kazim. So these are the kinds of things. So in the *haykal-i Imami*, wherever our merit (*istiqhaq*) lies. We might be nothing, just simple believers. We might be in the Imam's noble *haykal* or noble *nasut* at the level of a hair or a nail. So for this reason, we want our entry to take place. We consider ourselves to be in the Imam's *haykal*, but it is possible that we are not. Believers have many shortcomings. If they don't ask for forgiveness, don't repent, then the matter is finished. So there are many such things. Nails and hair, look, what are they? They are shed. Hairs grow and are shed. Nails grow and are shed. So that is why when we recite Surat al-Jumu'ah, the explanation for Friday is that it is in this way: `Huwa lladhi ba'atha fi l-ummiyyina rasulan minhum yatlu 'alayhim ayatihi wa yuzakkihim wa yu'allimuhumu l-kitaba wa l-hikmata wa in kanu min qablu lafi dalalin mubin` (He is the One who sent among the unlettered a Messenger from themselves, reciting to them His verses and purifying them and teaching them the Book and wisdom, although they were before in clear error). This verse which has come, that the Prophet was born in Makkah, and in a place where it was all desert. Before that, a plant had never grown there. And in that era, the *jahiliyyah* (ignorance) of Arabia, in that, the Prophet was born. `Huwa lladhi ba'atha fi l-ummiyyina` (`Huwa lladhi` meaning, He is the same Lord, `subhana-Lladhi`, who, Allah, the Great and Exalted, is the one who sent a messenger in the land of Makkah). And what did he do? `yatlu 'alayhim ayatihi wa yuzakkihim wa yu'allimuhumu l-kitaba wa l-hikmata wa in kanu` (reciting to them His verses and purifying them and teaching them the Book and wisdom, although they were). Now, along with that, there is the explanation of Surat al-Jumu'ah, that `Idha nudiya li-s-salati min yawmi l-jumu'ah` (When the call is proclaimed for the prayer on the day of Jumu'ah). I have explained this many times on Fridays. That when `Idha nudiya li-s-salati min yawmi l-jumu'ah, fa-s'aw...` what is it? `fa-s'aw ila dhikri-Llah wa dharu l-bay'` (then proceed to the remembrance of Allah and leave the trade). You should immediately get ready. On Fridays, people keep their shops open, they are doing business. That should not happen. Rather, `fa-s'aw ila dhikri-Llah wa dharu l-bay'`. Close the buying and selling. And what should you do? At that time, you should be engaged in prayer. So, `nudiya li-s-salati min yawmi l-jumu'ah`. `fi yawmi l-jumu'ah` has not come. `min yawmi l-jumu'ah` has come. `fi yawmi l-jumu'ah` would mean 'in the day of Jumu'ah'. Allah says that in the day of Jumu'ah, if you are... that didn't happen, rather `min yawmi l-jumu'ah` came. `min yawmi l-jumu'ah` means 'from'. 'From'. This means that Jumu'ah is attributed to the Prophet. You are being called into the *shari'ah* of the Prophet, and you are deviating. `min yawmi l-jumu'ah`. Then the explanation of Jumu'ah is separate, what is *Jim*, what is *Mim*, what is *'Ayn*, what is *ha'*. These are all things. So the point is that based on one's *istiqhaq* (merit), everyone will get a place there. What is *istiqhaq*? It is what we have done in this world. As you sow, so shall you reap. Whatever one does here, one receives it there. Meaning, after death, in the court of the hereafter, in the field of the hereafter, you will get what you have done here. You sow here, and the crop rises there. Remember that as we do, so shall we be rewarded.

"Here, Imam al-Zaman, the *suwarat* that are ready from your *da'wah*, you entrust them to the Imam who comes after you." There is no doubt in this, and you have promised that every person will be granted a place in the *haykal-i Imami* according to his merit. Indeed, this will not be violated. This is the system of Allah, that every Imam entrusts it to the Imam who comes after him.

Meaning, as Hazrat Shakir sahib used to say, that a *da'wah* is like a fisherman who has caught fish from the whole river. For whom did he catch them? For his children. He catches them and gives them to his family. Here too, from various nets, fish are being caught, meaning *suwarat* and *rihiyah*. All are gathered, and from that, both *nasut* and *lahut* are prepared. *Nasut* is a different matter, *lahut* is a different matter, and when there are lessons on *da'wah* terminology, it becomes clear that *nasut* is the physical form and *lahut* is the abstract form, it is not visible.

`Inna lladhina kafaru lan tughniya 'anhum amwaluhum wa la awladuhum mina-Llahi shay'a, wa ula'ika hum waqudu n-nar` (Indeed, those who disbelieve - never will their wealth or their children avail them against Allah at all. And it is they who are the fuel of the Fire).

The *kuffar* (disbelievers), their wealth and their children will never be able to save them from Allah at all. They are all the fuel of Hell. Meaning, for the *munkirin-i wasi'* (deniers of the successor), the apparent knowledge of *shari'ah* that they acquired from the *natiq* (`alayhi s-salam`) will not be beneficial for them. And their followers will also be of no use to them. They will not be saved from the anger of the *wasi'*. These people are the fuel for the *da'wah* of the *addad* (opponents), who keep igniting the fire of strife.

Look, here the word *kafaru* has been mentioned. *Mushrikin* (polytheists) are different, and *kafirin* (disbelievers) are different. *Mushrikin* are those who, when the Prophet (`salawatuLlahi 'alayh`) spread Islam, at that time in Makkah, they were all *mushrik*. They were idol-worshippers. They were... adulterers, alcoholics, all sorts. And the Prophet brought them into the fold of Islam and acquainted them with Islam. This, the battles the Prophet fought, whether it was Uhud, Badr, Khandaq, or Khaybar, all of them were against the *mushrikin* of Arabia. But what is mentioned here? `alladhina kafaru` (those who disbelieved). Those people who, after bringing faith, turned back. *Kufr* means to turn back after bringing faith. And who are those people? They are the ones who did not accept the *wasi'* (successor) of the Prophet. They accept the Prophet, and they profess it very loudly, but after that, they do not accept the *wasi'* of the Prophet. So not accepting the *wasi'* of the Prophet is like not accepting the Prophet himself. Because the Prophet gave the command. You accept the great one, but you don't accept the command of the great one. Then the matter is over, you are not accepting the great one. If you do not obey the command of the great one, then it is of no use. Understood? This is the method of *da'wah*.

`Ka-da'bi ali Fir'awna wa lladhina min qablihim, kadhdhabu bi-ayatina fa-akhadhahumu-Llahu bi-dhunubihim, wa-Llahu shadidu l-'iqab` (Like the custom of the people of Pharaoh and of those before them. They denied Our signs, so Allah seized them for their sins. And Allah is severe in penalty).

Just as what happened to the people of Pharaoh and those before them. They denied our signs. Allah seized them because of their sins, and Allah is severe in punishment. Here, Pharaoh. Pharaoh was the great one of his region, and his name was Walid, Walid bin Mus'ab. That was Walid. But when Janab Musa's (`alayhi s-salam`) rank increased, he should have accepted him. He did not accept. And what did he say? `Ana rabbukumu l-a'la` (I am your lord, the most high). I am the lord of all. He became arrogant, he transgressed. `Fir'awn-a tagha` (Pharaoh transgressed). This is recited in Surat al-Jumu'ah, that how he transgressed, and how he rebelled, and he had to face his punishment. So whether it is Pharaoh or Namrud, Qabil or Shaddad, or after them, Abu Jahl or Abu Lahab, all who are the *zidd* (opposite/enemy) of the *awliya' Allah* (friends of God), what will they be? They are *kuffar*, and they will go to Hell. Because they accept the Prophet but not the *wasi'*. They accept Musa but not Harun. The matter is finished. Here, they accept the Prophet but not 'Ali. And who is 'Ali? Just as the Prophet is *Ashraf al-Anbiya'* (the most noble of prophets), in the same way, 'Ali is *Ashraf al-Awsiya'* (the most noble of successors). He is the collection of them all. If you go into the *haqa'iq* (realities), you will find that whether it's Harun or Yusha', or Sahib or Ibn al-Nafis, all the *awsiya'*, their Sayyid is Maulana 'Ali (`salawatuLlahi 'alayh`). Just as the *Ashraf al-Anbiya'* is the Prophet, in the same way, the *Ashraf al-Awsiya'* is you. So where are the collections of all *awsiya'* gathered? In this personality. Why do we revere 'Ali more? Because we know the inner reality that all these things are gathered in him. For the one in whom more things are gathered, it is incumbent upon us to revere them.

Meaning, the *munkirin* (deniers) and *addad* (opponents) of the *wasi'* will meet the same fate as that of the deniers of Sayyidna Musa and Harun... the *hudud* of Pharaoh, and the fate of those who denied the *A'immah al-Islam*. The Imam of the time seized their *suwarat* when they committed the major sin of denying the love of the *A'immah al-Jalal* (Imams of Glory). The Imam (`'alayhi s-salam`) never spares such people.

Yes. This is about accepting the *zidd*. The followers of the *awliya'-i zidd* (enemies of the friends of God)... what are they? They are *kafirin*. They have denied the great one and have not accepted. They will become the fuel of Hell. Because this is a matter of the *wasi'*. The rank of the *wasi'* is very special. We commit small sins, we don't accept the great one, it's alright. But this is a matter of the Imam. What will you do here? The *tawbat al-Llah* (repentance to Allah) should be such that one does it in the darkness of the night, in the mosque, weeping profusely. That one has said something against the *hudud*. So this is a very special thing, that Maulana 'Ali... for this reason, in the foundation of Islam, the chapter of *wilayah* is special. Whether you perform Hajj ten times, or give zakat, or do any good deed, as long as there is no *Wilayat-i Amir al-Mu'minin*, which is the soul of the pillars (*arkan*), then nothing is complete. *Wilayah* is separate. And you all have known for several days that *wilayah* is for Amir al-Mu'minin. Just as there is a mother's love, the Prophet is in the station of the father, and in the station of the mother is Janab Amir al-Mu'minin (`salawatuLlahi 'alayh`). Love for the mother is necessary. For this reason, it is *Wilayat-i Amir al-Mu'minin*. The word *wilayah* is not mentioned for the Prophet. If we don't have *iman* in the Prophet, don't obey him, we are not even Muslims. We are Muslims, *ma sha Allah*. But are we also *mu'min* (believers)? We are *mu'min* only at that time when we accept Amir al-Mu'minin, 'Ali ibn Abi Talib. And not just 'Ali, but his descendants, up to this point. There have been breaks in between, in many places. Those who broke away are separate. Branches emerged, rivers emerged, all separate. But where we have reached, that is the ship of Sulaymani, and we have reached here. After Maulana Hasan came Janab Husayn, and the events that passed: the secret death of Janab Hasan, the poisoned death of Janab Husayn (`salawatuLlahi 'alayh`). And at that time, the greatest *hadd* of the Muhammadan era who emerged from the lower ranks was Salman al-Farsi. Salman. Salman is the head of all the *awliya' Allah* and the *du'at*. Salman is just Salman. There is no other option. There is no duality. The Prophet (`salawatuLlahi 'alayh`) said, `minna Ahl al-Bayt` (He is from us, the People of the House). If it were said for the Imams, it's one thing. It was said for Salman that he is from the Ahl al-Bayt. What a thing! So when you understand the ranks, you will realize Salman's status. That Salmani lineage of *da'wah* continued. The Hasani lineage, the Mustawda'in lineage, that became the Salmani lineage. And it continued, and finally, 1000 years passed since the Prophet. When 1000 years passed, a great personality came into existence in the world, and that is Sayyidna Sulayman ibn Hasan. At the time of the 35th Imam. And at that time, those who accepted Sayyidna Sulayman, who accepted Sayyidna Humam, they are the ones who are saved. And the opponents will face the punishment for their deeds. This explanation has already been given yesterday. This Salmani lineage, Hasani lineage, it became the Sulaymani lineage. And today, we consider Sayyidna Sulayman sahib as a unique personality of our time. It was bound to happen after 1000 years. Ignorant people think that Sulayman and Dawood Qutub fought and there was a split, and they had a blast amongst themselves, this happened, that happened. That's nothing. It's all an era of stories. Where one had to turn back, they turned back from their beliefs. And that, Sayyidna Humam, in his *qasidah*, says... Hazrat Shakir sahib in his book has written that the *Sur-i Awwal* (First Trumpet), Sayyidna Sulayman sahib writes, is my being. My appearance (*zuhur*) is the *Sur-i Awwal*. And at that time, what happens at the blowing of the Trumpet? When the first Trumpet of Qiyamah is blown, at that time, people will fall into a state of unconsciousness (*ghashi*). They will fall into a state of unconsciousness. So today, our opponents are, in a sense, in a state of *ghashi*. And when will they awaken? On the day of judgment. When the Qa'im al-Islam is mentioned and he appears, at that time the *Sur-i Thani* (Second Trumpet) will be blown, and at that time they will say, "If only we had been believers! We didn't know this."

And look at what kinds of miracles happened at the time of Sayyidna Sulayman sahib. There was a person, a Dawoodi, whose father was a Dawoodi and very wealthy. You all know the story, but I have to tell it as many people are new, many young people are new and small. And at the time of Sayyidna Sulayman sahib, whether it was the time of Sayyidna 'Ali or Sayyidna Ja'far, at that time, there was someone named Hasan. Hasan bin 'Abd al-Malik. 'Abd al-Malik was very wealthy, and he had a large mansion, and he had a big house, and a lot of capital. His son, Sayyidna Hasan bin 'Abd al-Malik, Hasan, at the age of 15, upon seeing the *nass* (text of designation) of Sulayman sahib (`qaddasa-Llahu ruhahu`), turned. And said that Sulayman is right, Dawood Qutub is wrong. Just like that. At 15 or 16 years of age. What is that age? But his Arabic was good, he knew all these things. He had natural ability. His father called him and said, "Look, Hasan, if you leave my house, then you cannot re-enter." He said, "No problem." That's it. He used to eat bread he bought with money he earned. Every day he would write one *para'* (part) of the Quran, his handwriting was good. He would write a *para'*, take it to the market, sell it in the square, and with the money he got, he would buy bread and eat it. He did this for years. And after that, he attained such a rank, at the time of Sayyidna 'Ali, Sayyidna Ja'far, he benefited so much, so tremendously, and received such support, that he himself reached the level of a *da'i*, the level of a *da'i-i kabir*. He is buried next to Sayyidna 'Ali sahib, and he has written many books of *haqa'iq*. Through the support of Sayyidna 'Ali and Sayyidna Sulayman, through the support of Sayyidna Ja'far, through the blessings of Sayyidna Humam, combining all these things, they made a Dawoodi boy into such a person that he became the shining star of the horizon.

`Qul lilladhina kafaru sa-tughlabuna wa tuhsharuna ila jahannama wa bi'sa l-mihad` (Say to those who disbelieve, "You will be overcome and gathered together to Hell, and wretched is the resting place").

Say to the disbelievers, "Soon you will be overcome and gathered in Hell, and it is an evil abode." Meaning, the deniers of the station of the *wasi'* will soon be overcome by the proof of truth and will seek refuge in the sect of the *da'wah* of the *zidd* (opponent). And it is an evil place of refuge.

`Qad kana lakum ayatun fi fi'atayni l-taqata, fi'atun tuqatilu fi sabili-Llah wa ukhra kafiratun yarawnahum mithlayhim ra'ya l-'ayn` (There has already been for you a sign in the two armies which met - one fighting in the cause of Allah and another of disbelievers. They saw them as twice their number by sight).

Indeed, for you there was a sign in the two groups that met in battle, one fighting in the path of Allah and the other being disbelievers. The believers saw them as twice their number. Meaning, in the *da'wat al-islam* (call of Islam) of the *natiq* (`'alayhi s-salam`), the meeting of the people of truth and falsehood is a proof of this point: `fi sabili-Llah` (in the path of Allah), meaning in the love of the *wasi'* and in proving his truth, the grateful believers establish their proof over the group of disbelievers. The *addad* (opponents) become frightened and cannot face them. They only have apparent knowledge which is insufficient as a proof. And the followers of the *wasi'* have knowledge of both the apparent (*zahir*) and the inner (*batin*), which is double theirs. Therefore, they are helpless in a confrontation.

And Allah supports with His victory whom He wills. All the common people work with apparent knowledge. You will see that there are volumes upon volumes of Quranic exegesis (*tafsir*). In this exegesis, they analyze the fine points of grammar, why there is a *zabar* (vowel mark) here, why a *zer* here, why this, why that. Where is the pronoun referring to? This is all that. But the real meaning, the *mafhum*, that the *A'immah al-Tahirin* have explained, they are unaware of that. So they have great reverence for the apparent Quran, but they have left the Quran-i Natiq (the Speaking Quran). The Quran-i Samit (Silent Quran) is one thing, and the Quran-i Natiq is Janab 'Ali (`salawatuLlahi 'alayh`). So there is a big difference between the two.

`wa-Llahu yu'ayyidu bi-nasrihi man yasha', inna fi dhalika la-'ibratan li-uli l-absar` (And Allah supports with His victory whom He wills. Indeed in that is a lesson for those of vision).

And Allah helps whom He wills. Indeed, in this incident, there is a lesson for those who have sight. Meaning, the *wasi'*, to establish his proof, grants his support to whomever he wills. This opens the eyes of those with inner vision. This is the inner interpretation (*ta'wil*). The apparent verse refers to the event of Badr, in which Allah helped the few Muslims with angels.

`Zuyyina li-n-nasi hubbu sh-shahawati mina n-nisa'i wa l-banina wa l-qanatiri l-muqantarati mina dh-dhahabi wa l-fiddati wa l-khayli l-musawwamati wa l-an'ami wa l-harth` (Beautified for people is the love of that which they desire - of women and sons, heaped-up sums of gold and silver, fine branded horses, and cattle and tilled land).

For people, the love of desires has been made attractive. For example, women, children, heaps of gold and silver, branded horses, and cattle and farms, the love for these. Meaning, for those who are attached to the *da'wat-i rasul* (call of the messenger), which is the apparent *da'wah*, the beloved things are these: opposing 'Ali, increasing their own *hudud* (ranks) and followers, claiming the ranks of the *'ulama'-i zidd* (scholars of the opposition), claiming to be enriched with inner and outer knowledge, and claiming that they can also do *muqasarah* (a spiritual exercise), that they can become *mufidin* (benefactors), and that by becoming Caliphs, they can turn the *da'wah* towards themselves. The love for all these desires is placed in their hearts, and they are happy with it.

This is about the *munkirin* (deniers). Wherever there have been turning points in the *dawr al-satr* (era of concealment)... it came at the time of Janab Ibrahim. Who was there in Ibrahim's time? I have explained it many times, that Azar, who was his maternal grandfather, he was an idol-worshipper. He was not just an idol-worshipper, he was an idol-maker. He was an idol-maker and an idol-worshipper. And he was your grandfather. You, when you were young, seven or eight years old... your childhood was such, you used to go with him, but as soon as you received spiritual station... what did you do in the books? When spiritual station came, and Janab Ibrahim was made a prophet of Allah, at that time you announced, and first, what did you do? You gave the *da'wah* to your relatives, to your family members first, then speak to others. So among the family members was your grandfather. You said, "O Azar, now come into my *da'wah*." The *da'wah* of Nuh was ongoing, and it ended. You became a *nasiq* (abrogator). You abrogated the *da'wah* of Nuh and spread your own *shari'ah*. At that time, you gave the *da'wah* to Janab Azar, but he deviated, and what did he do? It is mentioned about him in the book that `ankara wa-stakbara` (he denied and was arrogant). He denied. And after that, a second time, it advanced, and a time came when the Prophet (`salawatuLlahi 'alayh`)'s Islam spread, and you brought Islam and began to spread it. At that time, Abu Bakr, the first Caliph. The first Caliph, he was from the *hudud* of the *da'wah* of 'Isa. He was of old age. He thought that it might be him, because 'Isa had prophesied that after me a great one will come whose name will be Ahmad. So, he thought that. And the Prophet's miracles were such, and he was so truthful and trustworthy, that he immediately accepted the Prophet. He accepted, but in private, you said, secretly, you said, "O Messenger of Allah, we accept you, but after you, take care of me." Because he was a great personality, at that time in the *hudud* of 'Isa, he was a *hudud-i talibiyyah*. "Take care of me." As soon as he did this, the Prophet's mouth uttered: `Hadha 'aduwwu-Llah fa-tabarra'u minhu` (This is an enemy of Allah, so disassociate from him). This is the enemy of Allah, disassociate from him. Just as was said for Azar, for Janab Ibrahim. `Hadha 'aduwwu-Llah fa-tabarra'u minhu`. This happened twice. Then, you, Da'i-shah, write that after him, a great... the matter advanced, and when the time of Sayyidna Humam came, at that time, these Dawoodis, our opponents, the great *hadd* of that time, 'Abdullah al-Mahfuzi, was in the *hudud* of Dawood ibn 'Ajabshah. His stronghold was in Yemen, and he was well-known. But he became arrogant. And what did he do? When he saw that Dawood Qutub had all the accounts, all the properties, the saliyas of Ahmedabad were with him, the money was with him, and the keys to the treasury were with him, and the books were with him, he immediately left Sayyidna Humam's side. At that time too, it was announced that `Hadha 'aduwwu-Llah fa-tabarra'u minhu` (This is an enemy of Allah, so disassociate from him). This is an enemy of Allah, disassociate from him. So wherever such enemies of religion arise, disassociation is necessary. It's not that we accept 'Ali and also accept the first Caliph. We accept the first Caliph in the same way we accept Manmohan Singh. That you are the head of the country, it is necessary to accept you. But that is apparent. We don't accept him from the heart. From the heart, we accept whom we must accept.

Number? 45. ...*al-Fatihah*... *al-Fatihah*.

<!-- AUG:ibrahim-2015 lecture 14 -->

Yeh, 44 ke aakhir mein hai. 44. Yeh aur ho gaya. Khatam ho gaya shayad. 180 safa. 180. Aap se bacha lijiye. al-sabireen was-sadiqeen, sadiqeen. 180 hai. Upar. Yeh kya hai? Aap se bacha lijiye. Kya likha hua hai? Al-sabireen was-sadiqeen. Haan wahi. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. al-sadiqeen was-sadiqeen wal-qaniteen wal-munfiqeen wal-mustaghfireena bil-ashar. Zor se boliye. Meri taraf se. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Al-sabireena was-sadiqeen wal-qaniteena wal-munfiqeena wal-mustaghfireena bil-ashar (In the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful. The patient, the truthful, the devoutly obedient, the ones who spend [in charity], and the ones who seek forgiveness before dawn).

Ya'ni, taqiyyah ke zamane mein masa'ib par sabr karne wale, aur wasi' ki taraf da'wat karne pe sach bolne wale, aur maqam-e-wasayat ki taraf bulane wale, aur 'ulum se fayda pahunchane wale, aur chhupi hui da'wat-e-batinah mein ifada-e-ta'leem se qayam karne wale. Ji. Ya'ni sabireen o sadiqeen o qaniteen ke ma'na yeh hain, ya'ni taqiyyah ke zamane mein jo log khamosh baithte hain ke bhai zyada gadbad nahi karna, 'awam se ulajhna nahi. Sabireen mein hote hain, sadiqeen hain, qaniteen hain. Ihtiyat se raat karte hain, zyada jo hai shor sharaba nahi karte. Ya'ni taqiyyah ke zamane mein masa'ib par sabr karne wale aur wasi' ki taraf da'wat karne mein sach bolne wale. Wasi' ki taraf jo da'wat karna hai, us pe jo hai peeche nahi hatna hai. Shuru se aa raha hai, yeh do-teen martaba aa chuka hai, ke usme jo hai sach bolne wale. Aur maqam-e-wasayat ki taraf bulane wale, aur 'ulum se fayda pahunchane wale, aur chhupi hui da'wat-e-batinah, da'wat-e-batinah mein ifada-e-ta'leem ka qayam karne wale. Ya'ni, zahiri kuch ho ya na ho, magar batini da'wat jo hai woh apni chalti rahegi. Jaisa mukhtalif zamanon mein jo hai mukhtalif hua hai. Ke bhai, jo Fatimi A'immah guzre hain Misr ke andar, woh das Imam guzre hain. Gyarah se leke bees tak. Woh gyarah se bees tak jo hai woh hukumat bhi thi, Imam bhi the. Uss waqt jo hai bharpur jo hai da'wat chalti thi. Barah jazeeron mein puri da'wat thi. Barah jazeeron se matlab yeh hai ke aaj jo hai teen jazeere hain. Uss waqt barah jazeeron mein da'wat phaili hui thi. Aur kaisi thi? Sicily, ya'ni Sicily ke jo ilaqah hai wahan bhi da'wat thi. Saqaliyah, Saqaliyah bolte hain Sicily ko. Wahan bhi da'wat thi. Toh woh bhi aisa hai ke... woh darte nahi the. Hukumat apni thi. Woh jo hai uss waqt jo hai taqiyyah ka zamana nahi tha. Lekin taqiyyah ke zamane mein aap khamosh raho, zyada... magar batini taur par jo hai da'wat barabar phailao.

Shahidallahu annahu la ilaha illa huwa wal-mala'ikatu wa ulul-'ilmi qa'iman bil-qist. La ilaha illa huwal-'azizul-hakim (Allah witnesses that there is no deity except Him, and [so do] the angels and those of knowledge - [that He is] maintaining [creation] in justice. There is no deity except Him, the Exalted in Might, the Wise). Ab dekhiye iska... Tarjuma yeh hai, khud Allah ne gawahi di ke iske siwa koi ma'bood nahi, aur mala'ikah ne bhi, aur in ahl... aur ahl-e-'ilm ne jo 'adl par qayam hain, ke iske siwa koi ma'bood nahi, jo taqat be-adeel aur woh hakeem hai. Ya'ni, wasi' ke siwa koi Imam nahi. Ab dekhiye kaise is martaba... Shahidallahu annahu la ilaha illa huwa. Hamesha padhte hain Shahidallahu annahu... Kya matlab hai? Allah ne gawahi di ke koi nahi hai... ilah siwaye Allah ke. Toh aapki gawahi khud apne se dete koi? Koi dusra dena. Gawahi jo hai, gawah dusre hote hain. Jaisa nikah mein hai wali alag hota hai, gawah alag hote. Toh gawah jo hai, Allah ne gawahi di. Shahidallahu annahu la ilaha illa huwa. Nahi hai koi ma'bood siwaye uske, hu. Wahan jo hai Allah nahi aaya hai. Toh kya matlab hai? Shahidallah ke ma'ne, woh jo hai 'aqle awwal hai ke Allah... 'aqle awwal ne gawahi di ke nahi hai koi ma'bood siwaye uske. Jis waqt jo hai mawjudat shuru hoti hai, us waqt jo hai bataya gaya 'aqle awwal ne sabse pehle tawheed Rabbul 'alameen ki, aur kya kaha? Ke na main apne se kisi ko paida kar sakta hun, na koi mujhe paida kar sakta hai. Balke hum sab ka paida karne wala koi dusra hai. Woh iqbal jo kiya, usne i'tiraf kiya, aur baghair mu'allim ke, baghair mulzim ke kiya. Toh sabse pehli gawahi thi dunya ki. Dunya nahi, 'alam-e-ruhani ki. Yeh dunya to kuch baad ki baat hai. Bahut... alag baat hai, bahut door hai. Lekin jo 'alam-e-ruhani tha, jahan ajba'-e-nurani the, wahan par usne gawahi di. Toh woh sirf dunya ka pehla aadmi nahi tha, balke universe ka tha. 'aqle awwal ne sabse pehle gawahi di. Toh uski taraf ishara hai ke shahidallah. Allah kyun laaye? Isliye ke uske bare mein bhi log hairan hain. Ke kaise to gawahi de diya woh? Baghair mu'allim ke, baghair mulzim ke kis tareh se gawahi de di?

Toh shahidallahu annahu la ilaha illa huwa. Allah, kya hai? Khud Allah ne gawahi di ke iske siwa koi ma'bood nahi hai. Aur mala'ikah ne bhi. Ab mala'ikah kaun hain? Dekhiye, aage kya hai? Wa ulul-'ilmi qa'iman bil-qist. Ya'ni wasi' ke siwa koi... Ya'ni ulul-'ilmi qa'iman bil-qist ke tarjuma, ke mala'ikah ne bhi gawahi di hai. Mala'ikah kya hain? 'aqle awwal ke baad jo hai, das uqool jo hue, woh sab ulul-'ilm hain. Ilm diye gaye, woh ajba'-e-nurani jo hain. La ilaha illa huwal-'azizul-hakim. Ke jo hai, ahl-e-'ilm ne, jo 'adl par qayam hain ke iske siwa koi ma'bood nahi hai. Sab ne gawahi di. 'Uqool ke 'alam mein agar woh log nahi dete, to wahan nahi rehte, yahan aa jate. Toh sabse pehle jo hai 'aqle awwal ne di, uske baad munba'is awwal ne di, aur uske baad jo hai saat uqool aage badhe. Is tareh se nau uqool hue. Aur uske baad jo hai jo munba'is thani tha, ya'ni jo 'aqle... thani banta tha, aur jo hai teesra number aa sakta tha, lekin ho gaya kya? Woh 'ashir ho gaya. Ke usne dekha ke bhai main to peeche reh gaya. Ab toh sab mawjudat kar chuke hain. Kyunki is tareh se jo hai 'aqle awwal ko dekh kar sab hairat zada ho gaye. Toh hairat mein hona, woh Allah ki sifat hai. Ke Allah ke bare mein hum hairan ho jayein ya shauq karein, usko Allah kehte hain. Toh 'aqle awwal ne jo hai gawahi di. Jab sab se pehle. Aur unko dekh kar do aadmi uthe. Do... Dekhiye ek ke baad do aaya hai. Isiliye jo hai ginti mein ek ke baad do aayega. Koi bhi aap ginti le lo, Japanese le lo, Chinese le lo ya English le lo, sab ke paas one ke baad two aayega. Lekin, ek khaas baat usme yeh hai, ke jaisa hamesha har zuban mein aap dekhein ke singular ke baad plural aata hai. Wahid ke baad jama' aata hai. Lekin 'Arabi ek woh zuban hai ke jismein ek ke baad jo hai tasniyah aata hai.

Tasniyah. ...ata-Allahu bakha huwa, bakha-huma. Hum alag hai. Bakha-hu ya'ni unko Allah taala bakhsh de. Bakha-huma, un dono ko. Munasib ke liye aata hai, do haath ke liye aata hai. Aur bakha-hum ya'ni un sab ke liye. Yahan jo hai do ka jo hua, toh dual personality, do aadmi uthe. Do rising star the, jisko jo hai 'aqle awwal ke baad 'ain, munba'is awwal aur munba'is thani. Munba'is awwal jo hai, jagah mustahqil tha, isliye ke usne jo hai batini taur par dekha ke 'aqle awwal kis tareh se kiya hai. Kya wajah hai ke yeh aage badh gaya wahan se. Toh usne jo hai batin ko dekha. Aur jo zahir ko dekh raha tha woh 'aqle thani. Woh jo hai kya tha? Usne kaha ke bhai kya behtareen inke poshak dekho kaisi hai, falana. Ya'ni unke appearance ko dekhne laga. Jaise hi dekha, woh tikao ho gaya. Toh ek jo hai zahir ko dekh raha tha, ek batin ko dekh raha tha. Toh us waqt jo hai 'aqle awwal ke baad munba'is awwal aage badh gaya. Aur woh stagnant ho gaya, theher gaya, ruk gaya. Kaun? 'aqle thani. Aur itne mein jo hai yeh ulul-'ilm jo hain, jo saat uqool aage badh gaye. Saat uqool aage badhe, toh 'aqle awwal aur munba'is awwal do aur yeh saat, nau ho gaye. Uss waqt inko khayal aaya ke bhai main to wahan tha choti pe tha. Kahan aa ke reh gaya main? Main kyun jo hai jamid ho gaya? Uss waqt jo hai, woh sawal kiye apne immediate boss se, ya'ni jo nawan dayra tha, nawe dayre mein laakhon karodon log... ajba the. Unse poocha gaya ke bhai meri kya khata hui ke main yahan... Toh bole aap wahm-e-masawat mein the. Ke bhai, aap ne jo hai Allah taala ki 'ubudiyat qubool ki aur 'aqle awwal ko jaana. Lekin apne saath jo tha, woh bahr... batin ko dekh raha tha, aap zahir ko dekh rahe the. Yeh farq tha. Aur aap samjhe ke hum dono barabar hain. Yahan bhi koi rutbe ke liye jo hai do aadmi nikalte hain, toh ek ki nazar jo hoti hai, usko hi badhaya jaata hai. Toh yahan waisi hua, ke jo hai woh 'aqle 'ashir ho gaya.

Ya'ni wasi'... ab isko jo hai jo Allah ke liye kalimat aaye hain, woh aap Sayyidna Husain Zuljaddain jo hain, ya'ni... Ya'ni, wasi' ke siwa koi Imam nahi. Iski gawahi natiq ne di. Aur jo da'wat ki khidmat par ma'moor hain, aur jo ulul-'ilm... ahl-e-'ilm, ahl-e-'ilm-e-batin se... batin hain aur maqam-e-wasi' ki ma'rifat rakhte hain. Jo ghuluw aur ghulu nahi karte. Dekhiye ab kya hai yeh... ya'ni wasi' ke maqam ko natiq ne... ke mere baad man kuntu mawlahu fa hadha 'Aliyyun mawlahu. Yeh jo hai gawahi jo hai natiq ne di. Aur uske uske alawa, jo khaas bande the, ya'ni Salman Farsi hai, Miqdad hai, 'Ammar-e-Yasir hai, Abu Dharr Ghifari hai. Ba'z khaas jo hain, yeh sahabi hain. Sahabi toh ya'ni Makke mein bahut the. Lekin jo sahih iman rakhte the, na ghuluw karte the, na... karte the, woh chand the. Aur kaun the woh? Balke i'tidal par qayam the. Jo ghuluw aur ghulu nahi karte, balke i'tidal par qayam hain, woh sab ne bhi gawahi di... yehi log hain, ki gawahi di, yehi gawahi di ke wasi' ka hum-sar aur hum-misal nahi. Haan. Aur wasi'... wasi' jo hai woh hum-misal koi nahi hai. Ya'ni wasi' paighambar ek hi hain. Imam kai hain. Wasi' ke jo bete hote hain, woh imamat wahan se shuru hui. Mawlana Hasan se shuru hui aur aaj tak chali aa rahi hai. Magar Mawlana Ali salawatullahi 'alayh ke darje ko koi nahi pa sakta. Na Husain, na Hasan, na koi. Uske liye kya kalimah hai? La hawla wa la quwwata illa billahil 'aliyyil 'azim. La hawla, hum jo padhte hain la hawla padh ke woh... karte hain kisi cheez se bachne ke liye. Lekin uske ma'na kya bataye hain ta'wil mein? La hawla. Koi hawl nahi hai. Na koi quwwat hai. Wa la quwwah illa 'Aliyyil 'azim. La hawla wa la quwwata illa billahil 'aliyyil 'azim. Ke na Hasan waise hain, na Husain waise hain. La hawl ek hai aur quwwat ek hai. Woh na Husain ka darjah hai, na Hasan ka hai, jo 'Aliyyil 'azim ka darjah hai. Yeh kya hai? 'Adl pe qayam hain. Hum-sar jo hai Ali ka koi nahi hai aur wohi hakeem hain. Aur inke siwa koi Imam nahi.

Innad-deena 'indallahil-Islam (Indeed, the religion in the sight of Allah is Islam). Ya'ni, natiq 'alayhissalam ke nazdeek deen sirf ta'at ka dusra naam hai. Aur woh yeh hai ke Allah ke hukum se wasi' ko inka haq, haqq-e-wasayat de diya, de dena hai. Pas, ta'at jo hai woh badi cheez hoti hai. Jab paighambar ne kaha ke man kuntu mawlahu fa hadha 'Aliyyun mawlahu, jitna majma' tha us waqt mein usne kaha ke qalu bala ya rasulullah, qalu bala. Bakhin bakhin ya rasulullah. Ghadir Khumm mein jo hai jab a'lan farmaye, toh us waqt jo hai jitne log the, 70,000 ka majma' tha. Aur saamne khade the. Jo log peeche reh gaye the unko paighambar aage bulaye. Jo aage nikal gaye the unko leke aaye, jahan khade the aap. Mimbar banaya gaya, kahan? Ghadir Khumm mein. Ghadir Khumm ki jo miqat hai, wahan maidan mein khade rahe, aur woh jagah par jo hai Ali par nas hui. Aur kya kiye pehle? Ke woh chokiyan jo hote hain oont ke upar rakhne ke, uske... kyunki mimbar to koi tha nahi wahan, jangal ki jagah hai. Toh ek ke upar ek chokiyan rakh kar, aap khade ho gaye. 63 saal ki 'umr. 63 saal mein aap ka inteqal hua, teen mahine ke baad inteqal hua. Aur janab-e-Ali 35 saal ke. Aur janab-e-Ali baazu khade the, aap upar chadhe, aur Ali ko uthaya. Ab dekhiye 35 saal ke jawan ko jo hai 63 saal ka budha uthaya. Aur utha kar aisa uthaya, hadha 'Aliyyun mawla. Man kuntu mawlahu fa hadha 'Aliyyun mawlahu. Main aaj tak jiska mawla tha, aaj se yeh Ali uske mawla hai. Yeh nahi hai ke majme mein se koi khade ho jaye ke hum bhi Ali hai na, mera bhi naam Ali hai. Toh Ali kaun hai? Woh jo hai Ali Murtaza hai.

Toh ta'at ka naam jo hai, woh deen hai. Deen kya hai? Ek hi chala aa raha hai. Adam 'alayhissalam bhi deen phailane aaye, Nuh bhi aaye, Ibrahim bhi aaye, Musa aaye, 'Isa aaye, aur janab-e-paighambar aaye. Toh deen jo hai Allah taala ka, woh ek hi chala aa raha hai aaj tak. Ya'ni ta'at ka. Bade jo hain unki ita'at karo. Lekin shari'atein alag alag hain. Yeh ek technical cheez hai. Ke deen jo hai ek hi chala aa raha hai, lekin shari'atein badalti hain. Ibrahim ki shari'at alag, Musa ki alag, 'Isa ki alag, aur janab-e-paighambar ki alag. Jaisa jaisa zamane ka lihaz hota hai, waise shari'at ke ahkamat aate hain. Yeh aakhri shari'at hai jo bilkul dastur-e-Muhammadi bolte hain, aur jo hai yeh badi khaas... matlab, poori jame' hai. Ke jo Tawrat mein aaya hai, ya... Zabur mein hai, ya jo hai Injil mein hai, usse badh kar jo hai yeh hai ke jo hai janab-e-Nabi salawatullahi 'alayh... ke jariye se jo aaya, wohi Qur'an hua, aur wohi jo hai ahkamat hain.

Toh ta'at ka naam dusra deen hai. Deen ka naam ta'at hai. Aur woh yeh hai ke Allah ke hukum se wasi' ka haq de do. Wa ma yantiqu 'anil-hawa in huwa illa wahyun yuha (Nor does he speak from [his own] inclination. It is not but a revelation revealed). Nabi koi kalam nahi karta hai jab tak ke us par wahi nahi aa jaati. Wahi aati hai, to hi wohi bolta hai. Aur Nabi bolta hai. Jo kabhi aisa i'tiraf nahi hua ke bhai Nabi jo hai jo kuch bolte the woh sachche the. Aur bachpan se aap jo hai mashhur ho gaye, ke sachche aur ameen. Toh koi ikhtilaf padta hi nahi tha Makke ke andar. Yahan tak ke jo dushman-e-deen the, aapko qatl ke dar pe hain, woh log aap ke paas la kar... da'irat rakhte the, ya'ni jo apne amanatein rakhte the Makke mein. Dost rakhte to alag baat hai, dushman rakhte the. Ke bhai inse sachcha koi nahi.

Wa makhtalafal-ladheena utul-kitaba illa min ba'di ma ja'ahumul-'ilmu baghyan baynahum (And those who were given the Scripture did not differ except after knowledge had come to them - out of jealous animosity between themselves). Aur na ikhtilaf kiya in logon ne jinko kitab di, magar ba'd is baat ke ke inke paas 'ilm aa chuka. Warna, ikhtilaf ki... wajah, aapas ki dushmani aur hasad thi. Wajah ikhtilaf ki... Wajah ikhtilaf ki aapas ki dushmani aur hasad thi. Ji. Ya'ni unke paas kitab aa gayi thi, Qur'an-e-Majeed haathon mein tha. Lekin bughz tha, hasad tha. Ke yeh Ali kaise? Hum bhi jo hai, kya the? Agar Ali damad hain, toh janab-e-Abu Bakr aur janab-e-Umar paighambar ke susre the. Khusur hain. Woh badh kar hain ya yeh badh kar hain? Bole yeh kya hukum laga diye? Masjid-e-Nabawi jo itni choti si masjid thi, us zamane mein bahut choti thi. Aur har jagah jo hai yahan ghar the, baazu. Chota sa ilaqah us zamane mein tha 1400 baras pehle. Toh har ek aadmi apne ghar se seedha aake masjid mein andar aa gaya. Idhar se aa raha, idhar se aa raha. Barah, chaudah darwaze the aur ab to bahut badi ho gayi, lekin us zamane ki baat. Toh ek waqt wahi aayi aap pe ke tamam darwaze band kar do. Jibril-e-Ameen aaye aur aap se kaha ke sab darwaze band kar diye jayein siwaye Ali ke. Ali ka darwaza khula rahe, baqi... Ab shuru ho gayi che-me-goyiyan. Wohi hasad. Ke bhai yeh kya baat hai? Ke damad hain, chachere bhai hain, unka darwaza khula rahega aur jo hai paighambar farma rahe hain ke iska nahi, sab jo hai darwaze band kar do. Uss waqt paighambar ne kaha ke wa ma yantiqu 'anil-hawa in huwa illa wahyun yuha. Bhai koi baat nahi karta jab tak ke Allah ki taraf se hukum na aa jaye. Sab jaake khamosh ho gaye.

Ya'ni, rasul ki zahiri shari'at ko maanne walon ne is waqt ikhtilaf kiya jab inke paas maqam-e-wasi' ke muta'alliq wazeh daleel aa chuki. Maanne se inkar kiya. Wajah iski wohi hai ke... wohi ke Ali se inko dushmani thi. Dil ki kadurat zahir ki aur ikhtilaf par utar aaye. Hasad... Ke bhai yeh Ali kyu aa gaye? Aur unka ek matlab tha andar se, woh yeh ke paighambar jo hai 'Isa ke baad bada Nabi aane wala hai. Janab-e-'Isa jo hai peshan-goi kiye hain ke mere baad ek bada Nabi aayega jiska naam Ahmad hoga. Toh Ahmad jo hai paighambar ka naam aasmanon mein hai aur Muhammad salawatullahi 'alayh yeh zameen mein hai. Yeh to maan liye ke bhai paighambar 'ameen o sadiq hain, hum kuch keh nahi sakte. Lekin uske baad yeh nubuwwat ka ghar to ho gaya, imamat ka ghar nahi hona. Imam kaise inke baad? Hasan ko bana rahe hain, Husain ko bana rahe hain, yeh kya hai? Unko malum hi nahi ke Hasan kaun hai. Unki aankhein hi nahi thi, unki 'ilm hi nahi tha unke paas. Toh ab kya hai? Hasad, dushmani ho gayi. Aur kya kiye? Wajah uski yeh hai ke Ali se unko dushmani thi. Dil ki kadurat zahir ki aur ikhtilaf par utar aaye.

Dekhiye agar... matlab... paighambar ko jo maanna, yeh Islam mein aana hai. Ek bade platform pe sab ko la diya gaya. Lekin ab uske baad jo chota dayra hai, wasayat ka, usme kitne log aaye? Woh hai iman ka dayra. Agar paighambar ke jaisa saare log wasi' ko bhi maan lete, toh phir ikhtilaf kahan hota yeh dunya mein? Shi'a, Sunni, falana falana falana jitne hain, woh sirf ikhtilaf yeh hai ke paighambar ke baad Ali hain ya khalifa Abu Bakr hain. Hum nahi maante. Toh isi liye jo hai be-bahra ho gaye, khatam ho gaye, hat gaye. Toh yaad rakhein ke Ali ke jo hai, woh wazeh daleel aane ke baad, yeh dekhiye wazeh dalayil aa chuke. Ya'ni paighambar ne ek baar nahi, Zul-'Ashirah ki majlis... jo jabke paighambar saade... Ali jo hai saade das saal ke the, us waqt se le kar aakhri umr tak aap jo hai fazilatein gina rahe hain janab-e-Ali ki. Aur unko jo hai aage badha rahe hain. Toh samajh mein aa jaana chahiye tha ke bhai aaindah yeh jo hai amr-e-khalifa banenge. Lekin, nahi maanna tha, nahi maane. Aur agar maante toh yeh sab baat aati nahi thi.

Matlab yeh hai ke jitnon ki qismat mein tha, jo hubb-e-Ali rakhte the, jo najat-dahinda hone wale hain, ya samjho aage badhne wale hain, woh Ali ko bhi maan-ne wale. Baqi jo hai sab, woh... kuffar hain ek lihaz se. Aur kaise hain? Ke kufr kiye hain unhone, Ali ke maqam mein. Shirk kiye hain. Shirk kaun sa hota hai? Shirk fi maqamillah nahi hai. Allah ke bare mein shirk nahi kiye. Fi maqam-e-Mawlana Ali salawatullahi 'alayh. Shirk kiye hain, unke saath, bhai yeh bhi khalifa, yeh bhi khalifa. Yeh pehle hain, dusre hain, teesre, yeh chauthe hain. Yeh shirk hai. Uss zamane mein ek jo hai, aaj se, Nizam ka zamana tha, 80-90 saal pehle, yahan jo hai 'Ashurkhana-e-Zehra mein, Zehra Masjid mein, ho rahi thi. Aur bade... kar-o-far se jo hai woh bayan ho raha tha. Chote agha sahab. Uss waqt jo hai Nizam of Hyderabad aaye. Andar se Shi'a ho gaye the. Magar the, Usman naam tha. Usman Ali Khan. Aake baith gaye. Ab neeche jo hai zillullah, jo hukumat ke... Nizam, Nizam-ul-Mulk baithe hain, aur in padhne mein jo hai... bahut hota hai, lekin Shi'a log to trained rehte hain. Woh jo hai shuru kar diye. Ab thodi majlis hui, uske baad jo hai Nizam ne roka. Ab Nizam roke to rukna padta hai. Badshah tha. Bole, Maulvi Sahab, aap yeh bataiye ke aap tafzeeli hain kya? Dekhiye 80 saal pehle hua hai, 80-90 saal pehle. Aap tafzeeli hain kya? Tafzeel ya'ni aap fazilat dete hain kya Ali ko yeh teeno par?

Toh Shi'a log tafzeeli bolte hain. Woh jo hai ek sect hai, ek maanne wale hain, jo Ali par... Ali ko jo hai teeno se badh kar maante hain. Usko kehte hain tafzeeli. Ab jab sawal kiye Nizam, toh us waqt... unhone haath jod ke kahe ke bhai... ya Badshah Salamat, haan main tafzeeli hun. Bole samjhaiye kaise. Ab Nizam pooch rahe hain. Bole samjhaiye kaise. Toh Nizam ko malum tha. Bahut learned aadmi, bahut qabil aadmi the. Lekin woh sab logon ko sunana chah rahe the. Bole ab samjhaiye Maulvi Sahab, kaise hain tafzeeli? Ali jo hai kaisa rutbe mein bade hain? Toh us waqt unhone kaha ke aap yeh jaaniye ke sabse pehle dunya mein kaun sa Nabi aaya? Bole Adam aaye. Yeh Nizam ne kaha. Phir bole uske baad kaun aaye? Bole Nuh aaye. Uske baad kaun aaye? Toh bole Ibrahim aaye, dunya mein. Jo natiq Nabi hain ek ke baad ek aate gaye. Aur Ibrahim ke baad Musa aaye, Musa ke baad 'Isa aaye. Paanch Nabi aaye. Uske baad kaun aaya? Bole Nabi hamare sarwar-e-kaynat aaye. Nabi aaye. Toh bole Nabi aaye, aur unka darjah kya tha? Bole Khair-ul-Mursaleen hain, Sayyid-ul-Mursaleen hain, Khatam-ul-Anbiya hain, Sayyid-ul-Bashar hain. Saare jo hai sifat hain.

Toh bole yeh kya baat hui? Ke bhai Adam pehle aaye the, Adam bade hain. Aur phir uske baad Nuh hain, Ibrahim hain, Musa hain, 'Isa hain. Lekin Nabi jo hai aakhir mein aake sab se bade ho gaye. Haan, bade hain. Bola hai. Toh bole jab Nabi jo hai aakhir mein aake bade ho sakte, toh yeh chauthe khalifa jo hai, woh teeno se badh ke nahi ho sakte? Teeno se kyun nahi badh sakte? Bole wah wah, kya jawab diye hain, kya jawab diye hain. Suno bole, suno bole kya Maulvi Sahab kya bol rahe hain. Ke yahan Hyderabad mein hua yeh. Toh matlab yeh hai ke jo jaanne wale the, ahl-e-'ilm the, unko malum tha ke Ali ki kya fazilat hai. Lekin 'aam log, jinko bughz tha. Bughz-e-Abi... yeh aapko do-teen martaba bol chuka hun ke jo hai Karbala ke maidan mein un logon ne kaha ke bughz-e-Abik, hum aapke baap ke bughz mein maar rahe hain aapko. Aur kya hai us waqt? Wa man yakfur bi-ayatillahi fa-innallaha saree'ul-hisab (And whoever disbelieves in the verses of Allah - then indeed, Allah is swift in account). Aur jo Allah ki aayaton ka inkar karega, toh jaan le, Allah bahut... tez hisab lene wala hai. Ya'ni, jo a'imma-e-deen ki mukhalifat ka inkar karte hain, woh jaan lein ke Mawlana Sabir, al-A'immah, inka hisab bahut jald aur tezi se lenge. Ab yeh dekhiye yeh puri peshan-goi aa gayi. Ke jo Allah ke aayaton par... aayat jo hai nishani ko bolte hain. Allah ki nishani kaun se hain? Ke Adam, Nuh, Ibrahim, Musa, aur janab-e-jitne bhi a'immah hain, sab nishaniyan hain Allah taala ki. Aayatein hain. Yeh jo hain jo aaye, toh unko maanne se inkar kar diya. Maqamat ka inkar karte hain. Aur jaan lein ke Sabir al-A'immah kaun hain? Sabir al-A'immah bole ya Sabir al-Nutuqa bole, woh ek hi baat hai.

Toh paighambar... janab-e-Qa'im aayenge, toh woh nutuqa ke bhi khatima karne wale hain aur a'immah ka bhi khatima karne wale. Woh khatam, qiyamat hai. Khatima ya'ni aakhri. Toh Sabir al-A'immah jab aayenge, bahut jald hisab lenge. Toh ab hisab kitab ka waqt aa gaya hai. Ab chand baras reh gaye. Ke us waqt hisab kitab hoga. Aur jo... sahih maanne wale hain, unko jaza milegi, aur jo nahi maanne wale hain, unko azraq mein phenk diya jayega. Ab woh mawjudat jaane to malum padega ke kis tareh se azraq hote hain, kis tareh se jo hai kaun se kaun se shape mein unko dala jaata hai, jisko 'azab-e-adna' kehte hain. 'azab-e-adna ke baad 'azab-e-atraf hota hai, 'azab-e-atraf ke baad sakhar... 'azab-e-akbar, al-Haqqah ka surah jo hai... inna 'azaba... jo hai surah isi par tashreeh hai. Ya'ni Sabir al-A'immah saatve hain. Woh saatve Imam honge. Aur jo hai anbiya ke aur a'imma'on ke aakhir.

Aur yeh bhi nahi hai, hamare jo Qa'im 'alayhissalam hain, unki ma'rifat karna aap. Unko malum hona ke Qa'im aane wale hain, hai bhai... woh jo hai hashr hoga, aur hashr ka maidan hoga... woh to hoga. Aap ki haisiyat kya hai? Aap kitna jaante hain unko? Bhai Sabir-e-A'immah bole to khatam ho gayi baat. Nahi, bahut hai peeche. Ab jitna aapko samajh mein aata hai utna hai. Ke bhai, woh jo hai Sabir al-A'immah bhi hain, aur jo hai nutuqa ke aakhir mein bhi hain. Phir dusre jo hai, yeh sitr ke khatam karne wale nahi hain. Sitr ek raat ke khatam nahi. Weekend jo hota hai, hafte ki raat khatam ho rahi hai toh dusre din itwar aata hai. Toh woh sitr jo hai, sitr kya hai... balke poore saat... jo sitr guzre hain, usko khatam kar rahe hain aap. Saat sitr ya'ni kashf o sitr. Chaar laakh baras ka hisab karne wale hain. Ab jo Qa'im aa rahe hain, aur isi ke liye unka naam kya hai? Qa'im-e-Muntazar. Woh Qa'im jiska bahut zamanon se intezar ho raha hai. Sitr ke Qa'im, saat hazaar baras ka hisab karne wale. Kashf ka Qa'im jo hai, woh kab hua? Kashf ka Qa'im jab Adam 'alayhissalam paida hue. Uss waqt jo hai janab-e-Hunaid jo hai, woh kashf ke Imam kahe jaate hain. Lekin yeh Qa'im jo aa rahe hain, woh saat kashf aur saat sitr ke malik hain.

Badi badi kitabein hain, kya unke bayanath hain, aap samajh paana mushkil. Itni oonchi baatein. Aur kaun bataye hain? Hamare paas ke ek mansoob, Mawlana Ikram Ali Sahab. Kya bayan kiye hain. Du'at jo hai ashash kar rahe hain unki 'ilm par. Ke kaisa jo hai... Ikram Ali Sahab ka. Ikram Ali Sahab jo hai, woh hasti hain jo sabse chote mansoob hue. Saade pachchis, chabbis saal mein mansoob ho gaye. Aur qabr aap hamesha ziyarat karte hain, woh karte hain. Unki kya haisiyat thi? Mansoob ke rutbe se aur aage nikal gaye the. Lekin jo bataya gaya hai, manasib ke darje mein hain. Aur usme, unko jo hai, 23 saal mein aap jo hai, padhne ke liye chale gaye, wahan, Najran. Us waqt Badr mein tha ya Najran mein tha. Wahan padhne gaye aur jo hai dhai saal, teen saal padhe. Jab padh ke wapas aa rahe the, toh ho raha kya raha tha ke har jume mein, namaz ki pabandi thi. Aur bahut hoshiyar the. Magar dekhne mein aap dekhiye ke 250 baras... 150 baras pehle jo hai, kya budhu-bash honge, kya kaathi hogi, kya unke jo hai khane peene ka hoga, kuch bhi nahi. In ghar se chale gaye padhne ke liye. Padhne ke liye gaye to wahan din raat jo hai padhte the. Aur har jume mein Da'i Sahab jo hai unko aage badha rahe hain. Saamne wahan hai, wahan se yahan la rahe, yahan se yahan. Ab wahan jo bade bade daadi wale the Najran mein, woh pareshan ke bhai yeh bachcha to jo hai wahan aaya hai, Hindustan se, aur unko jo hai aage badha rahe hain. Aur hum to yahan reh gaye. Hum to kai kitabein khatam kar liye. Uss waqt jo hai pooche ke bhai... Maulvi yeh to ek muhajir hijrat karke aaya hai bachcha, yeh chota bachcha hai, yeh woh hai. Bole tum unki surat-e-ruhani nahi dekh sakte. Yahan jo surat-e-ruhani unki dekh rahe hain ke Da'i Sahab dekh rahe hain. Bole Buraq ke jaise jaa rahi hai. Buraq kya hai Buraq? Bolo... Buraq ghoda, jo Nabi alayhissalam jis par baith ke jo hai woh mi'raj ke upar safar kiye the. Lekin hai kya Buraq? 'Aam 'awam mein mashhur hai ke ek ghode ki shakal hai aur aurat ka sar hai. Yeh... tasveerein batate hain woh karte. Waisa nahi hai. Buraq jo hai matlab yeh hai barq se nikla hai. Barq jo hai electronic hai. Ya'ni electricity aur electronic ke jaisa tezi se daudti hai, waisa jo hai unki jo hai ruhani kaifiyat badh rahi thi rozana. Toh har jume mein jo hai aage badha rahe hain. Phir jo kitabein likhe hain, woh... reference lete hain du'at, kabhi Da'i ka Da'i leta hai, theek hai. Woh bhi Da'i hain, yeh bhi Da'i hain. Bhai yeh falane Sayyidna Hasan, Sayyidna Hasan yeh bataye hain, ya Sayyidna Sulaiman Sahab ne yeh bataye... woh ek... baaz kitabon mein aisa hai jismein likhe hain ke Sayyidna Ikram Ali ne yeh likha hai. Toh mansoob ka hawala dena, ke ek mansoob jo hai mu'min ka hawala de, ke bhai un falane jo hai sahab jo hai yeh likh rahe. Yeh kaisa hota hai? Lekin qabiliyat thi aapki. Toh matlab yeh hai ke humko fakhr hai ke hamare paas jo hai ek aisa mansoob bhi paida hua, jo itni kam 'umri ke andar jo hai, aap jab aaye to saade pachchis saal ke the. Aur mansoob bana diye gaye.

Uss waqt Dawoodi'on mein yeh tha ke bhai ab Sulaimani'on mein koi aisa... sab budhe to khatam ho gaye, koi hai hi nahi. Kyunki us waqt chote, jawan choti thi, Hyderabad ki. Toh iske liye koi... aise aadmi ko mansoob banaya gaya. Aur aake, ab woh... upar aayat... ab aap jo hai jo kaam kiye, woh aisa kaam kiye, behas karne mein, usme, sab mein thaat thi. Toh karke, Dawoodi'on ke ek bade sheikh ko, Lukmani family, puri Lukmani family aa gayi. Sulemani bana diye. Lukmanji Lukmani jo... Bombay, Baroda, jahan jahan bhi hain, woh sab jo hai Sayyidna Ikram Ali Sahab ke zariye se, ke unhone jo hai... Fa-in hajjuka fa-qul aslamtu wajhiya lillahi wa manit-taba'an (So if they argue with you, say, "I have submitted my face to Allah, and [so has] whosoever follows me"). Phir jab woh aap se hujjat karein, toh keh dein... toh keh dene aur jo meri pairvi kare... karne walon ne Allah ke liye apna chehra jhuka diya. Haan. Ya'ni, wasi' ko qayam karne ke muta'alliq munafiqeen agar aap se behas karein, toh keh dijiye, maine, maine to istaba'... maine to rutba-e-wasayat, jo mera chehra hai, Ali ko saump diya. Ali ko saump diya. Main ya'ni, jidhar rukh hai woh Ali ki taraf ho gaya. Ab uske ma'na jo hai bahut door ke hain. Ke jo hai, hum jo hai... kidhar phirate hain? Ka'batullah ki taraf rukh karte hain namaz padhte waqt. Ali ki taraf phirayenge? Toh mamsul kaun hai Ka'batullah ke? Kaun paida hue Ka'be mein? Kaun Imam-e-wasi' the us zamane mein? Bahut samajhne ki baat hai. Ke woh Hajar-e-Aswad kya hai? Mizab-e-Rahmat kya hai? Aur jo hai jo rukn-e-Yamani hai, rukn-e-'Iraqi hai, woh kya kya matlab rakhte hain. Jo hajj ke dauran jo sab hote hain, usme bataya jaata hai. Toh baharhal yeh hai ke maine to apna chehra phira diya hai.

Ab chehra phira dene se hum jo hai, kya karte hain? Jab niyat lete hain, Allahumma inni nawaytu an usalliya salata... salatuz-zuhri, rak'atayni lillahi azza wa jalla, mustaqbilal-ka'batish-sharifati... Mustaqbilal-ka'batish-sharifati. Not to the Ka'bah, towards the Ka'bah. Not to the Ka'bah. Ka'be ke hum... Ka'bah to jo hai... Mahasabhai hain, aaj se takriban 80-90 saal pehle, Hyderabad mein behas ho gayi. Aur woh logon ne kaha ke bhai yeh kya musalman log kehte hain? Hum jaisa pooje to hamare ko bolte hain ke aap... mushrik hain ya falana hain, Hindu hain, falana hain. Woh log bhi to Hajar-e-Aswad ko choomte hain. Usko maante hain, pooja karte hain uski. Pooja to nahi karte hain, lekin bataye woh bhi. Ab koi jawab do. Toh bade bade musalman jo, Sunni, Shi'a waghera sab bade 'alimon ko jawab dene ke liye... wahan, Hyderabad ke ek jagah mein. Aur bole iska jawab diya jaye. Mahasabhai bahut sakht the us waqt. Ke bhai yeh... hamare samne jawab dijiye, ke aap hum jaisa pooja karte hain, aap bhi jo hai Hajar-e-Aswad ko choomte hain, kya farq hai? Phir aap Islam, Islam kya keh rahe hain? Uss waqt jo hai, koi jawab nahi diya na Sunni, na Shi'a, na woh. Ahmadiyya firqa jo hai, Qadiani hain, woh bade qabil rehte hain. Lekin woh firqe se hum jo hai us firqe se hata diye is waste ke unhone aakhri Nabi nahi maana paighambar ko. Warna woh aise qabil bahut rehte hain. Toh Qadiani, Ahmadiyya firqa mein se ek ne jawab diya, ke Hajar-e-Aswad ko nahi choomte, woh jagah ki qadr hai. Agar wahan se Hajar-e-Aswad nikal diya jaye, toh jo hai, hum Hajar-e-Aswad ko nahi choomenge, jagah ki ahmiyat hai.

Hajar-e-Aswad ko, patthar ko nahi choom rahe hain. Patthar ka bataya gaya hai ke jannat se laya gaya, aur woh shuruaat jab... Makke mein jo hai, shaffaf, barf ke jaisa tha. Lekin abhi kala hai. Hajar-e-Aswad. Aswad kehte hain kale ko. Toh log jo hai ta'wilan bayan kiye hain, ke yeh sab hamare gunah hain jo usme 'aks nazar aa rahe hain, hamare gunah. Kalapan jo aaya hai. Warna Hajar-e-Aswad jo hai, woh jannat se laya gaya tha. Woh sab uski tareef hai. Bahrhal, Sayyidna Mawlana Muhammad Sahab bahut sa bayan kiye hain. Chaliye yahan tak... Jiski ma'rifat mein 'aql-e-insani hairan o sargardan hai. Aur isi ma'na mein woh Allah hai. Aur mere baad aane wale a'immah bhi iske saamne sar jhukane... jhukaye hue hain. Ya'ni ta'at jhukaye. Ab yeh dekhiye, Ali ke saamne. Main musalsal do-teen roz se bayan kar raha hun ke jab jitne bhi jo hain Ali ko maanne wale hain, aur Ali ke a'immah hain, Imam-e-Hasan se leke 28ve Imam tak, woh tamam jo hai unke hijabat hain. Husain ko maanna hai, Ali ko. Hasan ko maanna hai. La hawla wa la quwwata illa billahil 'aliyyil 'azim. Toh yeh nahi hai ke a'immah bhi sar jhukaye hue hain. Sar jhukaye hue. Koi jo nahi, aur usse pehle kya hai? Ashhadu an la ilaha illallah, aur ashhadu anna Muhammadar rasulullah. Allah ki humne gawahi di, ya'ni musalman hone ke waqt mein, Allah ki gawahi, kalima-e-tayyibah, aur dusra jo hai, ashhadu anna Muhammadar rasulullah. Muhammad rasulullah. Toh sirf hum nahi de rahe hain, a'immah de rahe hain. Ke Muhammad rasulullah hai.

Toh Muhammad salawatullahi 'alayh ka darjah ek khaas hai. Ke unki gawahi dena jo hai zaroori hai. Allah ke saath saath. Jab tak paighambar ki gawahi nahi denge. Aur phir hamare lihaz se, Ali waliyullah. Yeh nahi kahe hain. Hum azan mein dete hi hain. Toh teeno ki gawahi denge. Toh Ali ke jaisa koi nahi hai. Jitne a'immah guzar rahe hain, woh bhi jo hai unke... zurriyat se hain. Ali ke beton mein hain. Husain ki zurriyat se hain. Husain ke beton mein, Husain ke baad jo hai Zainul Abidin aaye, Muhammad Baqir aaye, uske baad Ja'far Sadiq aaye, Isma'il aaye, Muhammad bin Isma'il aaye, 'Abdullah aaye, Ahmad aaye, ek ke baad ek chale aa rahe hain. Baap ke baad beta, aur walad min walad. Baap ke baad beta aur ek ke baad ek. Yeh toot hi nahi sakta silsila. Main musalsal jo hai bayan kar raha hun, aap logon ke zehan mein hoga. Wa lan tajida li-sunnatillahi tabdeela. Allah ki jo yeh sunnat hai, jismein koi... koi farq nahi aayega. Karodon barson guzar jayenge. Lekin Imam jo hai baap ke baad beta hi banega. Uske siwa nahi banega. Chahe qiyamat aa jaye, qiyamaton ke qiyamat aa jaye, lekin jab qiyamat ke baad phir jo hai dusra kashf aayega. Har din ke baad raat aati hai, raat ke baad... aap na din ko badal sakte hain, na raat ko badal sakte hain. Raat ke baad aapko malum hai ke cheh ghante ke baad suraj niklega. Aap usko change hi nahi kar sakte. Toh isi tareh se, Allah ki jo sunnat hai, kashf ke baad sitr aayega, sitr ke baad phir kashf aayega. Toh humko 'ilmi lihaz se, woh aankh hamari to hai nahi. Hum kab zinda rehte nahi rehte malum nahi. Lekin yeh yaqeen rakhein ke sitr jab khatam hoga toh kashf ka zamana aayega. Yeh da'wa hai, Allah taala ka. Aur kashf ke baad phir sitr aayega. Phir kashf ke baad phir sitr aayega. Yeh raat aur din ka chakkar jaisa dunya mein dekh rahe hain, aur iske upar main bayan kar chuka hun ke ashhadu anna deeni 'ala mithal-e-khalq. Mithal-e-khalq pe maine apni deen ka paya rakha hai. Din raat jaisa aap dekhte hain, isi tareh se yeh... jab suraj nikalta hai usko din kahenge, jab suraj doob jaata hai usko raat kahenge.

Ya'ni shams-e-ruhani. Jaisa paighambar ka zamana gaya toh phir Ali ka zamana shuru hua, qamar nikla. Toh yeh tamam technical jo hai ta'wilat hain, jo azbar rehna chahiye hamare ko, mu'minon ko. Take hamare namaz mein barkat paida ho. Nahi to kuch bhi nahi hai. Na aap shams ko samjhe, na... ash-shamsu wal-qamar bahut dafa aaye hain, aasman o zameen aaye hain, woh sab ke muta'alliq malum hona chahiye kya matlab hai. Toh yeh yaad rakhein ke deen mein koi baat jo hai laghw nahi hai. Har ek ke peeche ma'na rakhe gaye hain. Aur ma'na aapko jitne bataye gaye, usse andar bhi ma'na hai. Yeh parat ke neeche parat hai. Lekin jitni aapka samajh hai, utna bataya jaata hai upar wale ki taraf se aur jo hai... ab jitna padhenge aap, bade se bade kitabein likhe hain. Ek se ek haqa'iq jo hai, main agar Ali ke darjah agar bayan karun, to aap do ghante le lunga. Do ghante. Sirf Ali ke samjhane ke. Itna unka wus'at ka darjah ooncha hai. Kya 'alam mein aapka kya iftikhar hai, kya jo hai aapki... wasi' ka darjah hai. Sabir... ausiya hain aap. Aur jo hai kya kya hai? Qa'im ki... mein bhi Qa'im hain. Asas ke darje mein rehte hain. Daur ke Qa'im hain. Daur kisko kehte hain? Saat kashf aur saat sitr ko. Ek nahi hai, kai cheezon mein malumat hai.

Toh hum yaad rakhein ke yeh chakkar jab malum hua, toh aapke namaz waghera mein kya barkat paida hogi? Namaz ki barkat us waqt hoti hai jabke namaz ki ma'na ko samjhenge. Ke do raka'at kyun padh rahe hain, teen raka'at kyun padh rahe hain. Maghrib ke teen raka'at hote hain, Zuhr ke chaar raka'at hote hain. Kyun? Kuch to ma'ne honge peeche? Batao bolo. Yeh mukhalifeen hain hamare, Sunni'on ko batao bolo. Ke chaar raka'at kyun hote? Sab ma'ne bata chuka hun pichle... Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Al-Fatiha.

<!-- AUG:ibrahim-2015 lecture 15 -->

How many pages are left? It's ending. It seems there are more. Look ahead. At 51? Yes, 51. Yes, this is it. Where did this come from? It's ending. 180? It must be 180. It's 180. 181. It is 181. 181.

*Wa man yakfur bi-ayatiLlahi fa-inna 'Llaha sari'u 'l-hisab* (And whoever disbelieves in the verses of Allah, then indeed, Allah is swift in account). *La ilaha illa Allah*. Two hundred.

*Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim*. *Wa man yakfur bi-ayatiLlahi fa-inna 'Llaha sari'u 'l-hisab* (And whoever disbelieves in the verses of Allah, then indeed, Allah is swift in account). And whoever denies the verses of Allah, let him know that Allah is very swift in taking account. A bit louder. Speak a bit louder. Speak louder. Keep it in front. *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim*. *Wa man yakfur bi-ayatiLlahi fa-inna 'Llaha sari'u 'l-hisab* (And whoever disbelieves in the verses of Allah, then indeed, Allah is swift in account). And whoever denies the verses of Allah, let him know that Allah is very swift in taking account. Meaning, those who deny the stations of the Imams of the `din`, let them know that the `sabiq` Imams will take their account very soon and swiftly.

This was also explained yesterday. Those people who commit `kufr` (disbelief) in this way... what kind of `kufr` is it? It is also `shirk` (polytheism). `Shirk` in what way? It is not `shirk fi 'Llah` (polytheism in God), but rather `shirk` in the station of Maulana 'Ali. They commit `shirk`. They are called `kafir` (disbelievers). The `mushrik` (polytheist) is one who does not believe in Allah or the `Hujjats`. But the `kafir` is one who denies 'Ali. So, I have mentioned before that the Prophet fought against the `mushrikin`. All the battles that took place—`Khandaq`, `Khaybar`, `Uhud`, `Badr`, etc.—in all of them, the Prophet fought against the `mushrikin`. But the three battles of Maulana 'Ali—`Nahrawan`, `Siffin`, and `Jamal`—all of these were against the `kafirin`. They became disbelievers after coming into Islam.

So, the Imams of truth, meaning they deny the stations of the `din`. Let them know that the `sabiq` Imams, meaning Maulana Qa'im `salawatuLlahi 'alayh`, when he comes, the accounting will be very swift.

*Fa-in hajjuka fa-qul aslamtu wajhiya liLlahi*. What does this mean, to take account swiftly? It must be swift, he is the final `Natiq`. But what does it mean? Swift accounting means that those people who sinned 1400 years ago, who committed `kufr`, what is their state now? 1400 years have passed, what happened? They have been "pelted" (`palta diya gaya`), meaning they are undergoing the lesser punishment (`'adhab-e-adna`). And when the time of Janab-e-Qa'im comes, then it will be the matter of the `Sakhrah` punishment. So now, what does it mean? That Hazrat, you know, don't you? The `darke` (levels of hell/punishment) are seven: `Raws`, `Khunnas`, `Khunnar`, `Raqs`, `Waqs`, `'Aqs`, and `Naqs`. There are seven such `darke`. One is of human form, four are of animals, one is of birds, and one is of plants (`nabat`), and the last one is of minerals (`ma'dan`). They are trapped in this `'adhab-e-adna`. And now when Janab-e-Qa'im comes, then whoever's account is such that they are undergoing the `atraf` (peripheral) punishment, they have been cast into it. When was that? People who sinned at the time of Adam (`'alayhi 's-salam`), at the time of Nuh (`'alayhi 's-salam`)—the incident of Nuh's ark—at the time of Ibrahim (`'alayhi 's-salam`). How will three, four thousand years pass? So they will also have to suffer the `atraf` punishment. And whoever suffers the `atraf` punishment, he will then receive the `Sakhrah` punishment. Now, what is its system? The system is such that if the sentence from Allah is five `darke`, if the sentence is, then five `darke` will they go through, and then they will be "pelted." Depending on their sins. If there are small mistakes, they are the `awarin` (common folk) who just went along with the crowd, or are like that, then they will go through four or five `darke`. Four are of animals. They will go into them, and then they will return in a `halal` form. In whichever `darke` they go, they will all go in `haram` (forbidden) forms. Remember this, the descending form can never be `halal`. And all the ascending ones, the `halal` animals we have—the she-camel, the cow, the goat, whatever it is—all of them are in an ascending state, in a `halal` form. Now, they are being rapidly changed chemically, plastic is being made, this is happening, many things of the world are happening. Why is this happening? Because the accounting must be swift. So, those who were "pelted" will now be brought into `halal` forms, and then the `da'wah` (call) will be presented to them. The `da'wah` of Allah is that the guide will come. If they accept, it's fine; otherwise, they will go again. And after the sixth, whoever is on the seventh, after the sixth, the seventh is the last `darkah`, that of minerals. So, whoever goes into that will also have to suffer the `atraf` punishment. And the `atraf` punishment is for 5000, 10,000, 7,000 years, like that. Meaning, it is not a snowy one. The `azabat` (punishments) are of two types: one is the heat of fire, and the other is the cold of ice. So, a human being cannot bear the cold of ice. The snowy regions have people... there are those in human form, and also in the form of animals. Whales, sharks, seahorses, penguins... all of them are suffering. And if they were given a voice, they would say, "What a calamity we are in!" You are happy seeing them, but what are we? We are suffering punishment. So, whoever suffers the `atraf` punishment will then receive the punishment of `Sakhrah`. And the punishment of `Sakhrah` is for when Janab-e-Qa'im comes. This punishment they have to suffer because they are the ones who denied. So, it has come in other books, in the narrations of Imam Ja'far al-Sadiq, that they are in such states.

*Fa-in hajjuka fa-qul aslamtu wajhiya liLlahi wa-man... ittaba'an*. Then, if they argue with you, say, "I and my followers have bowed our faces to Allah."
