Yes, now this is an address to the Prophet, that my followers... I am telling you that I have bowed my face for Allah. But what is the meaning of `wajh` (face)? What is the meaning of `ta'wil` (interpretation)? It starts with `ya'ni` (meaning). Meaning, if the hypocrites argue with you regarding establishing the `Wasi` (successor), then say, "I have entrusted the rank of `Wasayat` (successorship), which is my face, to 'Ali. In whose `ma'rifat` (gnosis) the human intellects are perplexed and wandering. And in this sense, he is Allah. And the Imams coming after me are also bowing their heads before him. Meaning, they are obedient."

Obedient to whom? To Maulana 'Ali. Now, in one respect, if we gain more knowledge from this, about what the matter of `hijabat` (veils) is, what is the status of Maulana 'Ali in front of the `A'imma Tahirin`... So, there are many details, many things. That his `nasut-tasawwur` is equal to the `nasut-taqas` of others. But right now, from the perspective of a `mu'min`, all the Imams from Maulana Hasan until here are the `hijabat` (veils) of Maulana 'Ali (`salawatuLlahi 'alayh`). So, 'Ali has a station, a very high thing, and he is the `juzwi qa'im` (partial `Qa'im`). He is called the `Asas-e-Awwal` (First Foundation). The `Asas-thani` (Second Foundation) is Janab-e-Qa'im. These are all ranks that are on different levels, and they have their meanings. So now, the `wajh` (face) is, "I have turned my face." And we say, `mustaqbil al-ka'bat ash-sharif al-haram`. What does this mean? We prostrate `towards` the Ka'bah, not `to` the Ka'bah. We do not prostrate to the Ka'bah. `Towards` the Ka'bah. Wherever we are, in India or anywhere, our direction is towards the west for us in Hindustan. We do that. From other places, it is different. The point is `mustaqbil al-ka'bat ash-sharif`. In a certain era, here in Mhasara... I mentioned it the day before yesterday... a gathering took place, 80-90 years ago. And people there asked, "You object to us, saying we worship idols and do this and that. But you do it too. You are worshiping a stone. You kiss the `Hajar al-Aswad` (Black Stone) and you kiss it with such passion." At that time, great `ashab` (companions/scholars) were called. `Ashab` means Sunni scholars, Shi'a... people from all sects were called. No one could answer, neither the Sunni nor the Shi'a. The `Ahmadiyya` sect, the `Qadiani`, got up and answered. And he said that the place has sanctity. If the `Hajar al-Aswad` is moved and placed in another place, it will not have it. Because you know that for about 150 years, the `Hajar al-Aswad` was removed from there. The `Qaramita` took it away. How it was at that time, I don't know, but the stone was gone. And when it returned, it came back in pieces. That is why a `cassette` (casing) was made there, and the pieces were stuck into it.

*Wa qul li 'lladhina utu 'l-kitaba wa 'l-ummiyyina a-aslamtum. Fa-in aslamu faqadi 'htadaw. Wa-in tawallaw fa-innama 'alayka 'l-balagh. Wa 'Llahu basirun bi 'l-'ibad* (And say to those who were given the Scripture and to the unlearned, "Have you submitted?" And if they submit, then they are rightly guided; but if they turn away, then upon you is only the [duty of] notification. And Allah is Seeing of [His] servants). Ask the People of the Book and the `Ummiyyun` (i.e., people of Makkah), "Have you accepted Islam?" If they accept Islam, they will be guided. And if they turn away, then upon you is only to convey the message. And Allah is watchful of His servants. Meaning, ask the `kalima-go` Muslims and those who claim `imamat`, the `Ummiyyun`, "Have you accepted the station of `wasayat`? If you know, you will be guided. If you turn away from obedience, then listen, my job is only to convey the truth (`tabligh-e-haqq`). And the `Wasi` himself..." Meaning, the Prophet's job is only to convey. Just as the `ayat` came: `Ya ayyuha 'r-rasulu balligh ma unzila ilayk... wa-in lam taf'al fama ballaghta risalatah` (O Messenger, announce that which has been revealed to you... And if you do not, then you have not conveyed His message). "If you do not do this, your prophethood will be at stake." So, remember that the Prophet's rank is of `tanzil` (revelation) and `wa'z` (preaching), and Janab-e-'Ali's (`salawatuLlahi 'alayh`) is of `ta'wil` (interpretation). So, `ta'wil` and `tanzil` are the two parts of the `din` (religion). And just as we now recite the `du'a` in the morning and evening, you cannot show any verse of the Holy Qur'an where the Prophet himself has interpreted it, except in the Hadith Mubarak. The rest, the one who interprets is Maulana 'Ali. And after him, the Imam who is given the authority. There are very high-ranking Imams, Maulana Baqir, Maulana Ja'far al-Sadiq, from whom our entire `Da'im al-Islam` is filled. The entire `Da'im al-Islam` is filled with narrations from these two.

Yes. `Tafsir` (exegesis)... No.

*Inna 'lladhina yakfuruna bi-ayati 'Llahi wa-yaqtuluna 'n-nabiyyina bi-ghayri haqqin wa-yaqtuluna 'lladhina ya'muruna bi 'l-qisti mina 'n-nasi fa-bashshirhum bi-'adhabin alim* (Indeed, those who disbelieve in the verses of Allah and kill the prophets without right and kill those who order justice from among the people - give them tidings of a painful punishment). Indeed, those who disbelieve in the verses of Allah and kill the prophets without right and kill those from among the people who command justice, then you give them tidings of a painful punishment. Meaning, those who deny the `imamat` of the `Wasi` despite clear and manifest proofs of its validity, and who try to erase the stations of the people of ranks (`ahl-e-maratib`) without any special reason, and also want to eliminate those who call towards the `wilayat` of 'Ali, which is the true justice (`'adl-e-haqiqi`), then inform them that the `Wasi` will sever ties with them, and it will be heavy for them.

Yes, this is a serious matter. That 'Ali will turn his face away. 'Ali will move away from there. Meaning, the `ta'yidat` (divine supports) will stop. Now, all the `ta'yidat` that we think are coming from above... from where are they coming? Here in this world, it is from the `Wasi-e-Payghambar`, the person of Janab-e-'Ali, that the blessing (`barkat`) comes. The Imam of every age is his `hijab` (veil), in a sense. All of them. And then from there, it comes to the `Huda'i`, then it comes down, it comes down. Filtering down, whatever is our `nasib` (share), `an-nasibu yusib`, whatever is our share, we receive it.

*Ula'ika 'lladhina habitat a'maluhum fi 'd-dunya wa 'l-akhirati wa ma lahum min nasirin* (Those are the ones whose deeds have become worthless in this world and the Hereafter, and for them there are no helpers). These are the ones whose deeds have been dropped in this world and the hereafter. And they have no helper. Meaning, their deeds have no value, neither in the previous `millat` (community/era) nor in this second `millat`. And there is no authoritative `hujjat` (`hujjat-e-qati'a`) with them to support and help them.

The previous `millat` and the later... what is `millat`? The previous `millat`. What it means is the system of `rikr` (return), the `karrati` system. From Adam (`'alayhi 's-salam`) until now. We have been told that a `mu'min` will be "pelted" every thousand years, he will come again. And he will be counted in another `karrah` (cycle). In a thousand years. How is a thousand years calculated? 280 years in the grave (`qabr`) and 720 years in the `buruj` (celestial spheres). Like this. So, whether one is in the grave or in the state of minerals, plants, or animals, these two combined make 280 or 300 years, and the upper part takes 720 years. This becomes 1000 years. Now, after 1000 years, they will come. So we are... how many? Seven thousand years have passed, seven times we have undergone `rikr`. Where it happened, where it was, we have no idea. Nor do we need to know. We just have to fulfill what is applicable in the `Shari'at-e-Muhammadi`, that's it, it's over.

`Habitat a'maluhum`. Their deeds have been dropped. `Habitat` means they have been dropped. These are the ones whose deeds in this world and the hereafter have been dropped. Dropped means they are nullified. It's over. And they have no helper. Meaning, their deeds have no value, neither in the previous `millat` nor in this one. All of them—the Jews, the Christians, all of them—in their time, for their respective `Natiq`... the Christians at the time of 'Isa, who became Christians, they were correct because 'Isa (`'alayhi 's-salam`) was a `tasdiq` (confirmer) that he was a prophet of Allah. But today's Christians are not correct. Ever since the Prophet came. The era of Musa came, and after Musa, 'Isa came. So 'Isa abrogated the previous `shari'at`, Musa's. Then he established his own `shari'at`. So, every `Natiq` that comes is also a `nasikh` (abrogator), except for Adam. Now, the Prophet who has come has abrogated all the `shari'ats`. And established his own `shari'at`. So remember that after the Prophet has come, no other `shari'at`'s system will work. They are all wrong. In their time, the Jews were also fine, they were of the `ahl-e-kitab`. They believed in Musa, 'Isa, the Prophet, Ibrahim. They believed in all of them. "Neither in the previous `millat` nor in the later one." And in this second `millat`. And there is no authoritative `hujjat` with them to support and help them. And to support them, there is no... there is no excuse for them.

*Alam tara ila 'lladhina utu nasiban mina 'l-kitabi yud'awna ila kitabi 'Llahi li-yahkuma baynahum thumma yatawalla fariqun minhum wa hum mu'ridun* (Have you not seen those who were given a portion of the Scripture? They are invited to the Book of Allah that it should judge between them; then a party of them turns away, and they are refusing). Have you not seen those people who were given a portion of the Book, meaning the Torah? Then they are invited to this Book of Allah so that it may judge between them. Then a group among them turns away and refuses. Meaning, those who have obtained apparent knowledge of `fiqh` from the `shari'at` of the `Natiq`, when they are called to the obedience of the `Wasi`, saying, "Come, he will resolve your doubts and suspicions..." In the time of Musa, you see, in the incidents of the Pharaoh, Musa was there, but people would turn to Harun. Harun was the `Wasi`. His... The whole world believes in the Prophet. How many believe in 'Ali? That is the real thing. The circle of becoming a `mu'min`, the circle of `iman` starts from Ghadir Khumm. Although there were `mu'min`s before that, but only a few, a handful. The great ones... who were intelligent, or let's say who had insight. They... Abu Dharr al-Ghifari, 'Ammar ibn Yasir, all of them used to acquire knowledge from Janab-e-'Ali. But after that, all those who... there are `tabi'in`... for them, it's different. A Sahabi is different, a Tabi'i is different, a Taba' Tabi'i is different. There are various... So, the one who saw the Prophet in a state of Islam... they say in a state of `iman`. The one who saw him in a state of `iman` is a Sahabi. They say "state of `iman`" because Abu Jahl and Abu Lahab also saw the Prophet. But they were not in a state of `iman`. That is their statement. In our view, it is that whoever believed had come into Islam. `Iman` had not started then. The circle of `iman` starts from Ghadir Khumm. So when one has to believe in the `Wasi`, one has to believe in the `tanzil`, no, in the `ta'wil`. People believe in the `tanzil`. In the morning, the discussion was about this. Why? Because the day before yesterday's discussion was that the Prophet said... that after me, who will fight? Against the `mushrikin` or, say, the `kafirin`. "Who will fight?" Who will do it? He asked. So, among the companions, all three were there. Abu Bakr said, "I will do it. I, I, O Messenger of Allah." You remained silent. Then Janab-e-'Umar said, "I will do it." No... this was in the morning. A third time... he asked a third companion... The third companion said, "No..." So, you, the Prophet, said, "He will do it who is mending the shoe." And at that time, in Janab-e-'Ali's hands was a shoe. He was the divider of the Fire. They became worried. So, such indications were given by the Prophet many times. But if they were not to believe, they did not believe. And this was... years ago, about 50 years ago. Meaning, I'm talking about '56, '54-'55. At that time, I used to study with Mun Abbas Sahab. In the morning... So at that time, he had started these discussions. It was in Gulistan-e-Ma'ruf, and this... At that time, I was thinking, this is... Abu Bakr and 'Umar are such great companions, but they don't believe, and... And how will their punishments be? At that time, as my age demanded... I said, "Ibrahim, these people have gone down two or three times and come back." This is the word of the `Basa'ir`. "And it is a very big one." He said, "`Sakhrah`... after going into `Sakhrah`, to come back... what is this called? 16,000 crore years. 16,000 crore." He said, "They have gone two or three times and come back." Still the same. This is the hardness in them. So at that time, it was explained that we don't take names much, but wherever the word `Iblis` comes, understand it to be the first two Caliphs. And where `Shaytan` comes, it is the others. Because `Shaytan` is more evil than `Iblis`. `Shaytan` misleads. `Iblis` just raises his hands. "I don't believe." And what does he do? `Iblis` means `ablasa min rahmati 'Llah` (he despaired of the mercy of Allah). He is despondent of Allah's mercy. But their evil remains. Their `shaytaniyat` (satanism) is great.

...he will resolve your doubts and suspicions and give the right verdict, then they are not ready to benefit from him. They turn their faces away and refuse obedience.

*Dhalika bi-annahum qalu lan tamassana 'n-naru illa ayyaman ma'dudatin wa-gharrahum fi dinihim ma kanu yaftarun* (That is because they say, "Never will the Fire touch us, except for a few numbered days," and they were deluded in their religion by what they used to invent). The reason for this is that they said... that the Fire will not touch us except for a few counted days. And the falsehood that they used to speak... `Wa-gharrahum fi dinihim`. And their disobedience is because they are deceived in their religion. Meaning, their ख्याल is that the trouble from the heat of doubts and suspicions will only last until a few specific Imams. Then the falsehood and slander they have built up will be erected, and they will collect so much material against the `Wasi` that they won't even realize they are on the wrong path. We will put this blame on the Messenger... This is what's happening in the world. The `tafasir` (commentaries) are filled. Ghazali is saying one thing, Razi is another, then all these... Bukhari... all of them have created a `tumar` (a large volume of writing). And they have confused the common public so much that they have no ability to understand. Their insight is gone. This was mentioned in a previous `ayat` about 'Abd al-Muttalib's son, Abu Lahab. When they used to study with their father, they were fine. After that, they changed. The `ayat` that came, which we recite in the field of Karbala, that Islam is a garden, and it was a green and flourishing garden. But people turned it into a desolate wasteland. These verses... the gardener of the garden becomes old. Weakness overcomes him. And the Prophet became ill. At the age of 63, when he made the declaration, after that declaration, his health deteriorated. And he was in a state of semi-consciousness. He reached Madinah and lived for three months, but his condition had changed. At that time, they said, "The Prophet is speaking nonsense (`hathyan`), `na'udhu billah min dhalik`." And he is delirious. These are the sentences of the second Caliph. "Don't give him pen and paper." He will write something. He will declare something. He had already declared 'Ali, he came after that. But they did not want the public to know. Even today, the `maulawis` who have insight, they know what the truth is. But in front of the public, they have to present something. They are doing wrong. And this is their livelihood. So what it means is that the layman, the `layman`, he is the one who is suffering. He has no knowledge, he was not told. The common Muslim people have not studied, know nothing. They are just following the beaten path. But whose punishment will it be? Those who are their leaders. So, it has also come about them that they are their `anfus` (souls). Their `nufus` (souls) are the ones who are misleading them. So, all the leaders are like this. You see that in the Umayyad era, what happened? In the Abbasid era, what happened? How many difficulties came, how many... And in that era, the system that was our `din`'s, when `taqiyyah` was practiced. Be quiet, sit down. Now, now is not the time for governance. There is no command. A `tumar` will be erected, and so much material will be gathered against the `Wasi` that they won't even realize they are on the wrong path. We will put this blame on the Messenger that you said nothing about the `Wasi`. Nothing. And we will make so many indications that the entire incident will be... under our slander... it will be buried. Yes. Now, they say something else. That what happened on the 18th? They say it was Hujjat al-Wada'. The Prophet performed his last Hajj, so he gave a sermon. It was Hujjat al-Wada'. They won't say it was Ghadir Khumm. A few people know, who are... So this is how history is, that history is a... I have written somewhere that history is a... it is such a thing that it takes whatever form you give it. It is like a vine. If you put it on a pillar, it will become like a pillar. If you put it on a wall, it will become like a wall. History has been manipulated. It happened during the Mughal era, it happened during the British era, and it happened during the Hindu era. Whatever they want, you... They are silent... and they did not understand. And they will suppress the incident of Ghadir Khumm by making slanders.

*Fa-kayfa idha jama'nahum li-yawmin la rayba fihi wa-wuffiyat kullu nafsin ma kasabat wa hum la yuzlamun* (So how will it be when We assemble them for a Day about which there is no doubt, and every soul will be recompensed for what it earned, and they will not be wronged?). Then what will their condition be when we gather them on that day whose coming has no doubt, and every soul will be given the full recompense for what it has earned, and no injustice will be done to them. Meaning, after all this slander, on that day whose coming has no doubt, meaning at the time of the `zuhur` (appearance) of the `Sahib` (`'alayhi 's-salam`), we will gather everyone, then what face will they show? And what answer will they give? At the time of Janab-e-Qa'im, every Imam will bring his army. Those who were his followers in his time, he will bring all of them. Janab-e-Qa'im will give the order. And they will all come. One Imam will bring his people. So that is a different day, what the field of `hashr` (gathering) will be, how it will be, only Allah knows. In any case, the point is that truth has always been suppressed. Truth has been... great efforts have been made to suppress it. Read history, and you will see the incidents of Janab-e-Ibrahim, see those of Janab-e-Musa, see... Every prophet, in his time, this is what happened. That every prophet was rejected, and he was tormented. And the Prophet says that every prophet has a `Shaytan`. For me, there are two `Shaytans`. Abu Jahl and Abu Lahab. And for 'Ali, there are three. So, the greater the person, the greater his `zidd` (opposite) is. Remember this. A small one never comes. All the great prophets who have passed, their `zidd` are also particularly significant. In any case, the foundation of our `din` is strong. The way we are proceeding, may Allah keep our faith strong so that this boat of Sulaymani reaches its destination until the end. And may Allah make it reach there. The day of Qiyamah...

*Wa 'l-hamdu liLlahi rabbi 'l-'alamin, wa 's-salatu wa 's-salamu*. In the name of Allah... To Sayyidina Muhammad and his progeny, the pure ones, the infallible ones, the `du'at`, the pure ones, and the `hujjat`, the pure ones, and all the `mu'minin` and `mu'minat`, `salawatuLlahi 'alayhim ajma'in`. Al-Fatiha.

Al-Fatiha.

<!-- AUG:ibrahim-2015 lecture 16 -->

My brother, see here. An eleventh-grade [student] is here. He has come till the end. Is he here? It is good, take this. The one who came earlier, brother. The one of the *ghazal*. Ha, the one on top of *Lillah al-Hamd al-Tahiyya*. *Lillah al-Hamd*. What page number? Fifty-one. Yes. *Lillah al-Hamd*. This is with the one with the *ghazal*. Correct. *Lillah al-Hamd al-Matin*. Correct. This one. It is not this. He is asking for this. Yes.

*Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Hidayah* (guidance) of *Imam al-Muttaqin* (the Imam of the pious), *Qa'id al-Ghurr al-Muhajjilin* (the leader of the bright-faced honorable ones), Maulana Ali ibn Abi Talib, *salawatullah 'alayh*, and from Allah the Exalted is the bestowal of success. To the purified progeny, to the righteous companions, to the successors, to the close ones. From the *hidayah* (guidance) of Sayyidna Husayn bin Maulana Muhammad... *salawatullah 'alayh wa 'ala abaa'ihit tahirin wa abnaa'ihil akramin*. *Al-Fatiha*. Recite *Surah al-Hamd* ten times.

*Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Dhaalika bi-annahum qaaluu lan tamassana an-naaru illaa ayyaaman ma'duudaatin wa-gharrahum fii diinihim maa kaanuu yaftaruun* (That is because they said, "Never will the Fire touch us, except for a few numbered days," and they were deluded in their religion by what they used to invent). Speak louder. *Dhaalika bi-annahum qaaluu lan tamassana an-naaru illaa ayyaaman ma'duudaatin wa-gharrahum fii diinihim maa kaanuu yaftaruun* (That is because they said, "Never will the Fire touch us, except for a few numbered days," and they were deluded in their religion by what they used to invent). The reason for this is that they said the fire will not touch us except for a few numbered days, and the falsehood they used to speak has deceived them in their religion. Meaning, their notion is that the trouble they are facing due to doubts and suspicions will only last for a few specific Imams. Then they will raise such a volume of lies and slander and forge such a quantity of material against the *wasi* (successor) that the thought will not even cross their minds that they are in error. We will levy this accusation against the Messenger that you said nothing regarding the *wasi*—you said nothing at all—and we will propagate it so much that the entire event of Ghadir Khumm will be buried under this slander of ours.

*Fa-kayfa idhaa jama'naahum li-yawmin laa rayba fiihi wa-wuffiyat kullu nafsin maa kasabat wa-hum laa yuzlamuun* (So how will it be when We gather them for a Day about which there is no doubt, and every soul will be compensated for what it earned, and they will not be wronged?). Then, what will their state be when We gather them on a day about whose coming there is no doubt, and every soul will be given its full recompense, and they will not be wronged? Meaning, after all this slander, when We finally gather them all on that day whose coming is certain—that is, at the time of the appearance of the *Sahib 'alayhi 's-salam* (the Master, peace be upon him)—then what face will they show? And what answer will they give? On that day, every soul will receive the full reward for its service to the *da'wah* (mission) or the punishment for its enmity towards the *wasi*—not less, not more. The *Sahib* will not wrong anyone.

This explanation is continuing that the doubts and suspicions that were created in Islam in that era... and the doubt was brought regarding the *wasayyat* (successorship) of Maulana Ali, *salawatullah 'alayh*. Everyone accepted the Prophet, and in Islam, a very large community came together. But when the investiture was made upon Ali, at that time the differences increased. And as has been stated, doubts were created among them. Doubt is a bad thing. In a state of doubt, there is no prayer (*namaz*), no alms (*zakat*), no fast (*roza*); there is nothing in doubt. You know that the *mansul* (symbolized meaning) of doubt is blood. The blood that comes out of our body—if a wound occurs or something happens—then doubt is meant by it. The meaning is this: just as water is for knowledge, so blood is a sign of doubt. That is why if blood equal to the size of one rupee comes out, you have to perform ablution again. So the coming out of blood... now, its other meanings are also there. If I state them, it would be good. The four humors that we have... in the human body, there are four humors: *sawda* (black bile), *safra* (yellow bile), blood, and *balgham* (phlegm). A human is composed of and has been created from these four humors. So there are various things. As you heard in yesterday's sermon, there is flesh, skin, and all the things... bones... all the parts of the body are there. But besides that, these humors do a great job. The temperaments that are formed in us are formed from these humors. So in it, there is *sawda*, *safra*, blood, and *balgham*. Phlegm, its sign is white. If it turns from white to yellow, you become ill. As for blood, blood is a sign of doubt. If it increases, one is sick; if it decreases, one is sick; if it becomes white, one is sick; if it becomes thin, one is sick; if it becomes thick, one is sick. This blood is the worst of things. Our physical life derives strength from it, but as long as it is inside the body, it gives strength. But when it comes out, anger comes from it. It is what goes to the brain and creates anger, and one gets agitated, and blood pressure rises. All these things are signs of blood. The other two things are *sawda* and *safra*. *Safra*, you know, when one vomits, the yellow substance that comes out, that is also a sign. Now, this is a visible thing, but its *mansul* (symbolized meaning) is that *sawda* and *safra*... both are the daughter of 'Umar and the daughter of Abu Bakr—'A'isha and *Safra*... this is Hafsa, these two are its *mansul*. And 'Abdullah bin 'Umar is of phlegm, and of blood is 'Abdullah... this... the second Caliph, that is 'Umar ibn al-Khattab. So remember that from these humors, human temperaments are formed. From this, agitation comes, from this... all the illnesses you have, if you read the books, you will find out, they are all signs of this. Here it has been stated that those who fell into doubt, such a community that used to believe in the Prophet, but as soon as the matter of Ali's successorship came, they immediately changed. So the doubts that occurred, that is a sign of doubt. How did this come about? When we were falling from the pre-eternal world, at that time, different people fell in three... three batches. And who are they? The *Nadhirin* (warners), the *Shakkin* (doubters), and the *Musirr wa Mustakbir* (the stubborn and arrogant). So in the *Shakkin*, that doubt was created there, and after coming here too, in the world, the chosen personalities, they did not believe in them. And that is why doubt was created.

*Qulillahumma malik al-mulki tu'til-mulka man tashaa'u wa tanzi'ul-mulka mimman tashaa'u wa tu'izzu man tashaa'u wa tudhillu man tashaa'u bi-yadikal-khayr innaka 'alaa kulli shay'in qadiir* (Say, "O Allah, Owner of Sovereignty, You give sovereignty to whom You will and You take sovereignty away from whom You will. You honor whom You will and You humble whom You will. In Your hand is [all] good. Indeed, You are over all things competent"). Now this is its translation. This is a verse from the Noble Quran, *Qulillahumma malik al-mulk*. Look at its translation. Say: O Allah! O Owner of the Kingdom! You give the kingdom to whomever You wish, and you take the kingdom from whomever You wish. And You give honor to whomever You wish, and You humiliate whomever You wish. Authority is in Your hand. You alone are capable of all things. Meaning, tell those who have accepted Islam that Allahumma—that is, Ali, in whose recognition human intellects are perplexed and bewildered—is the master of the true *da'wah* (mission) and the world of religion. He gives a share in the *da'wah* to whomever he wishes, and from whomever he wishes, he snatches away that permission. And whomever's rank and status he wishes to elevate, he does so. And if he wishes to bring someone down, he brings them down. In your hand of power... in your grasp of power is the esoteric knowledge. You alone... upon the apparent and esoteric أمر... you alone are capable over the apparent and esoteric matter. Wherever the word Allah has come, it is Maulana Ali, *salawatullah 'alayh*. This is the *ta'vil* (interpretation). It is the name of Maulana Ali, *salawatullah 'alayh*. And it is you who raises someone and... as many people who have fallen... you see, from ancient times, but we will talk about the Muhammadan era, as many people who were degraded at that time or were deprived of religion, what is it? They are now to suffer the punishment. Their time has come. 1400 years ago, they opposed. Whoever opposed has been receiving punishment. When will they get it? This is not in their hands, but the *Qa'im al-Qiyamah* (the one who establishes the Resurrection) will come, and at that time, there will be punishment and reward. He alone will give the punishment, and he alone will give the reward. And the reward will be given to those who serve the *da'wah*. And punishment to those who fell into doubt and did not accept Maulana Ali. They openly said that we do not accept. They do not accept. So this, the period of caliphate that passed, the period of 30 years that passed, the caliphate of Abu Bakr, 'Umar, and 'Uthman's caliphate, these 30 years that passed with Maulana Ali's silence, that very silence became our 30 fasts. That we too, from eating and drinking and from intercourse, refrain during these thirty... thirty nights. So these 30 days have been stated as the *mansul* (symbolic representation). It is the *ta'vil*. Its deeper realities (*haqa'iq*) are something else. I have stated them in the sermon. Now is not the opportune time.

*Tuulijul layla fin nahaari wa tuulijun nahaara fil layl, wa tukhrijul hayya minal mayyiti wa tukhrijul mayyita minal hayy, wa tarzuqu man tashaa'u bi ghayri hisaab* (You cause the night to enter the day, and You cause the day to enter the night; and You bring the living out of the dead, and You bring the dead out of the living. And You give provision to whom You will without account). O Allah, You are the one who makes the night enter the day, and makes the day enter the night. You are the one who brings out the living from the dead, and the dead from the living. And You... whom You wish... You give sustenance without account. Meaning, the Master of the Successorships himself establishes the distinction between the believer and the hypocrite. And in the same way, he separates the hypocrite from the believer. He proves the apparent by the esoteric and proves the esoteric with the help of the apparent. And You, whom You wish, by Your grace and generosity, grant much more than their entitlement. Your generosity is vast and limitless.

*Laa yattakhizil mu'minuunal kaafiriina awliyaa'a min duunil mu'miniina wa man yaf'al dhaalika fa laysa minallaahi fii shay'in illaa an tattaquu minhum tuqaatan wa yuhadhdhirukumullaahu nafsahu wa ilallaahil masiir* (Let not believers take disbelievers as allies rather than believers. And whoever does that has nothing with Allah, except when taking precaution against them in prudence. And Allah warns you of Himself, and to Allah is the [final] destination). Believers should not make friends with disbelievers instead of believers. And whoever does so, he will have no connection with Allah, except that you fear them, practice *taqiyyah* (precautionary dissimulation). Allah warns you about Himself, and the return is towards Allah. Meaning, the followers of the *wasi* should not maintain friendship with his enemies. If they befriend them, then they will have no connection or attachment with the *wasi 'alayhi 's-salam*, except that in the time of *taqiyyah*, in order to save themselves from their oppression, they should practice forbearance.

See, this often happens, that the time of *taqiyyah* is a different matter. Otherwise, turning one's face away from Ali is to ruin one's own life. If the word *bara'at* (disassociation) comes up, that one should declare disassociation from Ali, then one should never agree to it, even if one's neck is to be cut. The meaning is this, that the order to curse Ali, which happened in the era of the Umayyads, that get on the pulpit and curse Ali, curse the progeny of Ali. So now, if they have the government and they have power or strength, then it is better to do it at that time. But if they say to sever the relationship with Ali, then one should never agree. This has been specifically stated, that disassociation from Maulana Ali, *salawatullah 'alayh*, then our system of *karra* (return) gets disrupted. We are left good for nothing. And hypocrisy is a bad thing. Before this, an verse about hypocrites has come, what are its literal meanings, who is called a hypocrite. And *yukhrijul hayya minal mayyit, wa yukhrijul mayyita minal hayy* (He brings forth the living from the dead and brings forth the dead from the living). From the good, bad ones are born, and from the bad, good ones. Now look, Maulana 'Abd al-Muttalib, *salawatullah 'alayh*, who is a great being, Maulana Ali's... after him, his rank is very high. He is in the palace of the *jadd* (grandfather), and he is your grandfather, and besides that, you have great perfections. Your son is Abu Lahab. So from the good, bad ones are born. And some are such that... like Hasan bin 'Abd al-Malik, whose father was a Da'udi, and the son, he reached the rank of a *da'i*, Sulayman Jamal. Besides this, there are many, many people. There is Bilal. Look at Bilal, from where did he come, from Abyssinia, and became the beloved of the Prophet and the *mu'adhdhin* (caller to prayer). There are many examples of this kind, that *yukhrijul hayya minal mayyit*. It is not that a living person is brought out from a dead one... this is all the work of Allah, that He brings out a living from a dead one and a dead from a living one. But its meaning is that from one who is ignorant, a knowledgeable person is born. One who holds the value of religion is born. And from one who is a good scholar, bad ones are born in their sons. And he is finished, the lineage is finished. So remember this, that hypocrisy is more evil. And listen to one more thing, that is, the polytheist (*mushrik*), the hypocrite (*munafiq*), and the disbeliever (*kafir*), these three are with us... they are... that are taken for evil. So, the polytheist is one who does not believe in Allah at all. And for them, we say *musirr wa mustakbir* (stubborn and arrogant), and those who absolutely do not believe, the atheists, all these things, they are the opponents, the polytheists. The second is the one... who is a hypocrite. A hypocrite is one who has one thing in the heart and another thing on the tongue. It is not a straightforward matter. That is called hypocrisy. Now, its *mansul* (symbolized representation)... this is the *mansul*. What is its apparent form? The commands of religion that are for us, the commands of the Prophet's Shari'ah, to offer prayer, to perform *ruku'*, all have their *mansulat*. What is the meaning of *ruku'*? Why are there two *sajdahs*? Why is there one *ruku'*? All have meanings. If you read, you will find out. And besides that, along with this, the breaking of ablution (*wudu'*)... if you go to the bathroom, and urination and defecation occurs, your ablution breaks, and it is evident that you are going to the bathroom, so people know that this happens. So this is a sign of polytheism (*shirk*) and disbelief (*kufr*). The front part is polytheism, which is the greatest evil in the world. Polytheism, not believing in Allah, is the greatest... against monotheism... that is, polytheism is the worst thing and the greatest sin. Not just a major sin (*gunah-e-kabirah*), but even greater than that. Among the major sins that have been mentioned, the very first is polytheism. The second thing is lying. Lying, we tell lies in small matters. By lying, faces become black. Whether they become now or will become in the hereafter is not known, but they do. Lying, these are both intellectual sins. Lying and polytheism. And the third thing is the wine-drinker... drinking wine. The fourth thing is adultery. The fifth thing is murder. These are major sins that are not easily forgiven. If you kill a believing brother, then you should understand that its retribution is very severe. Only God is the Forgiver. Adultery, you all know what consequences arise from adultery. And along with that is drinking wine. Whether one drinks a little wine or a lot, the matter is finished. So remember that these are major sins. Here it has been stated that these three things: polytheism, disbelief, and hypocrisy. So the one of polytheism and disbelief, I have told you, that the two exits from where things exit the body, in liquid form and in solid form, they exit. These two are... one, polytheism, which is the front part, where we urinate. And the second is the one... which... the waste that exits, that is our disbelief. Meaning, this... its meanings in the deeper realities are many. I am just giving the interpretations now. And besides that, there is hypocrisy. Hypocrisy is said to be that which is one thing on the tongue and another thing in the heart. And what is that? It has been told by the *da'i* that when we pass wind, that is the *mansul* (symbolized representation) of hypocrisy. Why? Because just as when wind is passed, no one knows, similarly, it is a hypocrite. He is not known. But the person himself also sometimes does not know. That is hypocrisy. In that too, you have to... Sayyidna 'Ali bin Husayn, who was the *da'i* 40 years ago, he said that in such a situation too, you must perform *istinja'* (washing). The meaning is that these three are evils, and these are their *mansulat*. Keep in mind that we have to perform ablution daily, we have to pray daily, and this month is the month of worship. Especially the *mansulat*. Today is the day which... Maulana Ali, *salawatullah 'alayh*, who is our Imam of the time, *Imam al-Muttaqin* (the Imam of the pious), *Qa'id al-Ghurr al-Muhajjilin* (the leader of the bright-faced honorable ones), he was struck on the 19th night. God willing, when the sermon is on the day after tomorrow, there will be a mention of Ali's grief, but the strike that happened, it happened today. But he passed away on the 21st. His martyrdom was on the 21st. From the 19th to the 21st, you remained alive. And at that time, what did you do? You kept your killer alive. You did not take retaliation (*qisas*). You said, "Give him food from my food and give him water from my water." This is the character of Ali. And when I pass away, then take retaliation from him. *An-nafsu bin-nafs* (A life for a life). A life in return for a life. So this is the way of Islam.

*Qul in tukhfuu maa fii suduurikum aw tubduuhu ya'lamhullaah, wa ya'lamu maa fis-samaawaati wa maa fil-ard, wallaahu 'alaa kulli shay'in qadiir* (Say, "Whether you conceal what is in your breasts or reveal it, Allah knows it. And He knows what is in the heavens and what is on the earth. And Allah is over all things competent"). Say: Whether you hide the matters of your hearts or reveal them, in every case, Allah knows. And whatever is in the heavens and the earth, that too He knows. And Allah is capable of all things. Now see, this is a verse from the Noble Quran, and its translation is that Allah knows everything. But there are some things that the *Awliya' Allah* (Friends of God) also know. And for this, the verse has come, *wa ma ya'lamu ta'weelahu illallaahu war-raasikhuuna fil-'ilm* (and none knows its interpretation except Allah and those firmly grounded in knowledge). Besides Allah, no one knows the interpretation, and along with Him, those who are firm in knowledge, meaning the Imams, peace be upon them, as many of the pure Imams as there are, they know. And whomever the pure Imams tell, they know. The meaning is that not only Allah knows the interpretation, but the right to interpret belongs to the *Wasi* of the Prophet, and along with him, as many *awsiyya'* (successors) who have passed. You see, from Adam, every prophet had a *wasi*. Every prophet... meaning every *Natiq* (speaker-prophet). Every *Natiq*, who are the possessors of a Shari'ah, Adam, Nuh, Ibrahim, Musa, 'Isa, all of them... the commands of the Shari'ah that have come, with them is a *wasi*. With Hazrat Musa was Harun, and Hazrat Ali is the *Ashraf al-Awsiyya'* (the most noble of successors). Today's day is dependent on Maulana Ali. And we should affirm him, that in our hearts, the love of Ali has awakened. Until this happens, our prayer is useless. We are offering prayers without understanding them. We... if there is no love for Ali, if there is no loyalty to the Commander of the Faithful, then... you see, the first chapter that has been kept is "Loyalty to the Commander of the Faithful," not that prayer has been kept or that. So in the *mansul* of prayer, if you do not keep this in mind, then there is no benefit. That is why we say what? Facing the Holy Kaaba in the Sacred Mosque, *Allahu Akbar* (God is the greatest). That we are facing towards the Kaaba, not to the Kaaba. We are not prostrating to the Kaaba, we are doing it towards the Kaaba. And who is the *mansul* of that Kaaba? You all know.

Meaning, O Messenger, make the hypocrites hear that Allah is well aware of your intentions and your false statements. He knows the *da'wahs* (missions) of the Imams and the *hujjats* (proofs). In short, everything is in His grasp and power. *Yawma tajidu kullu nafsin maa 'amilat min khayrin muhdaran wa maa 'amilat min suu'in tawaddu law anna baynahaa wa baynahuu amadan ba'iidan, wa yuhadhdhirukumullaahu nafsahu, wallaahu ra'uufun bil-'ibaad* (On the Day when every soul will find what it has done of good present [before it] and what it has done of evil, it will wish that between itself and that [evil] was a great distance. And Allah warns you of Himself, and Allah is Kind to [His] servants). On that day, every soul will find its good deeds present before it, and he who has done evil will wish that, if only between himself and that day there was a long period. And Allah warns you about Himself, and Allah is compassionate towards His servants. Meaning, in the presence of the *Sahib 'alayhi 's-salam, zikruhu 's-salam*, every *hadd* (rank) and every person of status will find his good deeds, that is, his sincere service in establishing the *da'wah* of the *Wasi*, present in such a form that he will be happy. In contrast, the enemy of the *Wasi* will wish that this day were further away, so that the punishment could be postponed for some time and relief could be had. And Allah, that is, the *Natiq*, warns you against opposing his *Wasi*. And the *Wasi* is merciful to his obedient servants.

In the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful... from the *hidayah* (guidance) of Sayyidna Muhammad... Maulana Ali... *Al-Fatiha*.

*Al-Fatiha*.

[side conversations about logistical matters]

<!-- AUG:ibrahim-2015 lecture 17 -->



<!-- AUG:ibrahim-2015 lecture 18 -->

Yes. Idh qalat al-mala'ikah. Idh qalat al-mala'ikah.

A'udhu billahi min al-shaytan al-rajim. Wa bi-fadhli bismillah al-rahman al-rahim.

idh qalat al-mala'ikatu ya maryamu innallaha yubashshiruki bi-kalimatin minhu ismuhu al-masihu 'isa ibnu maryama wajihan fi al-dunya wa al-akhirati wa min al-muqarrabin.

When the angels addressed Maryam, "O Maryam, Allah gives you good tidings of a word from His side. His name will be Messiah 'Isa ibn Maryam, distinguished in the world and the hereafter, and he will be from among the close servants of Allah."

Meaning... Now, this is the good tiding of 'Isa. And 'Isa alayhissalam himself... 'Isa alayhissalam himself is the good tiding of Nabi sallallahu alayhi wa alihi, that after me a great prophet will be born and his name will be Ahmad.

Meaning, the *hujjat* who was the intermediary between the Imam of the time and the *wali*, he said that Imam alayhissalam will make him a *musawwada* of a master of a station, who will receive support from the Imam. His name is Masih, meaning one who travels in the land of the *da'wat*. He is 'Isa ibn Maryam. Meaning... Here, where we have Masih, the meaning of Masih is to heal the sick. That is not the meaning. Rather, he is made Masih from *siyahat* (traveling). Because it has been related about Janab 'Isa in the biography that... he is one who... he never used to stay in one place. You would go from here to there, and from there to there. You would preach. And you had no home. The people, who were your disciples—and at that time there were many disciples—they said, "O 'Isa, Ruhullah, everyone has a home, you have no home. You go from here to there, and there to there. You are our elder, we will build a home for you." First, he said there is no need. But when they insisted a lot, you said, "Alright, tomorrow I will tell you." The next day, they met 'Isa and he took them along. Going here and there, they reached the foothills of a mountain. And where there is a slope on the mountain, he says, "Build it here." They said, "How can we build it here? There is a slope here, this is a gradient." He said, "The condition of the world is also like this. What is the point of building a house here?" So these are the narrations about 'Isa, which are both exoteric and have this esoteric meaning behind them. That 'Isa alayhissalam did not stay in one place; he was always traveling. And that is why... Now, the station of 'Isa and the station of Musa, in the *mustawda'in*—meaning in the great realities, where we have the *muntakha*—even in the *muntakha*, the stations have changed. For Janab Ibrahim, `wa ja'alna ibrahima imama` (And We made Ibrahim an Imam). We made Ibrahim an Imam. So Ibrahim's rank became great. Then Nuh's `istafayna` (We chose). His rank also became great. And then there was Janab-e-Payghambar's. In between them are Musa and 'Isa. The rank of these two is less than that of Ibrahim, Nuh, etc. Although they had a very large community. Musa had a large Jewish era, and here was Christianity. So they, in their respective individualities... in the realm of individuality, they had many followers, which is why more people came here. But in terms of rank, they are lesser.

He is 'Isa ibn Maryam, meaning he is a *natiq* and will be the son of a *musawwada*. In the exoteric, because of establishing the *shari'ah*... he... and by giving the teaching of the esoteric, he will be distinguished. And he will be counted among those of high ranks.

wa yukallimu al-nasa fi al-mahdi wa kahlan wa min al-salihin. He will benefit the people of the *da'wat* in his time of training with the grace of knowledge, and then also after the completion of the ranks. And then he will be included in the group of the righteous *mutlaqin*.

qalat rabbi anna yakunu li waladun wa lam yamsasni basharun qala kadhaliki allahu yakhluqu ma yasha'u idha qada amran fa innama yaqulu lahu kun fa yakun. Maryam said, "O Lord, how will a child be born to me, when no man has even touched me?" He said, "Thus Allah creates what He wills. When He decides upon a matter, He just says to it, 'Be,' and it becomes." Now, this is an exoteric story, but if you read the books of *ta'wil*, you will realize that there is a *ta'wil* behind it. "Having a child" does not just mean this one story, that Janab Maryam said, "How... I have become old," just as it was for Ibrahim alayhissalam's wife, "How will I have a child now?" It is not like that. Rather, Maryam himself was a man, and after that, your point is, "How can such a master of a station be born from me, whom I can train?" Look, it will come ahead.

Meaning, "O my Mawla, how will I become a *musawwada* of someone? And why will anyone be attributed to me, when I have not benefited at all from the scholars of the high ranks of the *da'wat*?" Meaning, 'woman'—here, Maryam means woman. She is always a woman or a girl, wherever it is mentioned in the Quran Sharif, *mustawda'in* is intended. And... *mustafidin* is intended. And here, just as in the story of Janab Musa, when you went towards Madyan, you saw at a well that many people are drawing water and drinking it. And all the men are drawing water and drinking. Now, two girls were standing there; they were not getting water. At that time, you... Musa alayhissalam's majesty was great. Musa alayhissalam went and gave them water to drink; he drew water for them. Those people became very grateful. So, those two girls, what were they? They were *mustafidin* (those seeking benefit). They were not getting a chance. There were big *mufid* (benefactors) who were drawing water, meaning acquiring knowledge, but they had no chance. And these girls, what were they? They were the *mustafidin* of Janab Shu'ayb. Mawlana Shu'ayb, who was a prophet, he is the last prophet of Janab Ibrahim's... era. Ibrahim's era ends there with Shu'ayb. And then Janab Musa's era begins. So, in reality, there was to be a connection, an *ittisal*, between Janab Musa and Janab Shu'ayb. When this happened, he drew water for them, they went and expressed great thanks, told their father that a young man has come and gave us water to drink. He said, "Call him." He called him and they talked, and then he gave one girl in marriage to him. Meaning, he married her. What does marrying her mean? It means he made him his *mustafid*.

He said, "There is no problem. Don't worry. Allah is not bound by any particular method in such matters. He elevates whomever He wills. And when He establishes them, He Himself graces them with His help and support. And that person starts working according to the desired purpose. This success is not obtained by the strength of one's arm, until the Imam grants it." One is *kasab* (acquisition/effort), and one is *mawhub* (gifted/bestowed). *Kasab* means a person, whether he is a *mu'min* or a *muwasi'* or whatever, through his own brilliance, his own spiritual exercises, his own knowledge, he wants to attain a rank and move forward. So all those who want to move forward, they come under the masculine gender. Meaning, if you have to explain to them, teach someone a lesson... Sometimes there are some who are *majhul* (unknown/passive), they cannot do it. But they are great; they are prayerful, pious, all that. But they cannot explain to others. So there are two types. So what happens in that? One is *kasab*. Everyone acquires *kasab*, but then to repeat it is not within their power. To give an explanation... So what happens? Their rank is lower. And these ones who have the power to give benefit, they advance. And what happens? It is *mawhub* (gifted). Up to a certain extent, there is *kasab*. And when the rank of *kasab* reaches a certain level, then supports start coming from above. We don't know. *Ta'id* is such a thing that no one knows. Even the one who has the status, he also doesn't realize it. And that is why it is said that the *Hadd-i 'Ali* himself does not know whose *Hadd-i 'Ali* he is. The concept of *Hadd-i 'Ali*... no one knows. And if someone says so, they are liars. That I am his *Hadd-i 'Ali*, he is his *Hadd-i 'Ali*. They are liars. No one belongs to anyone. All... the count is with the Imam salawatullahi alayh. So remember that the *Hadd-i 'Ali*... in your congregation, someone is your *Hadd-i 'Ali*, someone is your *Hadd-i Adna*. The arrangement is only with the Imam. Here in the masjid, wherever we may be, it makes no difference, but the Imam has his arrangement. So when the stage of *kasab* ends—acquisition of knowledge—and in that too, there are two kinds. The practical form is different, the knowledge-based form is different. You have heard the practical form in the sermons: that reciting the *kalima tayyiba*, believing in the Day of Judgment, believing in resurrection, `anna al-jannata haqqun wa al-nara haqqun wa al-mawt haqqun` (that paradise is true, hellfire is true, death is true)—all these things, if they are there, then the practical form is formed. But after that, you read the injunctions of the *shari'ah*, you read the *ta'wilat*, you read the *maqbulat* and *maqbulat*, you read the *maqdumat*, and the *maqdumat* become greater for you. After that, progressing further and further, you reach the realities. This is the means of forming the knowledge-based form. So the knowledge-based form is superior in comparison. That is why `alladhina amanu wa utu al-'ilma darajat` (those who have believed and have been given knowledge, in degrees). There are no degrees in action. The one who performs more actions does not get higher degrees. Rather, the one who has acquired knowledge, he gets them.

wa yu'allimuhu al-kitaba wa al-hikmata wa al-tawrata wa al-injil. And Allah will teach him the Book of Wisdom and the Torah and the Gospel.

Meaning, the exoteric... the exoteric *shari'ah* and its... its *batin*. And his own *shari'ah* and its summary, the teaching of the *batin*, He will adorn him with. So it is that an *'alim* is one who knows both the *zahir* and the *batin*. The *batin* is superior, but both things must be known. Sometimes there are people who can only be refuted by arguing with the *zahir*. In a debate. For some, you have to tell the *batin*. So remember that the *batin* is given to the people of faith. The *batin* is not told to everyone. Especially in the time of *taqiyya*, it is better to be completely silent. You may be very advanced, but it is not necessary for you to tell everyone, because it is the time of *taqiyya*. Until there is an order from the Imam... we should only tell our own people. To the common people, those things which are of the *zahir*, refute them with that. That is called *mukasara*. *Mukasara* is one thing, and see, this is... a spiritual birth that happens, when a person comes from outside to give his hand. So the spiritual birth, just as the physical birth happens, in the same way, Sayyidna Qadi al-Nu'man salawatullahi alayh says that the spiritual birth is just like the physical birth. When a child is born, its umbilical cord... it is tied with it. And in a few days, that cord falls off. So the person opposite you, the cleric or whoever he is, tie him with his own beliefs. And slowly, his beliefs will break, they will end, and you will prevail. This is what Sayyidna Qadi al-Nu'man salawatullahi alayh says.

wa rasulan ila bani isra'ila anni qad ji'tukum bi-ayatin min rabbikum anni akhluqu lakum min al-tini ka-hay'at al-tayri fa-anfukhu fihi fa-yakunu tayran bi-idhni allah. And he will go to the Children of Israel as a messenger and will say, "I have brought a miracle from your Lord for you. For you, from kneaded clay... the form of a bird..." This is related about 'Isa, that from kneaded clay you would make a bird and breathe life into it, and it would... So this would be a spectacle. If one looks at the *zahir*. And the common people are all on the *zahir*. What meaning will they take? That he made something from clay and breathed into it, and it started flying. This is not a real thing. But where Allah Ta'ala says that where parables are given, the parables are given to us so that we can relate to them. If you tell something, a round thing, you will say 'ball', then they will understand a round thing. For a long thing, you will tell something different. So, `ussisa al-dinu 'ala mithal al-khalq` (the religion was founded upon the likeness of creation). The likeness of creation, meaning what is visible in the world, the religion is explained by giving its example. As has been mentioned here and there. So remember that giving examples is a science, which is called the science of *tamsil*. There is *ta'wil*, there is *tamsil*, and then there is the intended meaning. What is intended by this? From kneaded clay I will make a bird for you, then I will breathe into it... breathe into it. And by Allah's command it will become a bird. Meaning... Now look, meaning... this is the real meaning. If you only read this much, what will you understand? This is a translation. In every Quran Sharif, there is a slightly different wording, but it is that from kneaded clay... if someone were a big magician, and this is just magic. How can this be? What is the meaning? Sayyidna Husamuddin al-Razi says, "Meaning... he will go to the people of the *da'wat* and say to them, 'Look, I am the messenger of Allah.'" He will go to the people of the *da'wat*? What does that mean? To the believers. Meaning, to the Muslims or the believers. "I will make from clay..." From clay means those who know the exoteric sciences, who know a little bit about how to pray and so on. I will go to them and say this, that I am the messenger of Allah. "He has given me this miracle or proof that I... from among the *ma'dhunin*, I will select one person and..." *Ma'dhun*—those who are appropriate, that is a rank. *Ma'dhun*. There are also steps within the *ma'dhun*. The limited *ma'dhun* is different, the *ma'dhun* of the sanctuary is different, the absolute *ma'dhun* is different; all are different. So, remember that from among the *ma'dhun*, "I will select one and elevate him to the station of *hujjat*." *Hujjat* means *da'i*, to the rank of *da'i*. The station of *hujjat*, what is it? That they are so weighty in knowledge that they fly in the heavens of the *da'wat*. So their knowledge is like that. We have... if you read the accounts, you will realize what kind of scholars have passed. Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hiba, Sayyidna Hasan bin Hiba, and Sayyidna Ali bin Hiba, Sayyidna Hibatullah Sahab. These are all a generation that passed by, a special one. And Sayyidna Ibrahim, their father, Sayyidna Mahmud Nuh's son. These are all such people that... who were selected. And this is not of the Tayyibi era, but it is the miracle of the Qaimi era, that such scholars were born among us. The Dawoodis have the treasure. Look, from where they separated... We had neither the capital, nor the books; everything was taken away. If you listen to Sayyidna Sulayman Sahab's sermon, he says that they have more books, filled with those from the Tayyibi era. And today, the internet has become such that all the books are roaming the market. Until... 60 years ago... 60 years ago or more than that, *Da'a'im al-Islam* used to be considered forbidden by the Dawoodis. 60 years ago. Asif Fyzee Sahab, who was our barrister, he wanted—and he was very learned, meaning his Arabic was good—he wanted me to print the *Da'a'im al-Islam*, this one that is handwritten. He said, "Give it to me." Hazrat Ishaq Sahab had it. Hazrat Sahab refused. He said, "No, until the seniors give permission..." Sayyidna Tahir Saifuddin Sahab... was not in favor. And... now... there is a lot of difference between the era of 60 years ago and today's era. Today, you talk and the mobile phone is like a whole computer. At that time, it wasn't like that. Hazrat refused. He became very... he became disappointed and went and brought a copy from the Dawoodis. His name was Abdul Husayn; he went to Surat, Burhanpur, and from there he brought the book. And in the preface, he has written that I went to Sayyidna Tahir Saifuddin, he did not give it to me, and I brought it from here, Burhanpur or Surat. So sometimes it happens that according to the times... now knowledge has opened up. Because we have come very close to the era of unveiling. So now is the time for it to open. And this very thing, in the time of Adam alayhissalam, was the time for it to close, and at that time, he wanted to open it. That was his mistake. The regret that happened, and that he was forbidden from the tree, that is the meaning. That 7000 years ago, the thing we are explaining today, how could it have been opened 7000 years ago? It was the beginning of the concealment. The night was beginning. The night is a time for sleeping. Today is the time for waking up. So there is a lot of difference between that and this. According to the times, every work is done according to its time. We have the information of the era of unveiling, or its affairs; they are different. The periods of interval are different, and the periods of concealment are different. Now, this is the last part of the era of concealment. Since when? Since Sayyidna Muhammad's time. The Qaimi era has begun. We are not in the Muhammadi era. We are certainly the community of Muhammad, but... what is it? We are in the Qaimi era. And the Dawoodis don't even know that the Qaimi era has begun. They have no concept. They have the *Raahat al-'Aql* of the Qaim. They have big books, but when the hearts are blind, the spectacles for the eyes don't work. The hearts are blind. They don't know. You read the *Barahin*, Sayyidna Hazrat Shakir Sahab has written, and all are the benefactions of Sayyidna Sulayman Sahab. How this has reached a state of stupor.

And then I will breathe into it. What will I do? And then I will breathe. And then I will bestow my help and support. He will become a *hujjat* and will soar in the comprehension of high matters. He will not soar... that bird will start flying. Whatever I do, I will do it with the help of Mawlana Imam.

wa ubri'u al-akmaha wa al-abrasa wa uhyi al-mawta bi-idhni allah. And I will make the blind see. Now, this is famous, that you would make a blind person, a sightless person, see. Whether it happened in the exoteric or not, we have no idea. But its esoteric meaning is that what is it? Physical sight is different, spiritual insight is different. Physical sight is what your eyes see. But spiritual insight is what the eye of your heart sees. So, what is it of his? That for those with spiritual insight... what is written? And I will make the blind see, and I will cure the patient with leprosy, and I will also give life to the dead by Allah's command. Meaning, I will bestow the light of guidance upon the blind of insight. It's not physical sight, it's spiritual insight. Those whose hearts are blind, I will give them the light of guidance. I will bring those who exaggerate in the station of the *wasi* to the right path. And in this, there is also exaggeration. And in the matter of the *wasi*, it is always wrong to exaggerate. Neither exaggeration is right, nor falling short is right. And in the dead of ignorance, I will create the life of knowledge. This too I will do with the help and support of Mawlana Imam. And every... see, even the greatest of men, he praises his superior. That I received this from the Imam. We are receiving it from the *da'i*, so we are doing it. We are not doing anything. This acknowledgement should be there. This humility should be there. This modesty should be there. If this humility is not there, you cannot advance. That whoever is speaking should say that all this is given from above, I have nothing of my own. If you say, "I am explaining, I am doing this," it is wrong.

wa unabbi'ukum bima ta'kuluna wa ma taddakhiruna fi buyutikum. inna fi dhalika la-ayatan lakum in kuntum mu'minin. And what you eat and what you store in your houses, I will also give you news of that. Indeed, in all this, there is a sign for you, on the condition that you are believers. What you eat and what you store in your houses, I will give news of that. Did you understand anything from this? That I will give news of what you store in the storage room? And in all this, there is surely a sign for you, if you are believers. Meaning... Meaning, I will also tell you how much knowledge you have acquired, what your capacity is, and which sciences you consider exclusive to yourselves and hide from others. Surely, from this explanation, you must have realized that I am not false in my claim of prophethood. This is the word of Janab 'Isa. If you trust and believe in me, then these proofs should be enough for you.

wa musaddiqan lima bayna yadayya min al-tawrati wa li-uhilla lakum ba'da alladhi hurrima 'alaykum wa ji'tukum bi-ayatin min rabbikum fa-ittaqullaha wa ati'un. And I have come for this reason, that what was before me, the Torah, I may also confirm it. See, first they confirm. The Prophet also confirmed all—the Injeel, the Torah, the Zabur—that all were revealed from Allah's side. Now, what has been told to us is a very high matter, which people don't even know. That in the Injeel... those things... the deficiency that had arisen, Janab-e-Payghambar brought it in his *shari'ah*. Wherever a contradiction arises in the *shari'ah*, where a deficiency occurs or something like that happens, another great prophet comes and corrects it. The code of conduct is formed. So for every era, according to that, a book was revealed—the Torah, the Injeel, all. But the comprehensive one for all of them is the Holy Quran, and its scope has come from a very high place, from the sphere of the First Intellect. And all the other books, all have come from the Tenth Intellect. So if you know who the Tenth Intellect is and who the First Intellect is, it will be known that the Quran Majeed... `wa la ratbin wa la yabisin illa fi kitabin mubin` (and no moist or dry [thing] but that it is in a clear Book). That there is no moist or dry thing that is not in the Quran Majeed. Now you have to find it. `Afala ta'qilun? Afala tubsirun?` (Do you not then reason? Do you not then see?) Do you not review? Do you not see, do you not have intellect? That all these things, until you search and find them, you will not. Now, how to do this search? Through the words of the *du'at*. You cannot apply your own logic, that this is so, that is so. Whatever the *da'i* is indicating, you have to say that it has come from my superior, that's why I am giving it. Not from myself.

And for you, some of those things which were made forbidden for you, I will make them lawful. And I have brought signs from your Lord for you. I have brought signs. So fear Allah and obey me. Meaning, I have come to confirm the *ta'wil* of the *shari'ah* of the previous Prophet Musa. And some of the sciences whose benefit you were forbidden from, I will declare them permissible for you. And on my clear statement, I have brought proofs. This is the same thing, that in the *shari'ah* of Musa, the contradiction that existed, I have come to correct that. Every person does this. But the Shari'ah of Muhammadi is dominant over all, that whatever shari'ahs there were, in them there was some... that... according to the times, as people came forward, Allah revealed the book accordingly. But the final book came which was comprehensive for all, and that is the Furqan-i 'Azim.

So fear the opposition of Mawlana Imam, and accepting my word, obey me.

inna allaha rabbi wa rabbukum fa-'buduhu hadha siratun mustaqim. Indeed, Allah is my Lord and your Lord. So worship Him alone. This is the straight path. Meaning, Mawlana Imam is my and your... our... he is the nourisher of all of us. So obey him alone.

While pointing towards the *wasi* Sham'un. Then, pointing towards his *wasi* Sham'un, he said, "This is your Imam. His station is firm. And this is the straight path. I am establishing him for your guidance." That this is... after me, this one will remain established. So `ihdina al-sirat al-mustaqim` (Guide us to the straight path). The meaning of *sirat* is path, and elsewhere in the realities it is stated that *sirat al-mustaqim*... `ihdina al-sirat al-mustaqim`. O Allah, guide us on the straight path. We are on the straight path, why are we asking again? Because the one who believes in the Imam, we are certain that his son will also be an Imam. After the father, the son, one after another, until the Originator. So *sirat* in one sense means the son of the *hujjat* who is your esteemed son, who will be the next Imam. This is the meaning of `ihdina al-sirat al-mustaqim`.

[Prayers in Arabic]

[Recitation of Al-Fatiha]

<!-- AUG:ibrahim-2015 lecture 19 -->

[Esoteric Translation]: Meaning, the deniers made many plans to nullify the *imamat* of the *wasi*. Read the entire history, from the gathering of Dhul-Ashirah to here. "Oh brother, Ali has brought faith, he has brought Islam. How can a child bring it? He is 10.5 years old." He was 10.5 years old when he became the Prophet's *wasi*. The Prophet was 40. Look at the age difference. At that time, people said, "This is..." As you have heard in the *majalis*, the Prophet asked, "Who will be my *wasi*? Who will be my supporter? Who is ready to stand with me?" At that time, no one answered. There were 40 men of the Banu Abd al-Muttalib. But Ali said, "I am ready." "O Messenger of Allah, I am ready." You were 10.5, 11 years old. After he took the pledge three times, the Prophet said, "O Ali, you are my *wasi* here and in the hereafter." Now, the common Muslims don't accept this. "How could Ali have brought faith? Faith was brought by... Islam was brought by Abu Bakr. So the first *khalifa* is him." This is not correct. At that time, it was said, "Ali was young. The Prophet was not young. How can we accept him?" The Prophet was mature and wise, he was a Prophet. How can you compare the Prophet with Ali? The point is, their reasoning is flawed. That Ali was the first to enter Islam. In childhood, a child's Islam is not counted. But Ali, even as a child, was great. That matter was not accepted, and they turned to opposition. *Munafaqat* (hypocrisy) was going on from that time. But when the event of Ghadir Khumm happened... opposition happens when the matter of distributing offices comes up. When the Prophet said, `man kuntu mawlahu fa-hadha 'aliyyun mawlahu` (Of whomsoever I am the master, Ali is his master), then the trouble started. Until then, everyone was fine. "Ali is good, he is a companion." You read their jurists' writings, they say they were all friends, they loved each other very much. Remember, the wives of Ali were... in Islam, after the Prophet, the *sahabi* came, after the *sahabi* came the *tabi'in*, after the *tabi'in* came the *taba' tabi'in*. But among those who were believers, of different kinds, some turned to opposition. Many among the *taba' tabi'in*... whereas a *sahabi*, they say, is someone who saw the Messenger in a state of faith. The state of faith had not even begun. When the Prophet appointed his *wasi*, then the state of faith began. Before that, it was the state of Islam. There is a verse. The people say, "O Messenger of Allah, *nahnu amanna* (we have believed)." Tell them, you have not believed, say *aslamna* (we have submitted/become Muslim). Because submitting is one thing, and believing is another. So here, the meaning is that they were belying and denying the *wasi*. They accepted the Prophet. Look, the whole Islamic world accepts the Prophet. There are billions of people, two billion people. But the Shia, who have love for Ali, are fewer. Then even among the Shia, there are divisions and divisions. The people who are coming with the truth until the end are very few. So the point is that submitting (*islam*) is one thing, and believing (*iman*) is another. Just as submitting (*islam*) creates a form of action, like reciting `la ilaha illa-llah muhammadun rasulu-llah` and accepting one or two other things. Believing in the resurrection, believing in the raising after death, that heaven is true, hell is true, death is true, raising is true, all these things. After that, believing in the Imams, all these things together create the state of faith. The rank of faith is higher. And the circle is smaller. As Sayyidna Ja'far al-Sadiq has said, there is a large circle, that is Islam. Within it is a smaller circle. The one in the smaller circle can claim to be in the larger circle too. But the one in the larger circle... a smaller circle is inside a larger circle. The one in the large circle cannot say he is in the smaller circle. `is qala-llahu ya 'isa inni mutawaffika wa rafi'uka ilayya wa mutahhiruka mina-lladhina kafaru wa ja'ilu-lladhina-ttaba'uka fawqa-lladhina kafaru ila yawmi-l-qiyamah` (When Allah said, "O Isa, indeed I will take you and raise you to Myself and purify you from those who disbelieve and make those who follow you superior to those who disbelieve until the Day of Resurrection.") When Allah said, "O Isa, I am going to give you death and raise you to My presence and purify you from the taunts and accusations of the disbelievers, and I will give your followers superiority over the disbelievers until the Day of Resurrection." [Esoteric Translation]: Meaning, the Imam said, "O Isa, I will relieve you of your duties." The duty of Isa is over. When the matter of his killing came up, `wa ma qataluhu wa ma salabuhu wa lakin shubbiha lahum` (They did not kill him, nor did they crucify him, but it was made to appear so to them). They were put into doubt. Who? The people who were believers. They neither killed him nor... Here, they have put up pictures showing blood flowing, nails being hammered in. None of this happened. Isa was not even there. And who was there instead of Isa? All this has come in the books. Your *hijabs* (veils) performed the work. The one who was nailed was different, the one who was killed was different, the one who appeared later was different. Three *hijabs* were made for you. The inner matter is always different. If this inner matter didn't exist, then every person who reads the Quran would become a scholar. The people who are reading it, they are reading it with great recitation, with great *tajwid*. But are they scholars? Are they learned? So remember, `la yamassuhu illa-l-mutahharun` (None touch it except the purified). No one can touch this Quran until they are pure. Now look at how people are. Today, in the whole world, who prints the most? It's being printed in Germany, in Karachi, in Peshawar, in Delhi. In Delhi, now even Hindus are printing it, doing the binding, doing everything. So what is Allah's claim? `la yamassuhu illa-l-mutahharun`. No one can touch this. It's written on every Quran. So the point is that reading the Quran is within everyone's ability. You have been given ears, you have been given work, so you are doing it, printing it. But the real meaning, its essence (*lubb*), or its purpose (*maqsud*), are you getting it? You are not getting it. That essence is what you are being told by Sayyidna Hasan bin Nuh, what its meaning is. "O Isa, I will relieve you of your duties and include you in my circle and my assembly of *da'is*. And I will save you from the foul language and evil deeds of the disbelievers, the deniers. And until your era is complete, I will give all believers superiority and elevation over the deniers of the *wasi*." This is... meaning, the Imam has said it. The Imam of Isa's time was Maulana Salim, may peace be upon him. The support comes from him. "O Isa, your duty is over. I am relieving you. A great new Prophet is about to come." And look, after 600 years, a Prophet came. Otherwise, what used to happen? A gap of 1000 years, 1100 years. From Adam to Nuh's time, look. From Nuh to Ibrahim's time. Nuh's age is said to be 900 years. But that's not his age. No one lives for 900 years. Rather, it was his era. Nuh's era lasted 900 years. His age was 120 years. Then Ibrahim, Musa, Isa. Each one was given about 1000 years, more or less, depending on the era's difference. In this way, up to you, 7000 years have passed. The world will end in 7200 years. In that, between Isa and the Prophet, only 600 years passed. How did that happen? So short. Isa's law, Christianity, was spread throughout the world. But after that, Allah felt that corruption was entering the religion, so a great Prophet had to come. A small prophet couldn't do the job. So Prophet Muhammad, may peace be upon him, came to Makkah. `huwa-lladhi ba'atha fi-l-ummiyyina rasulan minhum yatlu 'alayhim ayatihi wa yuzakkihim wa yu'allimuhumu-l-kitaba wa-l-hikmah` (It is He who sent among the unlettered a Messenger from themselves, reciting to them His verses and purifying them and teaching them the Book and wisdom). The Prophet was sent to such a land, Makkah, where no one could imagine that faith and light would come. Because they were all drinkers, fornicators, such people who would bury their daughters alive. And the *kasah*, the skull, they would break it and drink wine from the skull. Such was the age of ignorance. But it was Allah's will, so the Prophet came. So how did he come after 600 years? Now, it has also been told by the *du'at* that it's not 600 years; 400 years are still going on. It has a *tattibah*, meaning a make-up. The 400 years that have come after Sayyidna Sulaiman are the era of the Qaim. But we are the *ummah* of Muhammad. We are the *ummah* of Muhammad, but we are not in the era of Muhammad anymore. We have moved beyond the era of Muhammad. The era of Sayyidna Sulaiman came. A great, wise personality came, and the era changed, and the era of the Qaim began. Imam al-Qaim came. Every era has a... duration. So our era is not the era of Muhammad. This era is even more advanced. The era of the Prophet was higher than the era of Isa. The era of Imam al-Qaim will be the highest of all. And that is why we have such great scholars born among us. After Sayyidna Umam, you see the people who have come, what kind of people have been born. How, in terms of beliefs. This is not apparent. The apparent ones passed through many kinds of conditions. There were wars, tribal wars, all sorts of things. But the inner matter is that the beliefs that were established by Sayyidna Hasan, Ismail bin Hiba, and Sayyidna Ismail bin Muhammad, they were all *da'is* of the *shumu'*. Such *da'is* were born who had not even appeared in the era of the *Zabiha*. Whose grace is this? It is the grace of Sayyidna Qaim. And his *juzwi Qaim* is Maulana Hakim, may peace be upon him. Because what is it? The era of the Prophet ended, but the era of Ali did not end. The era of the Prophet ended as a *Natiq*. But the era of Ali, why is it? As has been explained by Sayyidna Mu'ayyad and others, that... ...there is a *majlis* about Dhu al-Qarnayn. Dhu al-Qarnayn: the one with two horns or the one of two eras. So what is the one of two eras? Or the one with two horns? Until today, no one has been seen with two horns. Now look, our... Abu al-Kalam Azad explains that the one with two horns was... or the one who conquered the world, was Alexander the Great. This is his claim. Whose? Abu al-Kalam Azad's. And after him, another great scholar of theirs was born, Abu al-Ala Mawdudi. He wrote that no, this is Cyrus the Great, the king of Persia, who also had two bones protruding from his head. Those are the two horns. But neither this is correct, nor that is correct. Our *du'at* explain... ...that this is the one of two eras. And what are the two eras? The era of Muhammad and the era of the Qaim. Ali is the master of both. He is the *asas* until the Qaim comes. He is the *asas*. He is the *asas* of the Prophet, and he is the *juzwi Qaim* of your era. The era continues from the Mustaqarr Imam. This is also in the *batin* (esoteric). In the *zahir* (exoteric), we... ...the era of Isa, the era of Musa. You will never say the era of any of the Imams. Whose era will you say? The era of Musa, the era of Isa. But inside, those who are learned or understand the *batin*, they will say this is his era, this is his era.
