In the same way, understand that the era of Ali is ongoing, and until today, we who have recited his name belong to him. `thumma ilayya marji'ukum fa-ahkumu baynakum fima kuntum fihi takhtalifun` (Then to Me is your return, and I will judge between you concerning that over which you used to differ). "Then to Me is your return. Then I will judge your mutual disputes." Meaning, in the end, you will bring the case of your quarrels to me. And I myself will judge it. `fa-amma-lladhina kafaru fa-u'adhdhibuhum 'adhaban shadidan fi-d-dunya wa-l-akhirah wa ma lahum min nasirin` (And as for those who disbelieved, I will punish them with a severe punishment in this world and the Hereafter, and they will have no helpers). "Those who disbelieved, I will give them a severe punishment in this world and the Hereafter. And they will have no helper." [Esoteric Translation]: Meaning, the one whom the Imam of the Time has appointed, the denier of him will be cast into a severe punishment. Meaning, first they will be left in the darkness of their misguidance and ignorance. And then they will be afflicted with the real punishment. Now, this is complete. What is here? *Kufr* (disbelief). It doesn't say *shirk* (polytheism). That those who commit *shirk*. It says that those who have committed *kufr*, I will give them a severe punishment in this world and the hereafter. *Kufr* is one thing, *shirk* is another. *Shirk* happened with the Prophet, because during the Prophet's time, the polytheists came into Islam. So the wars the Prophet fought were all against the polytheists. But the wars that happened later, the war of Siffin, the war of Nahrawan, and the war of Jamal, these all happened in Maulana Ali's time, and he fought against the *kafirs*. What does *kafir* mean? One who covers up. Those who covered up the station of the Prophet's *wasi*, they were the people of *kufr*. They were the *kafirs*. And what will I do to them? I will afflict them with a severe punishment. And where will I cast them? In the manifest. The deniers will be cast into a severe punishment. And the meaning of this is that first, they will be left in misguidance. The entire world, the Islamic world, is in misguidance. Where is their leader? Who is their *khalifa*? Who are their great ones? The four *khalifas* came and went. And after them, the fifth caliphate was Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz, who was from the descendants of Muawiyah. He was a good man, a straightforward man. That's him. And after that, where are they? 1300 years have passed like this. Now who are the great ones? They say, it's him. King Farouk of Egypt. He is the one. Or after that, it changed again, Jamal Abdel Nasser. He is the great one of the entire Islamic world. Then it changed again, Saudi Arabia, King Faisal's time came. They consider him the great one. The point is, they had no great leader. They were all squabbling amongst themselves. And what happens in that? They will be afflicted with a severe punishment. The seven levels of the punishment of *adna* (the lower world) will be completed. And then, because of their misguidance and ignorance, they will be cast into darkness. Now, we should also realize this. This world has been running for 1400 years, and during this time, people who died, the people of the *awam* (common masses), where did they go? Where are they? When will they be raised? When Imam al-Qaim comes. So where are you? You have been thrown into the *darajat* (levels of hell). And when Sayyidna Qaim judges, they have already gone through one small punishment. After that, it is possible they will also suffer the punishment of *a'raf* (the heights), and in *sakhrah* (the rock/prison) will be their sentence. The master of punishment and reward is he. So remember, that is why it has come that the seven levels of *adna* are completed, and then the hereafter's... the hereafter means their hereafter, when they are cast into *sakhrah*. They will have to suffer in *sakhrah*. What is *sakhrah*? *Sakhrah* is 16,000 crore years. 16,000 crore. It's such a long duration. They will be thrown there. *Sakhrah* means... *sakhr* means stone. Below the earth, what is called *patal*, the netherworld, below the earth is a rocky land. It is hard, very hard land. They will be thrown there. And then, *niran-i-jahannam* (the fires of hell). *Nir* means... may Allah save us from it. *Nir*. *Nir* is... you can see the mention of *niran-i-jahannam* in Surah Amma Yatasa'alun. So *nir* is a heat... this heat is a different matter. The heat inside is a different thing. Just like bread is hot on the outside, but if you look inside, it's even hotter. When you remove the steam, you realize it. So it is very hot. So *nir*, which has a *kasrah* (vowel mark for 'i'), meaning it is below, that is the worst. And after that comes the *fathah* (vowel mark for 'a'). *Nar* (fire) is made from *nir*. *Nar* has both heat and light. In the fire. Light is goodness. For which Sayyidna Musa went to Tur to get fire, and he found prophethood. But there is also a bad side, that a person gets burned by fire. Then from this, what has come out is *nur* (light). One is with a *waw*, one is with an *alif*. These are all *huruf-i-illat* (weak letters). *Alif, waw, ya* are the *huruf-i-illat*. Without them, no sentence or word is formed in Urdu. So *niran-i-jahannam* is the worst, because it has a *kasrah*, it is at the bottom, the lowest. The *fathah* is in... there is... that is *nar*. *Nar* has both qualities, there is light and there is also that. But *nur* has less heat. Look at a tube light. It doesn't get very hot. If it stays on for two hours, how much heat should it produce? You check. So when will *nur* be created? To reach *nur* from *nir*, one has to pass through *nar*. And what will happen? When will *nur* shine? Until the *alif*, which has a value of one... and what is the value of *waw*? Six. There is a difference of five. Until one believes in the Panjatan (Five Holy Ones), follows the portion of the five, until then they will not be brought into the state of *nur*. So there is *nir*, *nur*, and *nar*. `wa amma-lladhina amanu wa 'amilu-s-salihati fa-yuwaffihim ujurahum wa-llahu la yuhibbu-z-zalimin` (And as for those who believed and did righteous deeds, He will give them their rewards in full, and Allah does not like the wrongdoers). "And those who believed and did righteous deeds, He will give them their full reward. And Allah does not like the wrongdoers." [Esoteric Translation]: Meaning, those who accepted the *wasi* and himself appointed a *khalifa* at his station, their efforts will not go in vain. Through the elevation of support and ranks, their good reward will be given. And Maulana Imam al-Haqq does not like those who are ignorant of the Imam. It's just. The people who are... at that time, a good *da'wah* was also going on, and a bad *da'wah* too. The people who believed, they believed in Salman and others. After that, the chain of imams became hidden. So the *du'at* were working, the *bab al-abwab* were working, the Imams... all those who were working. The people who believed in them were on the right path. And those who didn't believe were searching for Razi, searching for Ghazali, doing this and that. They wrote such books. *Bukhari Sharif*... what a book. The *Sihah Sitta*. Such thick volumes. And what is in it? You read it and see. Maybe we haven't read it, we just hear about it. Read it and you will find out what kind of narrations are in it. What are the narrations from A'ishah like? All this is fabricated. For them, *Bukhari Sharif* has the status that comes after the Quran. What do we have? After the Quran, if there is an excellent book, the Hadith of the Prophet is a different thing. But the inner matter is *Nahj al-Balagha*. The sermons of Maulana Ali are the most excellent things. So read this, it's over. He does not like those who are ignorant of the Imam. Ali is the one whom to believe in is the principle of our faith. If you don't believe in Ali and are doing the works of the world, there is no benefit. You are giving *zakat* for the world, fasting, doing everything. But there is no *ma'rifat* (gnosis). Whatever *ma'rifat* you have, whatever your attainment is, `al-nasibu yusibuk` (your portion will reach you). Whatever your portion is, Allah will give it. It's not that every clever person gets everything. Whatever is to be given, the one above will give it. The scale is with him. How much to give. How much is the grasping power? One grasps and then forgets. For some, it remains in the mind. So this... to think that just because someone is very clever, they have become very capable, that's not it. Cleverness is one thing, and capability is another. Just as there is a difference between *makkari* (cunning) and *shujari* (bravery). *Makkari* is a type of cleverness, but it is used for bad things. But cleverness is also used for good works. *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim*. [Prayer]... Allahumma, O... [Prayer]. *Fatiha*.

<!-- AUG:ibrahim-2015 lecture 20 -->

Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. *Wa ammā alladhīna kafarū fa uʿadhdhibuhum ʿadhāban shadīdan fī al-dunyā wa-al-ākhirah wa mā lahum min nāṣirīn.* (And as for those who disbelieved, I will punish them with a severe punishment in this world and the Hereafter, and they will have no helpers.) Those who committed *kufr* (disbelief), I will give them a severe punishment in this world and the Hereafter, and they will have no helper. Yes. This is the translation of the verse of the Noble Qur'an, *Fa ammā alladhīna kafarū fa uʿadhdhibuhum ʿadhāban shadīdan fī al-dunyā wa-al-ākhirah wa mā lahum min nāṣirīn.* (And as for those who disbelieved, I will punish them with a severe punishment in this world and the Hereafter, and they will have no helpers.) In this, it is said that those who commit *kufr*... So *kufr* is a different thing, and *shirk* is a different thing. The explanation is ongoing that *shirk* refers to those people in the time of the Prophet, the *mushrikīn* (polytheists), whom you brought into Islam. But by *kafarū*, it is meant to conceal, to cover. This happened in the time of Maulana 'Ali, that Janab Prophet appointed someone as his *khalīfa* (caliph), and they finished him. And they committed *kufr*. These are not *mushrikīn*, but rather, after coming into Islam, they concealed his rank. *Fa ammā alladhīna kafarū* (And as for those who disbelieved), they committed *kufr*. *Kafara* means to conceal. And for them is a severe punishment. Which one? It is in this world as well, and in the Hereafter as well. *Wa mā lahum min nāṣirīn* (And they will have no helpers). And they have no helper. Meaning, we think that they have a helper, but that is not the case. Rather, in the Hereafter, they will receive a severe punishment.

Meaning, the one whom the Imam of the Time established (*qā'im*), those who deny him will be cast into severe punishment. Meaning, first they will be left in the darkness of misguidance and ignorance, and then they will be afflicted with the real punishment. The punishment of the seven levels (*darajāt*). After fulfilling the lesser punishment (*ʿadhāb adnā*), they will have to suffer the most severe punishment of the Hereafter in the station of *ṣakhrah*.

Now, this explanation was just given, that how will those who do such things be punished? First, they will be left in darknesses. In the darkness within that. They will be left in darkness. Darkness means that today, all of Islam is afflicted with darkness, and they do not understand who is great. They consider every single person great and are wandering astray. Then, a real punishment will be given to them. When will that be? Seven levels (*darkāt*). There are seven levels. The first level is of human form. The second is of the chimpanzee and monkey, and so on. The third is of the bear. The fourth is of the beast. The fifth is reptiles. The sixth is birds, and the seventh is *nawās* and *ma'dan* (minerals). They are currently passing through these seven levels. Look, they will have to suffer it. He says, after fulfilling the punishment of the seven levels, the *ʿadhāb adnā* (lesser punishment). So this means that we who are sitting here... this book was written 250 years ago, and Islam came 1400 years ago. So what happened? They have already suffered the lesser punishment, or are suffering it. And their most severe punishment, which one will it be? The punishment of *ṣakhrah*. That which is below, in Patal (the netherworld). Hell is below, under the earth, and there they will have to suffer the punishment. For how long? 16,000 crore years. One *kawr-i-a'ẓam*. The duration of one *kawr-i-a'ẓam* is when they will... do it.

Alright. *Wa ammā alladhīna āmanū...* Now, in contrast to that is *āmanū* (those who believed). *Kafarū* came, and those people who brought faith... *Wa ammā alladhīna āmanū wa ʿamilū al-ṣāliḥāt fa yuwaffīhim ujūrahum wa-Allāhu lā yuḥibbu al-ẓālimīn.* (And as for those who believed and did righteous deeds, He will give them their rewards in full. And Allah does not love the wrongdoers.) And those who brought faith and did good deeds, their reward will be given in full. And Allah does not hold the wrongdoers as friends. i.e., those who accepted the *Waṣī* (successor) and he appointed a *khalīfa* (caliph) in his own place, their efforts will not go to waste. They will be given a good reward with exaltations (*tā'īdāt*) and the raising of ranks (*marātib*). And Maulana Imam does not like those who are ungrateful for the truth (*ḥaqq-nā-shinās*). This is understood. That those who... accept the *Waṣī*. In contrast to this are those who committed *kufr*. Those who commit *kufr* will not accept the *Waṣī*. So the level of *kufr* is not accepting the *Waṣī*. *Shirk* happened in the time of the Prophet. All the battles of the Prophet – Uhud, Khandaq, Khaybar, Badr – all these were against the *mushrikīn* (polytheists) of Makkah. But what happened afterwards, in the time of Maulana 'Ali, were the battle of Nahrawan, the battle of Jamal, and the battle of Siffin. In Siffin, Mu'awiyah came forward. In Jamal, 'A'ishah came. These battles that happened, after bringing them into Islam, after they accepted Islam, they then stood in opposition to 'Ali. So this is called... these things that happened later, were a war against the *kāfirūn* (disbelievers). The first ones were against the *mushrikīn*. There is a difference between the two.

*Dhālika natlūhu ʿalayka min al-āyāt wa-al-dhikr al-ḥakīm.* (This is what We recite to you of the verses and the wise reminder.) This We recite to you from the verses and the Wise Qur'an. i.e., this which We are relating to you is being related in explanation of the ranks of the *Nuṭafā'* (Prophets). And the Owner of the Imamate... his rank is also being explained, which is very strong, and His call is also firm.

I am explaining that all these *Nuṭafā'* must also be adorned, and the Imams must also be... The Imam is in a hidden state, and the prophets come by His command. They don't come by the prophet's command. Our Prophet's rank is different. But in the time of Adam, in the time of Nuh, in the time of Ibrahim... who made Ibrahim? The Imam of that time, Janab Salih. Who made Musa? Maulana 'Ut Salawatullah 'alayh. So the ones who make them are the Imams, and the ones who are made are the *nuṭafā'*, or prophets, or messengers, all of them. This is called *mustawdaʿ*. *Mustawdaʿ* means caretaker. All of them, from Adam until here. Today there are *du'āt* (missionaries); in that era, they were called prophets. Zakariyya, Sham'un, Daniyal, Shu'ayb, all these are among the *mustawda'īn*. And the Imam... Maulana Hasan and so on... and before that, the progeny of Maulana Isma'il. Meaning, Janab Ibrahim's son, Isma'il. Qaydar, Nabat, Salama, all of these are A'immah (Imams). And they are of a very high rank. And by their command, the prophets of the Banu Isra'il came. Who? Maulana Ishaq and his progeny, Ya'qub. After Ya'qub, Yusuf. After Yusuf, Ayyub. All of these are *mustawda'īn*, who hold a lesser rank. But their names came because it is the period of *sitr* (concealment). So in the period of *sitr*, you should remember that the sun does not appear. Rather, the stars shine. In the same way, this is a time of night in terms of our *dīn* (religion), and in it, who are shining? These are prophets... *du'āt*. It is their time. When the time of *kashf* (unveiling) comes, it will come in a few years. And when will that be? When the *ẓuhūr* (appearance) of the Imam happens. *Allāhumma 'ajjil farajahu.* (O Allah, hasten his relief). We pray that the Imam's *ẓuhūr* comes soon. Because the world has become so corrupt that in the entire period of *sitr*, we are at the very bottom. We are at the bottom right now. Just as garbage collects at the bottom, in the same way, the condition of the world today is that everyone is in a garbage-like state. So now we are just waiting for the Qa'im to come, and whomever He has to punish, He will punish, and whomever He has to reward (*jazā'*), He will reward.

*Inna mathala ʿĪsā ʿinda Allāhi ka-mathali Ādama khalaqahu min turābin thumma qāla lahu kun fa-yakūn.* (Indeed, the example of Jesus to Allah is like that of Adam. He created him from dust; then He said to him, "Be," and he was.) Indeed, the example of 'Isa in the sight of Allah is like that of Adam. He created them from clay, then said, "Be," and he was. i.e., in the sight of the Imam, the station of Hazrat 'Isa is like that of Adam. That he too was given progress from the rank of the *ma'dhūnīn* and raised to the rank of *Nāṭiq*. The breath of spirit was blown into him, so by the support of Allah, he became a *Nāṭiq*.

Now, the explanation that came earlier in *Alif-Lām-Mīm* and Surah al-Baqarah, the whole story of Adam... who was Adam? He was a human, whose name was 'Abdullah, 'Abdullah bin Tanjum. And this Janab Adam, in the court of Maulana Hunayd, was one of the *ḥudūd* (ranks), a *ḥadd*. And at that time, he was created from clay and water. This common story that is narrated, that he was created from clay and water... How can one be made from clay and water, brother? If Adam was made of clay and water, then we, the children of Adam, are also human. Why aren't we made from that? We have flesh, skin, bone. Why is this? This means that he... by "water and clay," it is meant that the knowledge of the *sharī'ah* is called clay in the terminology, in the *ta'bīr* (interpretation). And water is the *ta'wīlī* knowledge. You are reading the book of *ta'wīl*. So by teaching him *ta'wīl* and that, they prepared a student. Whom? Janab Adam. And after that, the Imam... the Imam of that time, Maulana Hunayd *salawatullah 'alayh*, He blew his spirit into him. "Blew his spirit" means He gave him *tā'īdāt* (spiritual support). When he was given support, then his rank increased. So from where did his rank increase? He was a *mukāsir* and a *ma'dhūn*, meaning he had the rank of a *manṣūb*, Janab Adam. He was raised higher and higher until he became a *Nāṭiq* of the time. The one who started the world was him. So Janab Adam is the one who started the world, and the one who ends it is the Qa'im. Always, the period will start with Adam and end with the Qa'im. This is the method of the *da'wah*.

Now, remember this, a new thing. That Adam was in Sarandib, in Sri Lanka. Sri Lanka, everyone knows. Adam's Peak is because of that. That Adam was born there. So the Adam of our *kawr* was also there. He was in Sarandib. And what was that? It is called *qiṣṣah ibtidā'iyyah* (the story of the beginning). So the original Adam is that one. Because he is also starting a period. I have told you that the one who starts every period is an Adam, and the one who ends every period is a Qa'im.

...and was made to perform the service of prophethood. With the support of the Imam and the elixir of substances, 'Isa went from where to where in an instant. No one can even imagine it. This act is, in reality, *kun fa-yakūn* (Be, and it is).

*Al-ḥaqqu min rabbika fa lā takun min al-mumtarīn.* (The truth is from your Lord, so do not be among the doubters.) The truth is from your Lord, so do not have any doubt in it. i.e., this incident is from your Lord. It is a true and real sign. Do not have any doubt in it.

Now, this... Adam's... it is being told that just like Adam, 'Isa's birth also happened. It means that he too progressed from a lower rank, just as Adam progressed. Janab 'Isa. And I mentioned about both of them yesterday, that Janab Musa and Janab 'Isa... all these prophets... and Adam. The rank of these three is lesser. Nuh, Ibrahim, and Janab Prophet's is greater. And in one terminology, six *Nuṭafā'* have passed: Adam, Nuh, Ibrahim, Musa, 'Isa, and Janab Prophet, six. So the first three, we call them *Ābā'* (Fathers). You must remember this. The first three: Adam, Nuh, Ibrahim. They are the ones who started the world, so they are in the place of fathers, and they are great. And the *Abnā'* (Sons) are who? Musa, 'Isa, and Janab Prophet. So this is the system we have, that the *Nuṭafā'* come one after another. And according to the *Nuṭafā'*, the periods are divided. We will never explain in terms of the Imam, that this is Maulana Isma'il's period, or Janab Hasan's period, or Husayn's. Rather, it will always be explained according to the *Nuṭafā'*. The period of Musa, the period of 'Isa. These incidents that have passed, that in Greece, philosophers passed away: Arastu, Jalinoos, Buqrat, and Suqrat. All these were in the time of Janab Musa. So all these people... so we will call it the period of 'Isa... the period of Musa. You don't take the name of the Imams. Why? Because it is the period of *sitr*, so in this, the prophets will be mentioned more, and the Imams will be kept hidden. This is the thing. You remember this. And in this, those like Suqrat were great scholars. They are called the first teachers of the world, Suqrat and Arastu. Arastu was in the time of Alexander the Great. So what is this? They were so great that they did not come under the *sharī'ah* of Musa. They did not come under Musa. This is a special point. That they were higher than Musa in rank. That is why they did not accept Musa. Because in the sight of Allah, their rank is very great. Whose? These who were the philosophers. Suqrat, Buqrat, and these people.

*Fa-man ḥājjaka fīhi min baʿdi mā jāʾaka min al-ʿilmi fa-qul taʿālaw nadʿu abnāʾanā wa abnāʾakum wa nisāʾanā wa nisāʾakum wa anfusanā wa anfusakum thumma nabtahil fa-najʿal laʿnata Allāhi ʿalā al-kādhibīn.* (Then whoever argues with you about it after [this] knowledge has come to you - say, "Come, let us call our sons and your sons, our women and your women, ourselves and yourselves, then supplicate earnestly [together] and invoke the curse of Allah upon the liars.") So, those who argue with you about 'Isa after knowledge has come, say to them, "Forget the arguments. Come, let us call our sons..."

Now this is the mention of *mubāhalah*. *Mubāhalah* happened at a special time, in the time of the Prophet. That the Christians... and the Jews... Christians, rather. And... his name is also given, that his name was 'Atiq and some other names. And they said, "We will have a confrontation with each other, between Islam and Christianity." This is called *mubāhalah*. The Prophet said, "Fine. You do one thing. You bring your men, we will bring our men. You bring your women, we will bring our women. You bring your children, we will bring our children." This *abnā'anā wa abnā'akum wa nisā'anā wa nisā'akum*, this is that same verse. *Wa anfusanā wa anfusakum*. "Fine," they said. The next morning, when the Prophet arrived, behind the Prophet stood 'Ali, Fatima, and on the sides, Hasan and Husayn. So for children, he took Hasan and Husayn. For women, there was only Fatima, Sayyidatna Fatima. And 'Ali was in the place of the self (*nafs*). When they came forward, at that time, from there, those three or four people... from that time, they saw from a distance. And such was the light of the Prophet, such a *nūr* was radiating, that they immediately said, "No, we cannot fight them." So the *mubāhalah* was finished. So this is a special verse, that the *mubāhalah* happened between the Christians and Islam. What? We will gather, and curse each other. Whoever the curse falls upon, he will be afflicted with punishment. But the turn did not even come.

"...call your sons, we will call our sons. We will call our women, you call your women. We will call our souls, you call your souls. Then let us raise our hands high to Allah and pray that the curse of Allah be upon the liars." i.e., when the Christians descended to stubbornness and said that 'Isa was not a *mustawdaʿ* but a *mustaqarr* Imam, then you made them agree to do *mubāhalah*. Because for this, you should bring your sons... for this very purpose, bring your sons, women, and souls to the field. And in the same way, call their close relatives for the confrontation. And then, raising their hands, make them ready to say this curse, that whoever is lying, may he be the target of Allah's anger and curse.

*Hāshiyah* (marginal note). This verse was revealed concerning the Christians of Najran. Now, in Najran at that time, there were Christians. And even so much so that they had a church there that was built to rival the Ka'bah. In Najran. I have seen it, from a distance. It was behind the mountains. In Najran, one was built. And the story of Abraha... who was Abraha? He was a king. A king, Abraha, who came before Maulana 'Abd al-Muttalib. He couldn't come before 'Abd al-Muttalib, 'Abd al-Muttalib's rank is very high. His *ḥijāb*, Buhayra al-Rahib, he challenged him. Who? Abraha. This... *Alam tara kayfa fa'ala rabbuka bi-aṣḥāb al-fīl.* (Have you not considered how your Lord dealt with the companions of the elephant?) The companions of the elephant, what is described, that surah was revealed on this. That Abraha said, "I will..." When... I told you that in Najran, they had built a church, and they used to worship there. So some Muslims, or someone, went and broke it. So Abraha got angry and said that since this has been broken, I will demolish the Ka'bah. He came for the Ka'bah. Riding what? An elephant. So his commander, his name was Shimr, Shimr bin Maqsud. He came out. And with him was an army. They came, riding elephants, to demolish the Ka'bah. At that time, a strange incident happened. And it was that it was the time of Maulana 'Abd al-Muttalib. Maulana 'Abd al-Muttalib's *ḥijāb* was working. Who? Buhayra al-Rahib. He has a very high rank. And when... what happened? They were preparing to attack, and at that moment, a cloud rose from the sea. The sea would be that of Jeddah. The sea is there, not in Makkah. So from Jeddah, a cloud rose. And it seemed as if black clouds were approaching. When they came closer, it was found that they were birds, and in whose... mouth... in whose beak was one stone, and in their two claws were two stones. So every bird had three stones. And they came and dropped... the first stone that fell, it fell on the head of Shimr bin Maqsud and came out from the bottom. And he was finished right there. In this way, each bird killed three. And thus, he was defeated. So, to vent his anger, he started a lot of looting in Makkah. Who? Abraha's forces. When the soldiers started doing this, at that time... many camels that belonged to Maulana 'Abd al-Muttalib were stolen, usurped. When they were usurped, at that time, Maulana 'Abd al-Muttalib went to the court of Abraha and said, "My camels, which your soldiers have seized, return them to me." At that time, it is mentioned in the *sīrah* (biography) that Abraha, who was the king, started laughing. He said, "'Abd al-Muttalib, you are worried about your camels? We have come to demolish the Ka'bah. Protect it! What have you come for your camels for?" Maulana 'Abd al-Muttalib said, "The camels, the female camels, they are my property, that's why I have come. And the Ka'bah is Allah's property. He is its protector, He is. Allah will protect it." A special incident happened. And after that, the incident of Abraha happened in this way.

This verse was revealed concerning the Christians of Najran, when they came to debate with the Messenger of the Arabs about Hazrat 'Isa. They wanted to prove 'Isa to be the son of Allah and the Messenger of Allah a liar. After a long discussion, the Messenger ended the back-and-forth by saying that no conclusion seems to be emerging, so the final course of action is that both parties tomorrow morning, along with our families whose mention is in the verse, should gather in the field and, raising our hands high, pray to Allah, "O Allah, curse the liars." The wise among the Christians thought that if this incident... if Muhammad is true, then we will be destroyed. His certainty and self-confidence and the signs were indicating that he must be the Messenger of Allah. Therefore, they refrained. They did not come for the *mubāhalah*. The Messenger, with him as "sons," brought the two Hasans (*Ḥasanayn*). As "women," his daughter Fatima al-Zahra. And as "souls," 'Ali. And the fifth was he himself. There was no one else. These were the *Panjatan Pāk* (Five Pure Ones), and they were truthful too. That is why they had come to curse the liars.

It is understood. It's clear. Yes. *Innā hādhā lahuwa al-qaṣaṣ al-ḥaqq wa mā min ilāhin illā Allāh wa inna Allāha lahuwa al-ʿazīz al-ḥakīm.* (Indeed, this is the true narration. And there is no deity except Allah. And indeed, Allah is the Exalted in Might, the Wise.) Indeed, these are the true events, and there is no deity worthy of worship except Allah, and indeed, Allah is the Peerless, the Wise. i.e., the truth and the statement based on veracity is this: that besides 'Ali, there is no *mustaqarr*.

The explanation that has happened before, that 'Isa is a *mustawdaʿ*, not a *mustaqarr*. So *mustaqarr* has a separate system. *Mustaqarr* means son after father, one after another. That is a chain that will continue until the Day of Judgment, and there will never be an interruption in it. *Wa lan tajida li-sunnati Allāhi tabdīlā.* (And you will never find in the way of Allah any change.) It is a way of Allah in which there will be no change. This is a special explanation. That the Imamate can never be broken. Never. But the *mustawdaʿ* changes. And these people, the Christians, they thought that 'Isa is an Imam. An Imam. And in this, I also explained yesterday, that in that era, Saint Paul created all the corruption. Saint Paul, he was... in the last days of the period of Musa, he was among the *ḥudūd*. And he did what? He made a prophet named 'Uzayr the son of Allah. He was popularizing this. But at that same time, the fame of 'Isa began to spread. Janab 'Isa came and brought a great religion. So at that time, he got scared and straightaway entered Christianity. Saint Paul. After entering, he went inside there, and his desire was not suppressed. That just as he could not make 'Uzayr, now I will make 'Isa the son of God. So in our case too, when... in the end of Musa's [period] they came, likewise in the last of 'Isa's period, among the *ḥudūd*, there was also the First Caliph. He also had this desire, that after you, O Messenger of Allah, I should be made [caliph]. This was his assumption.

Besides 'Ali, there is no *mustaqarr*. 'Ali is the one in whose gnosis the intellects of the wise are bewildered. And he has no equal. And He is the Wise in His actions. That's it. One verse.

*Fa-in tawallaw fa-inna Allāha ʿalīmun bil-mufsidīn.* (And if they turn away - then indeed, Allah is Knowing of the corrupters.) And if they turn their backs, then Allah knows well the creators of corruption. i.e., if they deny the *walāyat* (authority) of 'Ali, then there is no concern. 'Ali, in whose gnosis all are helpless and perplexed, He is well aware of the conspiracy of these corrupters.

Look, this book is a book of *ta'wīl*. So what you heard in the beginning, the translation, that is the literal one. Meaning, the book of translation... the Qur'an. But from "i.e.", the meaning is the *ta'wīl*. What is the inner meaning? That which has been written for us, it is the great grace of the *du'āt*, that they have opened this up. Otherwise, by just reading the translation, one doesn't understand anything these days. In the translation, there are many such things that you will become perplexed, thinking what is this... what will we understand from its translation? But if its *ta'wīl* is known, then its greatness is known.

*Qul yā ahla al-kitābi taʿālaw ilā kalimatin sawāʾin baynanā wa baynakum allā naʿbuda illā Allāha wa lā nushrika bihi shayʾan wa lā yattakhidha baʿḍunā baʿḍan arbāban min dūni Allāhi fa-in tawallaw fa-qūlū ishhādū bi-annā muslimūn.* (Say, "O People of the Scripture, come to a word that is equitable between us and you - that we will not worship except Allah and not associate anything with Him and not take one another as lords instead of Allah." But if they turn away, then say, "Bear witness that we are Muslims [submitting to Him].") Say, "O People of the Book! Come, let us all gather upon one word, that we worship none but Allah and do not associate any partner with Him. And let us not make beings like ourselves gods besides Allah. Then, if they still turn their backs, then say, 'You be witnesses that we are Muslims, i.e., those who submit.'" i.e., O Muslims, come! Accept the *walāyat* and authority of 'Ali. And his person is the word of justice and fairness. In his person, neither commit excess nor deficiency. Do not associate anyone as a partner in his station. And do not make anyone from among yourselves an Imam. After such clear words, if the enemy still does not accept, then O followers of 'Ali... you say this and... "We have accepted the *Waṣī*." Dissociate and break ties with them. Yes. That's it. It's finished. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim... [recites prayers]. Al-Fatihah. Fatihah. Fatihah.

<!-- AUG:ibrahim-2015 lecture 21 -->

Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. A'udhu billahi min ash-shaytan ir-rajim, Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. *Inna hadha lahuwa al-qasas al-haqq wa ma min ilahin illa Allah wa inna Allaha lahuwa al-'aziz al-hakim*. (Indeed, this is the true narration, and there is no god except Allah. And indeed, Allah is the Exalted in Might, the Wise).

Indeed, these are the true events. There is no deity besides Allah. And truly, Allah is the one with peerless wisdom. Meaning, the truthful and fact-based matter is that there is no *mustaqarr* (permanent one) besides Ali. Ali is the one in whose gnosis the intellects of the wise are bewildered. And he has no peer. And he is wise in his actions.

In the last discourse, it was stated that the chain of the *Imam-e-Mustaqarr* (Permanent Imam) is that Maulana Ali, Salawatullah 'alayh, the son of Maulana Abu Talib, is the successor and has been appointed. Thus, in relation to a *Natiq* (Prophet), there is a *Wasi* (legatee), and the *Wasi-e-Paighambar* (legatee of the Prophet) is you. But the chain of Imams that has been continuing through eras and cycles, that is not going to be broken. And the *Imam-e-Mustaqarr* is Maulana Ali, after Janab Abu Talib.

Now, in between this, Janab Abu Talib made the prophet a prophet. So, there is a system of the *da'wah* (mission) that for every *Natiq*, there is always a *Wasi*, and from the progeny of the *Wasi*, the *Imamat* (Imamate) continues. So this has always been the case, of this type, that with every *Natiq*, this very thing has happened. And Janab Ali is also the *Wasi-e-Paighambar*, but he is the *Imam-e-Mustaqarr*. It is very difficult to recognize his ranks. Because a... a... a... very lofty thing... we are incapable of understanding it. That your gnosis, what is it physically? What is it spiritually? What is it luminously? It is very profound. That what are you in terms of the *Muhtajib* (the veiled one)? What are you in terms of the *Hijab* (the veil)? What are you in terms of being the Prophet's *Wasi*? The things the Prophet received, he received them due to your trustworthiness (*amanat-dari*). The trust (*amanat*) was for you, and that *amanat* was fulfilled, and that is why the Prophet was called *Amin* (the trustworthy one). The *Amin* and *Sadiq* (truthful one) he was called was for this reason, that you delivered the *amanat*.

So, where the *amanat* is delivered, he is the *Amin-e-Sadiq*. You were both *Sadiq* and *Amin*. And you are the *Wasi-e-Paighambar*. So, in terms of being a *Wasi*, you are of a lower rank than Janab-e-Paighambar (the Prophet). But your *Muhtajib* is the *'Ayn-e-'Azima* (Essence of Grandeur), and over here, the Prophet's... those who are the *Muhtajib*... they are named *Ahmad*. And the name is *Mīm-e-Maryamah*. They are of a higher station. So, in that regard, the Prophet's... the chain that is there, is that of Ahmad, Salawatullah 'alayh. He is of a lower rank than the *'Ayn-e-'Azima*. But you... in the heavens, the Prophet's name was Ahmad, and Muhammad, Salawatullah 'alayh, is the name that was given on earth. These are all subtle points that are to be understood in the religion.

The intellects are bewildered. And there is no peer to him, and he is *Hakim* (wise) in his actions.

*Wa in tawallaw fa inna Allaha 'alimun bi'l-mufsidin*. (But if they turn away, then indeed, Allah is Knowing of the corrupters). And if they turn away from it, then Allah knows the mischief-makers well. Meaning, if they deny the *wilayat* (guardianship) of Ali, then there is no need to worry. Ali, in whose gnosis all are helpless and perplexed, is well aware of the conspiracies of those mischief-makers.

*Qul ya ahl al-kitab ta'alaw ila kalimatin sawa'in baynana wa baynakum alla na'buda illa Allah wa la nushrika bihi shay'an wa la yattakhidha ba'duna ba'dan arbaban min dunillah fa in tawallaw fa qulu shhadu bi-anna muslimun*. (Say, "O People of the Scripture, come to a word that is equitable between us and you - that we will not worship except Allah and not associate anything with Him and not take one another as lords instead of Allah." But if they turn away, then say, "Bear witness that we are Muslims [submitting to Him]"). Say: O Ahl al-Kitab (People of the Scripture), come, let us all gather upon one *kalimah* (word), that we shall not worship anyone besides Allah. And with Him, we shall not associate anyone as a partner. And we shall not make humans like ourselves lords besides Allah. Still, if they... if they still turn their backs, then say, "You be witness that we are indeed Muslims," meaning, those who submit.

Meaning, O you who... Now this is the translation of the verse. That we should... on one... gather upon one *kalimah*. There are Christians, there are Jews, there are all of them. But what is the real meaning? Meaning...

Meaning, O Muslims who recite the *kalimah*, come and accept the *wilayat* (guardianship) and *ta'at* (obedience) of Ali. And his being is the *kalimat-e-adl-o-insaf* (word of justice and fairness). Regarding his being, neither commit *ghuluw* (exaggeration) nor fall short. Do not associate anyone as a partner in his station. And do not make anyone from among yourselves an Imam. Even after such clear words, if the enemy does not accept, then O follower of the *Wasi*, you, by saying this... you, by saying that "we have accepted the *Wasi*," distance yourselves and declare disassociation (*barat*) from them.

Just as today it is the practice (*shewa*) of the believers to remain silent out of *taqiyyah* (precautionary dissimulation) and not to discuss much. This is for the reason that there is a command in the *Dar-us-Satr* (Period of Concealment) to always keep the *batin* (inner reality) hidden until the Imam's command is received. Now, there are many examples of this, many things are there, that today... today, books of *haqaiq* (realities) are being printed and are being distributed among people, and all these things are happening. But it is happening wrongly. It is not with the Imam's permission. There was a time, 70 years ago from today, when *Da'im ul-Islam* was not given to anyone outside. When Hazrat Asif Fyzee wanted to print it, he was denied permission. He said, "Until I get the command from my elder," at that time it was Sayyidna Taher Saifuddin, "until then, we will not give it." But he wrote in his introduction (*dibacha*), when he... he printed it, it had very good Arabic, all these things were there. But the point is that the body (*jism*) being good is not enough; the soul (*ruh*) must be good. The mind (*zehan*) must be good. So when he printed it, he wrote in it that "I did not get it from the Majlis-e-Sulaymani Jawwad." And I... from Surat, from Burhanpur, from there I acquired it and printed this. So he printed it, did well, and it came into the hands of the people. But that was wrong, because it was not with the Imam's permission.

So the person who is below, the *Da'i*, the *Mansub*, whoever they are, one should act upon their command. That did not happen.

*Ya ahl al-kitab lima tuhajjuna fi Ibrahim wa ma unzilat at-tawrat wa'l-injil illa min ba'dihi afala ta'qilun*. (O People of the Scripture, why do you argue about Ibrahim while the Tawrat and the Injil were not revealed until after him? Then will you not reason?). O Ahl al-Kitab, why do you argue about Ibrahim, whereas the Tawrat and the Injil were not revealed except after him? Do you not even understand this much? Now this is a strange thing, that they used to debate about the Tawrat and the Injil. And they would bring Ibrahim into it. They used to create disputes in between, concerning Ibrahim. Whereas, God Almighty says that Ibrahim came much earlier. The Tawrat and Injil came much later. Christianity and Judaism came much later. Why are you doing this? The Injil... was not revealed except after them. You don't understand. You are sitting there, bringing up old tales. Whereas, that is wrong.

Meaning, O Muslims who recite the *kalimah*... Now look, his... the principles (*usul*), the laws of Shari'at (*qawanin-e-shari'at*), and the ranks of division (*maratib-e-taqsim*), about *istida'* (deposition) and *istikhrar* (permanence), why do you argue? Now, what is the meaning of this? That Janab Ibrahim, 'alayhissalatu wassalam, what do we say? He is a Prophet. Like Adam, Nuh, Ibrahim, all of these are *Nutqa* (Speaking Prophets). But Zakariyya, Yahya, we accept every one of them. Because they were prophets, we also accept them. The Prophet said that all the 124,000 prophets who came, all of them are what? They are sent by God. We also believe that. But among them, there is a difference between *mustaqarr* (permanent) and *mustawda'* (temporary). In every one. Now, this Ibrahim... what do they think of Janab Ibrahim? That just as Musa alayhissalam, 'Isa alayhissalam, Zakariyya alayhissalam, all of them, big and small, are mixed together. Whereas God Almighty says that *fad-dalna ba'dahum 'ala ba'd* (We have preferred some of them over others). So what is the rank of Ibrahim? Janab Ibrahim's rank was the rank of *khullat* (close friendship). You demonstrated complete loyalty. And *wa ja'alna Ibrahim imama* (and We made Ibrahim an Imam). We made Ibrahim an Imam. So was Ibrahim an Imam or a Prophet?

What was he? Your *Hijab* (veil) that was there, his name was also Ibrahim, who was put into the fire. It has been clearly written that the Ibrahim who is mentioned in the Quran, "Ibrahim, Ibrahim," you are not being addressed, Ibrahim Khalilullah. Rather, he was a *Da'i*, your *Hijab*. All the *du'at* are in the veils of the Imam. So Janab Ibrahim, who held the rank of *khullat* and was an *Imam-e-Mustaqarr*, there is no concern with him, but it was this *Da'i Ibrahim* who was put in the fire. And his name was also Ibrahim.

So the purpose... it is more necessary to understand *mustaqarr* and *mustawda'*. Now look, today there is a gathering. You see, who was the *mustawda'*? And did the *mustawda'* handle the succession or not? Did he show trustworthiness or not? Da'ud Qutub. You have just heard the 6 o'clock sermon of the Hazrat, and you will hear it now too. That he was designated as a *mustawda'*. He was not *mustaqarr*. You were not made the owner (*malik*). But the Prophet... Da'ud bin 'Ajab Shah appointed as his successor Janab Yusuf bin Sulayman. At that time, things changed. Before that, he used to write *khatam bosi* (a show of subservience). In the majlis... if you read the *Sirat* of the Pir, you will understand. All the letters that used to come from Da'ud Qutub, he would write at the bottom, "Accept my *khatam bosi*." Does one write *khatam bosi* to an elder or to a junior? Da'ud Qutub used to write it. Because he was very old. When Sayyidna Jalal passed away, and Sayyidna Yusuf was appointed, even at that time he had a desire that I should become the head. He was old. But the greatness of Sayyidna Jalal was such that he was made the head. And for only four months he remained alive. And he received the *nass* (designation) 13 days before his death. Within those 13 days, he did whatever work needed to be done, he did it all. According to the *du'at*. Whoever had to be raised, he raised. Whoever had to be lowered, he lowered. You read his *Sirat*. This is the greatness of Sayyidna Jalal. And after that, Da'ud bin 'Ajab Shah was appointed. At that time too, you had the intention... he, that is, Da'ud Qutub, that I am worthy of being the *Da'i-ul-Mutlaq*. When this did not happen, then finally, the great *fitna* (schism) was at the time of Sayyidna Sulayman. Da'ud bin 'Ajab Shah, throughout his life, kept indicating that Sayyidna Sulayman, what rank does he hold? He was a child, and he is writing that he is a *fardand-e-dilband* (beloved son). And he is the leader of children and those who obey. And there is a whole list of his accolades (*khatabat*). How he used to describe him.

Anyway, the point is that whether he was a *Mustaqarr Imam* or a Prophet, why do you people argue about Ibrahim? But what is he? He is a *Mustaqarr Imam*. He is the son of Janab Salih. And Salih's lineage has been continuing from the beginning. This era, this era of the *Mustaqarr Imam*, that after the father, the son, and *wahidan 'an wahidin wa'lladhina ba'dih* (one after another). This rope will never be broken. And this is the *Habl-ul-Mamdud* (the extended rope). And from where does it start? From one *kawr* (cycle) to another *kawr*. And even if *kawrs* pass, the chain remains. *Kawrs*... whether it is a *dawr* (era) or a *kawr*, or *athar* (traces) or *ambar* (heaps), or *adwar* (periods), in all of them, this chain exists. *Wa lan tajida li-sunnatillahi tabdila* (And you will never find in the way of God any change). This is a *sunnah* (way) of Allah. This is not the *sunnah* of the Prophet, that today people change it, saying this is the Prophet's *sunnah*, this is how to become a *hafiz*, this is how to become that, they are doing this. That was Allah's *sunnah*. That Allah established a *sunnah*, and He cannot change it, it's final. The matter is closed. That after the father, it must be the son. The son of a *Da'i* does not necessarily become a *Da'i*. Someone else's son does not become one. If they are capable, they will become one. But the son of an Imam must become the Imam. This is our specific principle of the *da'wah*.

The point is that their claim was that Ibrahim divided the *Amr-e-Imamat* (Matter of the Imamate) into two parts after him. One part was named *Imamat-e-Mustaqarr* (Permanent Imamate), and the other's name was *Imamat-e-Mustawda'* (Deputized Imamate). And they had heard that Muhammad, alayhissalam, was merely sent for the renewal of the *Shari'at* (law) of Ibrahim and was sent as a *mab'uth* (one sent forth). And are *mustawda'* (deputized). They have no share in the *Imamat-e-Mustaqarra* (Permanent Imamate). So it was said, do they not even understand this much, that the *A'imma-e-Mustaqarrin* (Permanent Imams) appointed the *Mustawda'in* (Deputized Imams) only after the death of Ibrahim? Thus, every *mustaqarr* (permanent one), by his command, appoints a *mustawda'* for his service. He... he... This matter has been going on for many years. When Maulana Ibrahim came at a special juncture, when 3000 years had passed for the world, at that time Janab Ibrahim appeared. And Janab Ibrahim had two sons, Ishaq and Isma'il. Isma'il's rank was very high; he is an *Imam-e-Mustaqarr*. And Janab Ishaq was a *mustawda'*. He held the rank of a prophet, a *mustawda'*. Now, what rank he held... you read the *Bahs-ul-Ukhra* and you will understand. But Janab Isma'il was told that in the future, if you need to take work, take it from the progeny of Ishaq. And Ishaq alayhissalam was made bound, that you should obey, you and your progeny, Janab Isma'il. And this continued for 3000 years. In Lebanon and Syria and over there in the Middle East, all of Ishaq's progeny continued to work. The reward for the service of this progeny was given at the time of the Prophet. And the reward the Prophet received was because the progeny of Janab Ishaq had served the Imam with great sincerity. The Imam of every time. And it wasn't just one Imam. They continued on. After Janab Isma'il came Faizar, came Nawat, came Salam, all of these, as many as there are... all of them were served by the progeny of Ishaq. Whether they were Ayyub, or Yusuf, or Ya'qub, all of them worked by the command of the Imam. So remember that in that era, just as the Prophet used to work by the command of the Imam, in today's era, the *du'at* do. The *du'at*. When is this from? Now, you will hear in the sermon that after a thousand years of the Prophet, as the era changed, the era of the *Qa'im* began, and at that time the ranks of the *du'at* were elevated. And how much they were elevated, you will hear, Insha'Allah.

They wanted the Prophet, alayhissalam, to grant them a portion of the rank of *istida'* (being a deputy). Whereas this was not the Prophet's job.

*Ha antum ha'ula'i hajajtum fima lakum bihi 'ilmun fa-lima tuhajjuna fima laysa lakum bihi 'ilmun wallahu ya'lamu wa antum la ta'lamun*. (Here you are - those who have argued about that of which you have knowledge, so why do you argue about that of which you have no knowledge? And Allah knows, while you do not know). O people, you are the very ones who have just argued about that of which you had knowledge. So now... about a *shay'* (thing) about which you have no knowledge, why do you argue? And Allah knows, and you do not know.

Meaning, O people, you, after knowing everything about Hazrat Ibrahim, still continued to argue, and your knowledge could not benefit you at all. So now, what benefit will you get from arguing about an unknown *shay'* (thing)? Yesterday I explained that *shay'* in one sense means a believer. *Shay'*. Esoterically (*ta'wilan*), *shay'* means... And *nisf-e-shay'* (half a thing)... what is *nisf-e-shay'*? I explained the day before yesterday. *Nisf-e-shay'* is a hypocrite. And *la-shay'* (nothing) is a disbeliever. Now, you see that in Urdu too, they say *lasha* (corpse), which is a body from which the soul has departed, that is a *lasha*. So *la-shay'* is this.

Meaning, O people, you, after knowing everything about Hazrat Ibrahim, still continued to argue, and your knowledge could not benefit you at all. So now, what benefit will you get from arguing about an unknown *shay'*? Meaning, regarding Ali the *Wasi*, you have no gnosis (*ma'rifat*) at all. Arguing and debating about him will yield no result. So, only He knows him well, you do not.

*Ma kana Ibrahim yahudiyyan wa la nasraniyan wa lakin kana hanifan musliman wa ma kana min al-mushrikin*. (Ibrahim was neither a Jew nor a Christian, but he was one inclining toward truth, a Muslim [submitting to Allah]. And he was not of the polytheists). Ibrahim was neither a Jew nor a Christian, but he was a sincere Muslim. And he was not among the polytheists. Meaning, Ibrahim was neither excessive nor deficient. He gave every rightful person their due right equally. He neither diminished nor exaggerated anyone. Rather, his successor, whom he appointed by God's command, he gave him everything in its entirety. In their actions, there was not even a trace of *shirk* (polytheism).

Here it is... they were not Jews either. God Almighty is stating that they were not Jews. You people are arguing that they were Jews and Christians. Jews and Christians came later. Why are you arguing about it? He was a Muslim. He was a Muslim. Now look, Muslim means one who embraces Islam, one who accepts, one who submits. Now this is the rank of Ibrahim, and in his time it was said that our shari'ats... this shari'at is a separate thing, din (religion) is a separate matter. The din of God is one from Adam until here. But the shari'at (law) has been changing. Adam's shari'at was different, Nuh's shari'at was different. Wherever there have been major junctures in the *da'wah*, there, after Adam, came Nuh alayhissalam, after some 1000 and some years. In between, many prophets came. Many small prophets came. But the work couldn't be done by them, so God Almighty sent a great prophet like Nuh. And when Nuh's shari'at was coming to an end, meaning it was the final period, at that time Janab Ibrahim came. In this way, according to the *Nutqa* (Speaking Prophets), the era is... the era of *sitr* (concealment) is divided, that the era of Ibrahim, the era of Musa, the era of 'Isa, these will be mentioned. The era of Isma'il, or let's say the era of Hasan or the era of Husayn, has never been mentioned. So this is a system that the era of *sitr* will always be according to the *Nutqa*. Now, a second point is that where there are lofty subjects in the *haqaiq* (realities), Yaqub al-Sijistani, may Allah bless him, says that we think that six shari'ats have passed: Adam, Nuh, Ibrahim, Musa, 'Isa, and Janab-e-Paighambar. So in this, three are fathers (*aba'*) and three are sons (*abna'*). Adam, Nuh, Ibrahim are called the *aba'*. And the *abna'* are Musa, 'Isa, and Janab-e-Paighambar. So now, he says that in reality, there were only three shari'ats. The latter three shari'ats came to expand upon the first three shari'ats. They came to renew them, to elaborate on them. That Adam's shari'at was... who did it? Musa. 'Isa's shari'at was... their... Janab Nuh's. But Ibrahim's shari'at, the one who expanded it was Janab-e-Paighambar. That is why you see that in our din, the rulings that we have, of various kinds, in them, there are many things that are from the shari'at of Ibrahim. Such as the matter of *zabihat* (ritual slaughter), the matter of *khatna* (circumcision), several such things that were in Ibrahim's shari'at have also come into our shari'at. Because he is in the ancestors of the Prophet, Janab Ibrahim Khalilullah.

*Inna awla an-nasi bi-Ibrahim lalladhina-ttaba'uhu wa hadha an-nabiyyu wa'lladhina amanu wallahu waliyyu al-mu'minin*. (Indeed, the people most worthy of Ibrahim are those who followed him, and this prophet, and those who believe. And Allah is the ally of the believers). Indeed, the people most close to Ibrahim were those who followed him, and then this Prophet is... is, and those who believed, and Allah is the guardian of the believers.

Meaning, among the people of the *da'wah*, the closest to Ibrahim are the *A'imma-e-Hadin* (the Guiding Imams). Now, what does "close" mean? Now, that is... but "close" means that he himself is a *Mustaqarr Imam*. His son is an Imam, and his son's son is an Imam. So this chain is remaining. You should not think that the prophets were close. The rank of Janab Lut is separate. The rank of Janab Ishaq is separate. The rank of Janab Isma'il is separate. And the rank of Janab Isma'il is very lofty, and he is an *Imam-e-Mustaqarr*. But Ishaq is a *mustawda'*. A *mustawda'* is one who is a caretaker. As he is commanded, he does. Just as the *du'at* do today, the prophets used to do in their time. But Lut's rank... you read the stories of Lut, and you read them, you will be astonished at what kind of events took place. But Janab Lut's rank itself was between Ishaq and Janab Isma'il. He had many things which... neither Ishaq had, nor Janab Isma'il. Rather, it was an intermediate one. He was in special gatherings, for thousands of years. They were gathered. It was a matter of events. We are reading a book of *ta'wil* (esoteric interpretation), so it is difficult to mention.

Meaning, among the people of the *da'wah*, the closest to Ibrahim are the *A'imma-e-Hadin*, who remained firm on the religion and continued to propagate the religion of Ibrahim. And then this Prophet, Muhammad Rasulullah, and those whom he appointed for the propagation of the *da'wah*. And Allah is the master of the believers (*mu'minin*). That's it.

Al-Fatihah.

<!-- AUG:ibrahim-2015 lecture 22 -->

A'udhu Billahi minash shaitanir rajim. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim.
Ma kana li basharin an yu'tiyahullahu al-kitaba wal-hukma wan-nubuwwata thumma yaqula lin-nasi kunu 'ibadan li min dunillah, wa lakin kunu rabbaniyyina bima kuntum tu'allimuna al-kitaba wa bima kuntum tadrusun. (It is not for a human [prophet] that Allah should give him the Scripture and authority and prophethood and then he would say to the people, 'Be servants to me rather than Allah.' Rather, [he would say], 'Be pious scholars of the Lord because of what you have taught of the Scripture and because of what you have studied.')
Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. It is not possible for any human that Allah gives him the book, wisdom, and prophethood, then he says to people, "Leave Allah and become my servants." But he will only say that since you teach the book and also read it, then through your own actions, you should become people of Allah.
Meaning, no *Mustawda'* (trustee) has the right that when the *Mawlana Imam al-Zaman* entrusts him with the *Rutba-e-Natiqiyyat* (rank of a Natiq), grants him authority (*hukumat*) in the *Da'wat* (mission), and makes him aware of wondrous sciences, he then says to his believing followers (*murids*), "I am the *Imam al-Mustaqarr* (Permanent Imam), and there is no other."
Rather, he will say to his *Hudud al-Da'wat* (ranks of the mission), "You, according to the book, as a person of high station (*sahib-e-maqam*), should keep clarifying the great rank (*martaba-e-'azim*) of the *Imam al-Zaman* to the people. And the real purpose of your teaching and instruction (*dars-o-tadris*) should be this, so that they do not make anyone else their Lord (*Rabb*)."
This is completely clear. It means that no *Mustawda'* has the right to go to the level of the *Mustaqarr*. And the *Imam al-Zaman*, to whom he entrusts the *Natiqiyyat*, he can entrust it. He is the master (*malik*). Remember this, that the master, meaning... the rank of the *Mustaqarr* is different. And any human... the translation here is from the Holy Qur'an, it is for a human. It is not possible for a human. So who is the human here? It is the *Mustawda'*. The *Mustawda'in* who are of a lower rank should not consider themselves to be of a higher rank. Meaning, they should not think that they have become the *Imam al-Mustaqarr*.
The *Mustawda'* does not have the right that the *Mawlana Imam al-Zaman*, to whom he entrusts the *Rutba-e-Natiqiyyat*, grants authority in the *Da'wat*, and makes him aware of wondrous information, then he tells his believing followers (*murids*), "I am the *Imam al-Mustaqarr*." This is absolutely clear.
And there is no other. Rather, he says to his *Hudud al-Da'wat* that you, according to the book, the person of high station... Every speaker should know that the *da'wat* being given is towards the *Imam al-Zaman*. It is not towards himself, nor towards others. It is just that the support (*ta'id*) comes from there, from the *Imam al-Zaman*. Support is received. The elder gives to the younger. He speaks only because of that support. And everyone must confess that "I am not saying it, I am being told from above, so I am saying it. I am the medium." Nothing happens on its own.
Wa la ya'murakum an tattakhidhu al-mala'ikata wan-nabiyyina arbaba. A ya'murukum bil-kufri ba'da idh antum muslimun? (And he would not order you to take the angels and prophets as lords. Would he order you to disbelief after you had submitted?)
And he will not give you this command, that you should consider the angels and prophets as your lord. He said, what... what will he, after making you a Muslim, obedient, give you a command of disbelief and denial?
Meaning, he cannot tell you that besides the *Imam al-Mustaqarr*, you should consider the *Hudud al-Da'wat* and the *Mustawda'in* (trustees) as the Lords of the Da'wah. Yes. Is it possible that the revelation (*wahi*) puts you on the path of guidance and then the same revelation misguides you?
Wa idh akhadhallahu mithaqan-nabiyyina lama ataytukum min kitabin wa hikmatin thumma ja'akum rasulun musaddiqun lima ma'akum la tu'minunna bihi wa la tansurunnahu. (And [recall] when Allah took the covenant of the prophets, [saying], "Whatever I give you of the Scripture and wisdom and then there comes to you a messenger confirming what is with you, you [must] believe in him and support him.")
And when Allah took a covenant from the prophets that "I will give you the Book and wisdom, then when a messenger comes to you who confirms the things that are with you, then you must believe in him and you must help him." Yes.
Meaning, the Exalted Creator (*Sabiq-i-Ta'ala*) took a covenant from the *Mustawda'in* that you have been told about the *Ma'rifat* (gnosis) of *Amir al-Mu'minin* and his sublime knowledge. You must remain steadfast on this. And then when that messenger comes to you who confirms these very things, then you must immediately accept him.
Steadfast (*sabit qadam*)... many places... the word *sabit qadam* comes. *Allahumma sabbit qadamayya* (O Allah, make my feet firm). People do *masah* (wiping) of the feet. *Sabit qadamayya*, *subut qadamayya*. This is... this too is a guidance, that we must remain steadfast, meaning we must be saved from *ghuluww* (exaggeration) and *quluww* (falling short). Neither is *ghuluww* good, nor is *quluww* good. *Qaaf* is one hundred, *ghayn* is one thousand. Between these two is five hundred, and for five hundred, *sabit qadam* has the letter *seen*. That we... Allah the Exalted... *sabbit qadamayya*. Or understand it as *Allahumma sabbitna 'ala ta'atillah wa ta'ati rasulihi wa ta'ati imam zamanih wa hudud dinih* (O Allah, keep us firm in obedience to Allah, and obedience to His Messenger, and obedience to the Imam of his time, and the ranks of his religion). You should confess all these things.
Yes. ...when that messenger comes to you, who confirms these very things, then you must immediately accept him. Do not deny it, and you must help him in the propagation of the message (*tabligh-e-risalat*).
Qala a'aqrartum wa akhadhtum 'ala dhalikum isri? Qalu aqrarna. Qala fashhadu wa ana ma'akum minash-shahidin. (He said, "Have you acknowledged and taken upon that My commitment?" They said, "We have acknowledged." He said, "Then bear witness, and I am with you among the witnesses.")
Allah asked, "Have you acknowledged? And have you taken the responsibility of bearing this burden of mine?" They replied, "Yes, we have acknowledged." Allah said, "You be witnesses, and I am also a witness with you."
Meaning, did you acknowledge the station of the *Wasi*? Wherever Allah is mentioned, there is a mention of the *Wasi*. Yes. Meaning, did you acknowledge the station of the *Wasi*? And will you fulfill this covenant of mine? They acknowledged the fulfillment of the covenant, so He said, "Very good. Now make your respondents of the Da'wah (*mustajibin-e-da'wat*) witnesses to this, and I am also a witness."
Faman tawalla ba'da dhalika fa'ula'ika humul-fasiqun. (And whoever turns away after that - then those are the defiantly disobedient.)
So whoever turns away from this covenant will exit the religion.
It is clearly stated. See, wherever there are verses about the covenant (*'ahd*), this is what it is. Are you accepting with your heart or not? If not, then Allah the Exalted will... will destroy you. And all the verses of the covenant are like this. If the covenant is broken, then one reaches the stage of disbelief (*kufr*), and you will become deserving of Hell. So breaking the covenant... *Nakithin-e-'ahd* (breakers of the covenant). You see, *Nakithin-e-'ahd* in one era were the Jews and... those people. After that, the sects that have emerged among us, Sunni and Shia, they are the *Nakithin-e-'ahd*, *Mariqin*, *Fasiqin*, all of them. Today, the prayer we make, we do it for all of them. The opponents who are ours. Because the prayer of *Nas'aluka-llah*... its meaning is just this. *Nas'aluka-llah* is such a prayer that creates a distinction between us and the Daudis. They do not have *Nas'aluka-llah*. We recite it. So we seek refuge from these three things: *Mariqin*, *Nakithin*, and *Fasiqin*. And all three things are in this. You should read... Sayyidna Sulayman sahib's book, the biography of Mawlana... all the signs are present in them. *Nakithin-e-'ahd*. Khadim Husain used to write. All things... he used to believe that "you are the great one for me." As soon as he found out that Ahmedabad is completely his, all of India is his, the matter was finished. And then his *hawadin* (supporters)... he brought in Abdullah Mhafeejee and others. All these people came and caused corruption. They were worldly people.
Afaghayra dinillahi yabghuna wa lahu aslama man fis-samawati wal-ardi taw'an wa karhan wa ilayhi yurja'un. (So is it other than the religion of Allah they desire, while to Him have submitted [all] those within the heavens and earth, willingly or by compulsion, and to Him they will be returned?)
Do they desire a religion other than Allah's, although for Allah alone those in the heavens and the earth, in every state, are obedient? And they all will return to Him alone.
Meaning, do they want the *da'wah* (mission) of someone other than the *Da'wah* of the *Wasi*? Do they not know that before him, the heads of all the *Nutaqa'* and *Awsiya'* are bowed? Some in the hope of the reward to be received from him, and some out of fear of the punishment for disobeying him. And all must return to Him alone.
See, "to Him alone they will return" means to *Mawlana 'Ali (salawatullah 'alayh)*. He is the magnet that is inside the earth. And all geographers and scientists say that there is a gravitational force, the earth is pulling, and all things are returning to it. Now, if you want, if something falls from anywhere, it will land on the earth. If you are stopping it by force, that is a different matter. Otherwise, everyone is under force, meaning they are submitting. Now, see, where the mention of the heavens and the earth has come in the *ta'wilat* (esoteric interpretations), the heavens refer to the *Nutaqa'* and the earth refers to the *Awsiya'*. And the *Awsiya'*... who is the *Ashraf al-Wasi* (most noble of *Wasis*)? *Janab 'Ali (salawatullah 'alayh)*. So in the realm of the earth, there is the *Wasi* of the Prophet. And every thing... as we say, *kullu shay'in yarji'u ila aslihi* (everything returns to its origin). *Kullu shay'in* means every believer. Every believer will return to him, to 'Ali. Just as every thing is returning towards the earth, so too is there... this is a gravitational pull. But, and in the same way, it applies to Janab 'Ali. But when will they come? *Yawman ma'* (On a certain day). Allah knows when their days will turn and they will come forward. These are corruptions.
Qul amanna billahi wa ma unzila 'alayna wa ma unzila 'ala Ibrahima wa Isma'ila wa Ishaqa wa Ya'quba wal-asbati wa ma utiya Musa wa 'Isa wan-nabiyyuna min rabbihim, la nufarriqu bayna ahadin minhum wa nahnu lahu muslimun. (Say, "We have believed in Allah and in what was revealed to us and what was revealed to Abraham, Ishmael, Isaac, Jacob, and the Descendants, and in what was given to Moses and Jesus and to the prophets from their Lord. We make no distinction between any of them, and we are Muslims [in submission] to Him.")
O Messenger, say: We believe in Allah, and in that which has been revealed to us, and in this too, that which was revealed to Ibrahim and Isma'il and Ishaq and Ya'qub and their progeny. And that which was given to Musa and 'Isa and the prophets from their Lord. We believe in this too. We do not establish any distinction among these prophets. And we are obedient to Him alone.
Meaning, say that we believe in the Exalted Creator (*Sabiq-i-Ta'ala*). We believe in the command that was given regarding the *Wilayat* of 'Ali. And in the same chain, we believe in what was revealed to Hazrat Ibrahim, Isma'il, Ishaq, Ya'qub, and the prophets in their progeny. And then the *Ulu al-'Azm* (Possessors of Firm Resolve) messengers, Musa and 'Isa, and other prophets, the command that was revealed from the Lord of their Age, we accept it. We do not establish any distinction among them. See. From the progeny of Janab Ibrahim, Ishaq was born, Isma'il was born, and Ya'qub, and after that Ayyub and Yusuf. This entire dynasty is one that has passed as prophets. Among prophets, it is not necessary that the son becomes one after the father. But Ibrahim's son was Ishaq, Ishaq's son was Ya'qub, Ya'qub's son was Yusuf, and then the lineage continued to Ayyub. So all these are at the rank of *Nabi*, and Musa and 'Isa have also been included. From this, it is also apparent that Ibrahim's station is different, and that of Musa and 'Isa is different. In higher books, it is mentioned that the rank of Musa and 'Isa is much lower than the other *Nutaqa'*.
We do not establish any distinction among them. We accept all the bearers of ranks. By accepting them... we are obedient to them alone.
[unclear]
Wa man yabtaghi ghayral-islami dinan falan yuqbala minhu wa huwa fil-akhirati minal-khasirin. (And whoever desires other than Islam as religion - never will it be accepted from him, and he, in the Hereafter, will be among the losers.)
And whoever desires any other religion besides Islam, it will never be accepted from him, and he will be among the losers in the Hereafter.
Meaning, whoever shuns the *Wilayat* of *'Ali Amir al-Mu'minin*, and holds a belief in someone else, then none of the service he has performed in the Da'wah of the Messenger will ever be accepted. Now you see, the companions (*ashab*), how much service they performed, and how famous they became. That 'Uthman gave his whole house, Janab Abu Bakr did this. But when did they do it? When the government was established, and Islam had gained strength. But who did it first? *Mawlatina Khadijah* did it. The strength that came to Islam was from *Mawlatina Khadijah*. She gave all her wealth.
And he will be deprived of the benefits of the false Da'wah (*Da'wat-e-Batilah*).
Kayfa yahdillahu qawman kafaru ba'da imanihim wa shahidu annar-rasula haqqun wa ja'ahumul-bayyinat, wallahu la yahdil-qawmadh-dhalimin. (How shall Allah guide a people who disbelieved after their belief and had witnessed that the Messenger is true and clear signs had come to them? And Allah does not guide the wrongdoing people.)
Why would Allah guide a people who, after believing, became disbelievers? And they also gave testimony to the fact that the Messenger is true, and the proofs for this also came to them. And Allah does not guide the wrongdoing people. Those who turned back. Meaning, after coming into Islam, to turn back again is a sign of disbelief. The *mushrikin* (polytheists) are different, I have been explaining this for two or three days. *Mushrikin* has a different meaning, and *kafirin* (disbelievers) has a different one.
Meaning, the Exalted Creator does not guide those people who, after testifying to the truthfulness of the Messenger and believing in the *Risalat* (messengership), despite the presence of clear proofs, deny the *Wilayat* of the *Wasi*. And how many proofs there are for 'Ali. The virtues of 'Ali, the merits of 'Ali. That his door should be kept open. In the gathering of *Dhu al-'Ashirah*, 'Ali was the first to say, "I am ready to help you." In Mecca, on the night of the *Hijrah* when he slept, he simply covered himself with a sheet and went to sleep. Whereas Abu Bakr was with the Prophet. He is with the Prophet, with a great man, and yet he is crying in the cave of Hira. He kept crying in the cave of Hira. And the verse was revealed, *la tahzan* (do not grieve), that we are with him. Allah is with us, so why are you crying? At that time, the *sakinah* (tranquility) that is in the verse that was revealed, it is only in the singular form. The *sakinah* that descended, it descended only upon the Prophet. It did not descend upon Abu Bakr. So it became... *Yar-e-Ghar* (Companion of the Cave). *Yar-e-Ghar* is famous. Any close friend is called *Yar-e-Ghar*. The reason it came is that he was the companion of the Prophet in the cave. Because of being a companion.
And they do injustice by removing the *Awliya'* (saints) of the religion from their station.
Ula'ika jaza'uhum anna 'alayhim la'natallahi wal-mala'ikati wan-nasi ajma'in. Khalidina fiha, la yukhaffafu 'anhumul-'adhabu wa la hum yundharun. (Those - their recompense is that upon them is the curse of Allah and the angels and the people, all together. Abiding eternally therein. The punishment will not be lightened for them, nor will they be reprieved.)
These are the people whose recompense is this: that upon them the curse of Allah and the angels and all people falls. In which they will remain forever. Their punishment will not be lightened, and they will not be given any respite.
Meaning, the *Natiq 'alayhis-salam* will cut ties with them. He will deprive them of spiritual effusion (*ifada-e-dini*) and demote them from their ranks. And in this way, they will fall from the sight of all the Imams and the *Hudud* of the Da'wah. They will remain in the depths of descent (*darkat-e-hubut*) for ages. The severe punishment of doubt (*shukuk*) will not be lightened for them, and they will be given no respite in this matter. Now see, *darkat-e-hubut*... they will be thrown into it. Even today there are such people who... in the time of the Prophet... Fir'awn in the time of Musa... Namrud who was... or take it as in the time of Ibrahim... however many have persecuted the *Nutaqa'*, where are they at this time? The final reckoning (*tasfiyah*) will be at the hands of the *Qa'im*. Until then, where are they? They are experiencing the lesser punishment (*'adhab-e-adna*). See, in the depths... they will be thrown down. In which depth, Allah knows. Which depth are they in? We don't know. But they are experiencing the lesser punishment. And it is possible that their punishment is of delusion (*'adhab-e-izrab*). But the punishment of *Sakhr* will be at the hands of the *Qa'im*, and that will be the final time, on the Day of Judgment.
Illal-ladhina tabu min ba'di dhalika wa aslahu, fa'innallaha ghafurun rahim. (Except for those who repent after that and correct themselves, for indeed, Allah is Forgiving and Merciful.)
But yes, those people who, after disbelief, repent and reform, then indeed Allah is Forgiving and Merciful.
Meaning, those who return to the *Wasi* and correct their belief, then the *Wasi* will also mercifully forgive them. Now see, the successors of the *Wasi*... Mawlana Hurr. At the last moment, he was... his fate changed. His destiny changed. You keep hearing those couplets, about how he was an opponent, and he was the first to stop Janab Husain. But when he saw that "I was brought here to block the path, and here they are preparing for murder." At that very moment, he changed. "This is the family of the Prophet, and these people are about to kill them." He immediately turned his horse and came to Husain's presence. And his... and at the last moment, when he... the time of his death came, he was attaining martyrdom, he said, "Do not leave me and go." And from a distance, he... cried out, Janab Husain, and said, "Yes, it will be so." "Your resurrection will be with me." This is a very big thing. At such a time... the world of intention is a great event, the incident of Karbala. And in it, a person's destiny changes. It's a very big thing. And as many as there were, some were such that their fathers were given the glad tidings of Paradise, but the son became deserving of the punishment of Hell. So many such people... and those you hear about in the events of Karbala, and one who was destined for Hell, his son became a believer with a shield. In the world of intention (*'alam-e-niyyat*), whatever happened, its copies are found here. In the world of intention, it all happened. And then in the system of cycles (*karrat-o-nizam*), some other things also happened. The system of cycles is different, primordial enmity (*azali qusumat*) and primordial matters are different, and then there are environmental factors (*mahawliyat*), then there are *mub'athat*, *mujibat*. There are many such things which have an effect on a person. There are so many things that we don't even know, and we get support from somewhere. We get wealth from somewhere. Your *mub'athat*... in a previous cycle, you had a right, you didn't get it then, you are getting it now. These are *mub'athat*. *Mujibat* are a different thing. The system of cycles is a different thing. The system of cycles is a cycle of seven by seven years, and each cycle (*karrah*) is of one thousand years. Now where, in which... place you were born, where you passed away, no one knows. God has made us forget these things. Man is derived from *nisyan* (forgetfulness). He is one who forgets. He does not remember anything. So remember that this world is a... a strange marketplace, where there is good and there is bad. And the world is a prison for the believer (*sijn al-mu'min*) and a paradise for the disbeliever (*jannat al-kafir*). Now, the good things that the believers are getting, those are the good deeds (*hasanat*), yours and your parents', your grandparents',... you are receiving them because of the *hasanat*. Otherwise, the world is the prison of the believer. Whatever hardships we are enduring, we are doing it for this reason. And if you are doing it to earn the world, it is all wrong. If you are doing this so that your hereafter is good, then you will get the reward for it. So remember that the world is the prison of the believer and the paradise of the disbeliever. This is a special thing. I have narrated the incidents of Mawlana Hasan two or three times. That a Jew came to Janab Hasan... he was in tatters, wearing ordinary clothes, he came wearing dirty clothes, and said, "O son of Abu Talib, a... Janab Hasan... O son of Janab 'Ali, your grandfather has said that the world is the prison of the believer and the paradise of the disbeliever. And I see that you are wearing the best clothes." Mawlana Hasan used to wear very fine clothes. And winter in summer, summer in winter, sorrow in happiness, happiness in sorrow... he would eliminate that. He would give it away. So at that time, he said, "I see you in good clothes, and your grandfather has said this. And look at me, a disbeliever's paradise it should be, and look at my clothes." At that time, his saying was... see, in one or two sentences, the whole meaning becomes clear. It was this, that Hasan said... Mawlana Hasan said, "O man, if you knew what place you will be thrown into, compared to that, what you are standing in now is a very good place. It is very good. The fire of Hell... the heat of Hell... there you will reach. This is very good. And what I am being told about... what I am going to get in Paradise, compared to that, this is nothing."
